# 服装与配饰

**clothes**
英/美 /kləʊðz/ 美 /kloʊðz/
n. 衣服（总称）
[义] Items such as shirts, trousers, and dresses that people wear on their body. 穿在身上的衣物总称（shirt、pants、dress 等），无单数形式表总称。比 clothing 更口语；clothing 略正式、可作行业用语（clothing industry 服装业）。
[例] ① She packed a bag with warm clothes for the trip. 她为旅行装了一包保暖衣服。 ② He hung his wet clothes on the line to dry. 他把湿衣服挂在绳子上晾干。
[搭] change one's clothes 换衣服, casual clothes 便装, warm clothes 保暖衣物, wash clothes 洗衣服, put on clothes 穿衣服
[联] clothing 衣服（总称, 略正式）, garment 一件衣（较正式）, outfit 一套搭配, wardrobe 衣橱/全部衣物, laundry 待洗衣物, fashion 时尚, dress 连衣裙/穿着, wear 穿
[记] 复数形式表总称，不说 × a clothes。cloth（布料）与 clothes 不同词。

**clothing**
英/美 /ˈkləʊðɪŋ/ 美 /ˈkloʊðɪŋ/
n. 衣服；服装（总称）
[义] Clothes in general; the things that people wear. 衣物总称，不可数。比 clothes 略正式，常见于行业、法规、慈善语境（protective clothing 防护服，clothing store 服装店，donate clothing 捐赠衣物）。
[例] ① Workers must wear protective clothing in the lab. 实验室内工人必须穿防护服。 ② The charity collects used clothing for families in need. 该慈善机构为困难家庭募集旧衣物。
[搭] protective clothing 防护服, winter clothing 冬装, children's clothing 童装, clothing industry 服装业, donate clothing 捐赠衣物
[联] clothes 衣服（更口语）, garment 一件衣, apparel 服装（商业）, textile 纺织品, fabric 布料, wardrobe 衣橱, laundry 洗衣
[记] 不可数；不说 × a clothing，用 a piece of clothing 或 an item of clothing。

**garment**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːmənt/
n. （一件）衣服
[义] A piece of clothing; an article of dress. 单件衣物，可数。比 clothes 正式，常见于制造、零售、新闻（garment factory 服装厂，outer garment 外衣）。动词较少用。
[例] ① Each garment is inspected before it leaves the factory. 每件衣服出厂前都要检验。 ② She hung the delicate garment on a padded hanger. 她把这件娇贵的衣服挂在软垫衣架上。
[搭] outer garment 外衣, delicate garment 娇贵衣物, garment factory 服装厂, garment industry 服装制造业, a single garment 单件衣服
[联] clothes 衣服, clothing 服装, apparel 服装（商业）, outfit 一套搭配, textile 纺织品, tailor 裁缝, stitch 缝, seam 接缝
[记] 可数；a garment。garment 偏「成品衣」，fabric 偏「布料」。

**outfit**
英/美 /ˈaʊtfɪt/
n. 全套服装；搭配
v. 装备；配备
[义] A set of clothes worn together, especially for a particular occasion or activity. 为某场合或活动搭配好的一套衣服（a business outfit 商务装，workout outfit 运动装）。可数。动词 outfit 指为某人/机构配备装备（outfit a kitchen 配置厨房设备），服装语境较少单独用动词。
[例] ① She chose a simple black outfit for the interview. 她选了一套简洁的黑色衣服去面试。 ② He posted a photo of his hiking outfit on social media. 他在社交媒体上发了一张登山穿搭的照片。
[搭] business outfit 商务装, casual outfit 休闲搭配, complete outfit 全套搭配, outfit choice 穿搭选择, matching outfit 配套服装
[联] clothes 衣服, dress 连衣裙/穿着, suit 套装, uniform 制服, costume 戏服/服装, style 风格, fashion 时尚, wardrobe 衣橱, accessory 配饰
[记] 可数；an outfit。outfit 亦指团队、机构（a news outfit 一家新闻机构），需看语境。

**wear**
英/美 /weər/
v. 穿；戴；留着（发型/胡须）
n. 穿着；磨损
[义] To have clothing, shoes, or accessories on your body; to keep a hairstyle or beard. 身上穿着、戴着衣物或配饰；留着某种发型或胡须（wear a hat 戴帽子，wear glasses 戴眼镜，wear one's hair long 留长发）。名词 wear 指穿着效果或磨损（casual wear 休闲穿着，show signs of wear 有磨损痕迹）。
[形] wore / worn / wearing；第三人称单数 wears
[例] ① She always wears comfortable shoes for long walks. 她长途步行总穿舒适的鞋。 ② The carpet is starting to show signs of wear. 地毯开始出现磨损痕迹。
[搭] wear clothes 穿衣服, wear a uniform 穿制服, wear glasses 戴眼镜, casual wear 休闲穿着, wear and tear 正常使用造成的磨损
[联] dress 穿着/连衣裙, put on 穿上, take off 脱下, outfit 一套搭配, fashion 时尚, style 风格, wardrobe 衣橱, tear 撕裂（wear and tear 磨损）
[记] 状态动词，进行时表「一直穿着」较少用。wear out 穿旧/耗尽精力；wear off 效果消退。

**dress**
英/美 /dres/
n. 连衣裙；女装
v. 穿衣；穿着
[义] 名词指女式连衣裙（a summer dress 夏裙）；动词 dress 指给自己或他人穿衣（dress the children 给孩子穿衣），或表穿着风格（dress casually 穿着随意）。dress up 盛装；dress down 穿着随意/训斥。
[形] dressed / dressed / dressing
[例] ① She bought a new dress for the wedding. 她为婚礼买了一条新连衣裙。 ② He dressed quickly and rushed to the meeting. 他快速穿好衣服赶去开会。
[搭] summer dress 夏裙, wedding dress 婚纱, dress up 盛装, dress casually 穿着随意, get dressed 穿好衣服
[联] clothes 衣服, outfit 一套搭配, gown 长袍/礼服, skirt 裙子, blouse 女式衬衫, suit 套装, wardrobe 衣橱, fashion 时尚
[记] 可数名词 a dress。dressing 名词可指沙拉酱（salad dressing），不同义。

**shirt**
英/美 /ʃɜːrt/
n. 衬衫；上衣
[义] A piece of clothing for the upper body, usually with a collar, sleeves, and buttons down the front. 上身衣物，通常有领、袖、前襟纽扣（dress shirt 正装衬衫，T-shirt 虽名 shirt 但属针织短袖）。可数。美式 T-shirt 不算传统 shirt；英式 shirt 有时泛指更多上装。
[例] ① He rolled up his shirt sleeves before washing the dishes. 洗碗前他把衬衫袖子卷了起来。 ② She ironed his shirt collar flat. 她把他的衬衫领子熨平了。
[搭] dress shirt 正装衬衫, white shirt 白衬衫, roll up one's sleeves 卷起袖子, shirt collar 衬衫领, polo shirt polo 衫
[联] blouse 女式衬衫, T-shirt T 恤, sweater 毛衣, jacket 夹克, collar 衣领, sleeve 袖子, button 纽扣, cuff 袖口
[记] 可数；a shirt。wet your shirt 指辛苦劳作（美式口语）。

**T-shirt**
英/美 /ˈtiː ʃɜːrt/
n. T 恤
[义] A casual shirt with short sleeves and no collar or buttons, usually made of cotton. 短袖、无领、通常无纽扣的休闲针织上衣，多为棉质。可数。亦作 tee shirt；口语常简称 tee。
[例] ① He was wearing jeans and a plain white T-shirt. 他穿着牛仔裤和一件素白 T 恤。 ② The band sold T-shirts with their logo at the concert. 乐队在演唱会上出售印有其标志的 T 恤。
[搭] plain T-shirt 素色 T 恤, cotton T-shirt 棉质 T 恤, graphic T-shirt 印花 T 恤, T-shirt size T 恤尺码, band T-shirt 乐队 T 恤
[联] shirt 衬衫, blouse 女式衬衫, jeans 牛仔裤, casual 休闲的, cotton 棉, logo 标志, sleeve 袖子, collar 衣领
[记] 可数；a T-shirt。T 来自 shape（T 字形）。勿与 tea（茶）混淆读音语境。

**blouse**
英/美 /blaʊs/ 美 /blaʊs/
n. 女式衬衫；宽松上衣
[义] A shirt for women, often looser and more decorative than a men's shirt. 女式上衣，通常比男式衬衫更宽松、更有装饰性（silk blouse 丝绸女衫，blouse and skirt 衬衫配裙）。可数。男士宽松上衣偶亦用，但多指女装。
[例] ① She paired a silk blouse with tailored trousers for the presentation. 她用丝绸女衫搭配剪裁利落的裤子做汇报。 ② The blouse has delicate lace around the collar. 这件女衫领口有精致蕾丝。
[搭] silk blouse 丝绸女衫, white blouse 白衬衫, blouse and skirt 衬衫配裙, flowy blouse 飘逸女衫, button-down blouse 纽扣女衫
[联] shirt 衬衫, T-shirt T 恤, skirt 裙子, dress 连衣裙, collar 衣领, sleeve 袖子, lace 蕾丝, tailored 剪裁利落的
[记] 可数；a blouse。动词 blouse 军事用语指「松散下垂」，日常极少用。

**sweater**
英/美 /ˈswetər/
n. 毛衣；针织衫
[义] A warm knitted garment for the upper body, usually with long sleeves. 保暖针织上装，通常长袖（wool sweater 羊毛衫，turtleneck sweater 高领毛衣）。可数。英式 jumper 同义；美式 sweater 不含无袖背心（vest）。
[例] ① She pulled on a thick sweater when the temperature dropped. 气温下降时她套上厚毛衣。 ② This sweater shrank after I washed it in hot water. 这件毛衣用热水洗后缩水了。
[搭] wool sweater 羊毛衫, cashmere sweater 羊绒衫, turtleneck sweater 高领毛衣, knit a sweater 织毛衣, sweater weather 适合穿毛衣的天气
[联] cardigan 开衫, hoodie 连帽衫, jacket 夹克, wool 羊毛, knit 编织, warm 保暖的, sleeve 袖子, pullover 套头衫（英式）
[记] 可数；a sweater。sweat 汗与 sweater 同族（针织保暖「防出汗着凉」的民间联想），但 sweater 不表「汗衫」（美式 sweatshirt 才是运动卫衣）。

**cardigan**
英/美 /ˈkɑːdɪɡən/
n. 开衫；羊毛开衫
[义] A knitted sweater that opens at the front and is usually fastened with buttons or a zip. 前开襟、通常以纽扣或拉链固定的针织上装。可数。比 sweater 更强调「可敞开穿」。
[例] ① He wore a grey cardigan over his shirt in the office. 他在办公室衬衫外搭了一件灰色开衫。 ② He buttoned up his cardigan against the evening chill. 他扣好开衫抵御傍晚凉意。
[搭] wool cardigan 羊毛开衫, buttoned cardigan 纽扣开衫, open cardigan 敞开式开衫, cardigan sweater 开衫毛衣, lightweight cardigan 薄开衫
[联] sweater 毛衣, jacket 夹克, hoodie 连帽衫, button 纽扣, zip 拉链, knit 编织, wool 羊毛, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a cardigan。名字来自开襟羊毛衫推广者 Cardigan 伯爵，属服装史趣闻。

**hoodie**
英/美 /ˈhʊdi/
n. 连帽衫；帽衫
[义] A casual sweatshirt or jacket with a hood attached. 带连帽的休闲运动上衣或夹克。可数。亦作 hoody；口语高频，尤指青少年、运动、街头穿搭。
[例] ① He pulled up the hood on his hoodie when it started to rain. 下雨时他把连帽衫的帽子拉了起来。 ② Campus rules ban hoodies with offensive graphics. 校规禁止穿印有冒犯图案的连帽衫。
[搭] zip-up hoodie 拉链连帽衫, pullover hoodie 套头连帽衫, oversized hoodie 宽松连帽衫, hoodie season 穿帽衫的季节, wear a hoodie 穿连帽衫
[联] sweatshirt 运动卫衣, jacket 夹克, sweater 毛衣, hood 兜帽, casual 休闲的, sneakers 运动鞋, jeans 牛仔裤, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a hoodie。hood 指帽子部分；hoodie 为 21 世纪日常词，亦涉社会讨论（蒙面、安保）。

**jacket**
英/美 /ˈdʒækɪt/
n. 夹克；短外套
[义] A short coat, usually lightweight, worn on the upper body. 较短的上身外套，通常较轻（leather jacket 皮夹克，denim jacket 牛仔夹克，sports jacket 运动夹克）。可数。比 coat 短、常不御寒到严冬级别。
[例] ① He hung his jacket on the back of the chair. 他把夹克挂在椅背上。 ② She bought a waterproof jacket for hiking. 她买了一件防水夹克去徒步。
[搭] leather jacket 皮夹克, denim jacket 牛仔夹克, sports jacket 运动夹克/休闲西装外套, life jacket 救生衣, jacket pocket 夹克口袋
[联] coat 大衣, blazer 西装外套, sweater 毛衣, sleeve 袖子, collar 衣领, zip 拉链, button 纽扣, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a jacket。book jacket 指书籍护封，不同义。

**coat**
英/美 /koʊt/
n. 外套；大衣
[义] An outer garment worn outdoors for warmth or protection, usually longer than a jacket. 户外保暖或防护的外层衣物，通常比夹克长（winter coat 冬大衣，raincoat 雨衣，coat and tie 正装外套加领带）。可数。
[例] ① Don't forget your coat—it's freezing outside. 别忘了外套——外面冷极了。 ② She brushed the snow off her coat before stepping inside. 进屋前她掸掉大衣上的雪。
[搭] winter coat 冬大衣, raincoat 雨衣, coat and tie 正装（外套加领带）, heavy coat 厚外套, coat hanger 衣架
[联] jacket 夹克, overcoat 长大衣, blazer 西装外套, wool 羊毛, warm 保暖的, sleeve 袖子, button 纽扣, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a coat。coat of paint 指一层油漆，不同义。

**blazer**
英/美 /ˈbleɪzər/
n. 西装外套； blazer
[义] A smart casual jacket, often in a solid colour, worn without matching trousers. 较正式的休闲外套，常为纯色，可不配同料长裤（navy blazer 海军蓝西装外套，school blazer 校服西装外套）。可数。比 suit jacket 更强调「单件、非全套西装」。
[例] ① He wore a navy blazer with khaki trousers to the dinner. 他穿海军蓝西装外套配卡其裤赴宴。 ② The school blazer must be worn on formal days. 正式日必须穿校服西装外套。
[搭] navy blazer 海军蓝西装外套, school blazer 校服西装外套, blazer and slacks 西装外套配西裤, gold-button blazer 金扣西装外套, tailored blazer 剪裁利落的西装外套
[联] jacket 夹克, suit 套装, trousers 长裤, formal 正式的, collar 衣领, button 纽扣, uniform 制服, business 商务
[记] 可数；a blazer。名字据说来自剑桥划船队 Blazer 队服颜色，属词源趣闻。

**suit**
英/美 /suːt/
n. 套装；西装
v. 适合；合身
[义] A set of matching jacket and trousers or skirt, worn for formal or business occasions. 上下配套的外套与长裤或裙，用于正式或商务场合（business suit 商务西装，three-piece suit 三件套）。可数。动词 suit 表适合（That colour suits you 那颜色适合你）。
[例] ① He wore a dark suit to the job interview. 他穿深色西装去面试。 ② The tailor altered the suit so the shoulders fit better. 裁缝改了西装，让肩部更合身。
[搭] business suit 商务西装, three-piece suit 三件套, suit and tie 西装领带, tailored suit 定制西装, pinstripe suit 细条纹西装
[联] blazer 西装外套, tie 领带, formal 正式的, trousers 长裤, jacket 夹克, dress 穿着, fit 合身, interview 面试
[记] 可数；a suit。suite /swɪt/ 套房与 suit 同音异义；lawsuit 诉讼亦含 suit 但义不同。

**uniform**
英/美 /ˈjuːnɪfɔːrm/
n. 制服
adj. 统一的；一致的
[义] A special set of clothes worn by members of a group to show they belong to it. 表明所属群体身份的统一服装（school uniform 校服，military uniform 军装，nurse's uniform 护士服）。可数。形容词 uniform 表「一致的」（uniform size 统一尺码），与名词同形。
[例] ① Students must wear the school uniform on weekdays. 学生平日须穿校服。 ② The airline updated its crew uniform last year. 该航空公司去年更新了机组制服。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *uniformis*（统一的），*uni-*（一）+ *form-*（形状）→ 同一式样的服装。
[族] uniformity 一致性, uniformly adv.
[搭] school uniform 校服, military uniform 军装, wear a uniform 穿制服, uniform policy 制服规定, nurse's uniform 护士服
[联] outfit 一套搭配, costume 戏服, clothes 衣服, badge 徽章, rank 军衔/职级, dress code 着装规定, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a uniform。in uniform 指「穿着制服」。

**vest**
英/美 /vest/
n. 背心；马甲
[义] 美式：无袖上装，穿在衬衫外或内（sweater vest 毛背心）；英式 waistcoat 指西装马甲，vest 常指内衣或运动背心。可数。地域差异大：美式 undershirt/athletic vest，英式 waistcoat 为西装三件套的背心。
[例] ① He wore a sweater vest over his shirt in the library. 他在图书馆衬衫外穿了件毛背心。 ② In the UK, a vest means an undershirt worn under clothes. 在英国，vest 指穿在衣服下的内衣背心。
[搭] sweater vest 毛背心, bulletproof vest 防弹背心, life vest 救生衣（亦 life jacket）, vest pocket 马甲口袋, thermal vest 保暖背心
[联] shirt 衬衫, jacket 夹克, sleeve 袖子, layer 分层穿着, waistcoat 马甲（英式西装）, undershirt 汗衫, sweater 毛衣
[记] 可数；a vest。美式与英式义不同，国际交流须留意。

**pants**
英/美 /pænts/
n. 裤子（美式）；内裤（英式）
[义] 美式：外穿长裤（a pair of pants 一条裤子）；英式：内裤。可数复数形。美式与英式差异极大：英式长裤用 trousers，pants 指内裤。写作与口语须标明语域。
[例] ① He bought a new pair of dress pants for work. 他买了一条新的正装裤上班穿。 ② In London, "pants" can mean underwear, not trousers. 在伦敦，pants 可以指内裤而非长裤。
[搭] a pair of pants 一条裤子, dress pants 正装裤, yoga pants 瑜伽裤, cargo pants 工装裤, sweatpants 运动裤
[联] trousers 长裤（英式）, jeans 牛仔裤, shorts 短裤, slacks 西裤（美式）, leggings 紧身裤, belt 腰带, pocket 口袋
[记] 复数形表一条裤：a pair of pants。英式学习者勿在英国把外裤称为 pants 闹误会。

**trousers**
英/美 /ˈtraʊzərz/
n. 长裤（尤英式）
[义] An outer garment covering the body from the waist to the ankles, with a separate part for each leg. 从腰到踝、双腿分开的外穿长裤。复数形；美式更常说 pants 或 slacks。可数用法：a pair of trousers。
[例] ① He pressed his trousers before the meeting. 开会前他把长裤熨平了。 ② These trousers are too long—I need to hem them. 这条裤子太长——我得改短。
[搭] a pair of trousers 一条长裤, dress trousers 正装长裤, corduroy trousers 灯芯绒长裤, trouser pocket 裤袋, trouser suit 裤装套装
[联] pants 裤子（美式）, jeans 牛仔裤, shorts 短裤, slacks 西裤, belt 腰带, pocket 口袋, hem 改裤边, crease 裤线
[记] 复数；a pair of trousers。trouser 作形容词（trouser leg 裤腿）。

**jeans**
英/美 /dʒiːnz/
n. 牛仔裤
[义] Trousers made of denim, usually blue, worn casually. 丹宁布制成的休闲长裤，通常为蓝色。复数形；a pair of jeans。可数。品牌与版型多样（skinny jeans 紧身牛仔裤，ripped jeans 破洞牛仔裤）。
[例] ① She lives in jeans and sneakers most days. 她大多日子都穿牛仔裤和运动鞋。 ② These jeans are still stiff—they need another wash. 这条牛仔裤还硬——得再洗一次。
[搭] a pair of jeans 一条牛仔裤, blue jeans 蓝色牛仔裤, skinny jeans 紧身牛仔裤, ripped jeans 破洞牛仔裤, high-waisted jeans 高腰牛仔裤
[联] denim 丹宁布, pants 裤子, trousers 长裤, T-shirt T 恤, sneakers 运动鞋, casual 休闲的, belt 腰带, pocket 口袋
[记] 复数；a pair of jeans。Jean 亦作女子名，与复数 jeans 不同。

**shorts**
英/美 /ʃɔːrts/
n. 短裤
[义] Trousers that end above the knee or at the knee. 膝盖以上或及膝的短外裤。复数形；a pair of shorts。可数。运动、夏季、居家高频（running shorts 跑步短裤，cargo shorts 工装短裤）。
[例] ① It's too hot for jeans—let's wear shorts. 穿牛仔裤太热了——咱们穿短裤吧。 ② He packed gym shorts for the workout. 他带了运动短裤去锻炼。
[搭] a pair of shorts 一条短裤, gym shorts 运动短裤, cargo shorts 工装短裤, denim shorts 牛仔短裤, board shorts 沙滩短裤
[联] pants 裤子, trousers 长裤, jeans 牛仔裤, summer 夏天, beach 海滩, sneakers 运动鞋, T-shirt T 恤
[记] 复数；a pair of shorts。short 形容词与 shorts 名词不同形义。

**skirt**
英/美 /skɜːrt/
n. 裙子
v. 沿边缘走；回避
[义] A garment hanging from the waist, covering part or all of the legs, worn by women and girls. 从腰部垂下、遮盖部分或全部腿部的女装。可数。动词 skirt 表沿边而行或回避（skirt the issue 回避问题），与服装名词不同义。
[例] ① She wore a pleated skirt with a white blouse. 她穿百褶裙配白衬衫。 ② The policy skirts around the real problem of funding. 该政策回避了资金这一真正问题。
[搭] pleated skirt 百褶裙, mini skirt 迷你裙, long skirt 长裙, skirt and blouse 裙装配衬衫, pencil skirt 铅笔裙
[联] dress 连衣裙, blouse 女式衬衫, trousers 长裤, hem 裙边/裤边, waist 腰, fashion 时尚, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a skirt。mini skirt 亦作 miniskirt。

**gown**
英/美 /ɡaʊn/
n. 长袍；礼服；病号服
[义] A long dress, especially for formal occasions or ceremonies. 长礼服，尤指正式或典礼场合（wedding gown 婚纱，evening gown 晚礼服）。可数。亦指法官袍、毕业袍、医院病号服（hospital gown 病号服），须看语境。
[例] ① The bride's gown was embroidered with lace. 新娘的礼服绣有蕾丝。 ② Nurses helped him into a hospital gown before the scan. 护士在他做扫描前帮他穿上病号服。
[搭] wedding gown 婚纱, evening gown 晚礼服, graduation gown 毕业袍, hospital gown 病号服, silk gown 丝绸长袍
[联] dress 连衣裙, robe 长袍, lace 蕾丝, formal 正式的, wedding 婚礼, ceremony 典礼, veil 面纱
[记] 可数；a gown。nightgown 睡衣袍，属家居服。

**pajamas**
英/美 /pəˈdʒæməz/ 美 /pəˈdʒɑːməz/
n. 睡衣
[义] Loose trousers and a top worn in bed. 睡觉时穿的宽松上衣与长裤套装。复数形；a pair of pajamas。英式亦拼 pyjamas。可数。
[例] ① The kids put on their pajamas and brushed their teeth. 孩子们穿上睡衣刷了牙。 ② She bought soft cotton pajamas for the winter. 她买了柔软的棉质睡衣过冬。
[搭] a pair of pajamas 一套睡衣, cotton pajamas 棉质睡衣, pajama party 睡衣派对, pajama bottoms 睡裤, pajama top 睡衣上衣
[联] robe 长袍, nightgown 睡袍, sleep 睡觉, cotton 棉, comfortable 舒适的, bedroom 卧室, slippers 拖鞋
[记] 复数；a pair of pajamas。源自印地语/波斯语，经英属印度进入英语。

**underwear**
英/美 /ˈʌndərweər/
n. 内衣（总称）
[义] Clothes worn under other clothes, next to the skin. 贴身内层衣物总称，不可数。涵盖内裤、胸罩、背心等（cotton underwear 棉质内衣）。比 underclothes 更常用。
[例] ① Pack enough underwear for a week-long trip. 长途旅行要备够一周的内衣。 ② The label says to wash the underwear in cold water. 标签写着内衣要用冷水洗。
[搭] cotton underwear 棉质内衣, thermal underwear 保暖内衣, change one's underwear 换内衣, underwear drawer 内衣抽屉
[联] bra 胸罩, briefs 内裤, panties 女式内裤, undershirt 汗衫, socks 袜子, laundry 洗衣, layer 分层穿着
[记] 不可数；一件说 an item of underwear 或 a piece of underwear。

**sock**
英/美 /sɒk/ 美 /sɑːk/
n. 短袜
[义] A piece of clothing worn on the foot and lower leg, inside a shoe. 穿在鞋内的足部与小腿下段织物。可数；复数 socks。长短、厚薄各异（ankle socks 短袜，wool socks 羊毛袜）。
[例] ① I can't find a matching pair of socks. 我找不到一双配对的袜子。 ② She pulled on thick wool socks before putting on her boots. 穿靴子前她套上厚羊毛袜。
[搭] a pair of socks 一双袜子, ankle socks 短袜, wool socks 羊毛袜, sock drawer 袜子抽屉, pull on socks 穿袜子
[联] shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, sneaker 运动鞋, stocking 长筒袜, foot 脚, laundry 洗衣, hole 破洞（sock with a hole）
[记] 可数；复数 socks。a pair of socks。sock 动词俚语表「猛击」，不同义。

**shoe**
英/美 /ʃuː/
n. 鞋
[义] A covering for the foot, usually made of leather or other strong material, with a stiff sole. 足部外罩，通常皮革或其他坚韧材料，硬底。可数；复数 shoes。a pair of shoes 一双鞋。涵盖皮鞋、高跟鞋、平底鞋等大类。
[例] ① These shoes are too tight—I need a larger size. 这双鞋太紧——我需要大一号。 ② He polished his shoes before the ceremony. 典礼前他把鞋擦亮了。
[搭] a pair of shoes 一双鞋, leather shoes 皮鞋, high heels 高跟鞋, shoe size 鞋码, tie one's shoes 系鞋带
[联] boot 靴子, sneaker 运动鞋, sandal 凉鞋, slipper 拖鞋, sock 袜子, lace 鞋带, sole 鞋底, heel 鞋跟
[记] 可数；复数 shoes。if the shoe fits 谚语「若鞋合脚就穿上」喻坦然认错。

**sneaker**
英/美 /ˈsniːkər/
n. 运动鞋（美式）
[义] A soft shoe with a rubber sole, worn for sports or casually. 橡胶底软鞋，用于运动或日常休闲。可数。英式更常说 trainers；美式 sneaker 极口语化。
[例] ① He owns three pairs of sneakers in different colours. 他有三双不同颜色的运动鞋。 ② White sneakers go with almost any casual outfit. 白运动鞋几乎配任何休闲装。
[搭] a pair of sneakers 一双运动鞋, white sneakers 白运动鞋, running sneakers 跑步鞋, lace-up sneakers 系带运动鞋, designer sneakers 设计师款运动鞋
[联] shoe 鞋, trainer 运动鞋（英式）, boot 靴子, jeans 牛仔裤, hoodie 连帽衫, rubber 橡胶, sole 鞋底, athletic 运动的
[记] 可数；a sneaker。sneak 动词「溜走」与 sneaker 同族（橡胶底「无声」走动），属词源趣闻。

**boot**
英/美 /buːt/
n. 靴子
v. 踢；启动（计算机）
[义] A strong shoe that covers the foot and ankle, and sometimes the lower leg. 覆盖脚、踝乃至小腿下段的结实鞋靴（hiking boots 登山靴，rain boots 雨靴）。可数。动词 boot 计算机语境指「启动」（boot up 开机），与鞋义不同。
[例] ① She laced up her hiking boots before the trail. 上路前她系好登山靴鞋带。 ② My computer won't boot after the update. 更新后我的电脑无法启动。
[搭] hiking boots 登山靴, rain boots 雨靴, leather boots 皮靴, boot laces 靴带, pull on boots 穿靴子
[联] shoe 鞋, sneaker 运动鞋, sock 袜子, lace 鞋带, sole 鞋底, heel 鞋跟, leather 皮革, waterproof 防水的
[记] 可数；a boot。the boot 英式俚语指「解雇」（get the boot）。

**sandal**
英/美 /ˈsændl/
n. 凉鞋；拖鞋
[义] A light shoe with an open top, held on the foot by straps. 顶部敞开、以带条固定于脚上的轻便鞋（beach sandals 沙滩凉鞋，leather sandals 皮凉鞋）。可数。与 slipper（室内拖鞋）不同：sandal 多指外穿露趾凉鞋。
[例] ① He wore sandals to the beach and left them by the water. 他穿凉鞋去海滩，把它们留在水边。 ② Flip-flops are a type of casual sandal. 人字拖是一种休闲凉鞋。
[搭] beach sandals 沙滩凉鞋, leather sandals 皮凉鞋, strappy sandals 带条凉鞋, open-toe sandals 露趾凉鞋, a pair of sandals 一双凉鞋
[联] shoe 鞋, flip-flop 人字拖, slipper 拖鞋, beach 海滩, summer 夏天, strap 带条, toe 脚趾
[记] 可数；a sandal。sandalwood 檀香木与 sandal 不同词。

**slipper**
英/美 /ˈslɪpər/
n. 拖鞋（室内）
[义] A comfortable soft shoe worn indoors. 室内穿的柔软舒适拖鞋。可数。与 sandal 区分：slipper 多指包脚、室内、保暖或酒店一次性拖鞋（bathroom slippers 浴室拖鞋）。
[例] ① She changed into slippers when she got home. 她到家就换上了拖鞋。 ② The hotel provides complimentary slippers in each room. 酒店每间房提供免费拖鞋。
[搭] bathroom slippers 浴室拖鞋, house slippers 居家拖鞋, a pair of slippers 一双拖鞋, fuzzy slippers 毛绒拖鞋, slip into slippers 趿上拖鞋
[联] shoe 鞋, sandal 凉鞋, sock 袜子, pajamas 睡衣, comfortable 舒适的, home 家, hotel 酒店
[记] 可数；a slipper。slip 滑与 slipper 同族（易「滑入」的鞋）。

**hat**
英/美 /hæt/
n. 帽子（有檐）
[义] A covering for the head, often with a brim. 头部遮盖物，常有帽檐（straw hat 草帽，top hat 礼帽）。可数。比 cap 更泛指各类帽；cap 多指有舌的便帽。
[例] ① She tipped her hat politely to the neighbour. 她礼貌地向邻居碰了碰帽檐。 ② Wear a hat—the sun is strong today. 戴顶帽子——今天太阳很烈。
[搭] straw hat 草帽, top hat 礼帽, wear a hat 戴帽子, take off one's hat 脱帽, hat shop 帽店
[联] cap 便帽, helmet 头盔, brim 帽檐, scarf 围巾, sunglasses 太阳镜, sun 太阳, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a hat。hat trick 体育术语「帽子戏法」（连进三球），与穿戴无关。

**cap**
英/美 /kæp/
n. 便帽；鸭舌帽
[义] A soft flat hat with a peak or visor at the front. 前部有帽舌的软平顶帽（baseball cap 棒球帽，bottle cap 瓶盖为不同义）。可数。亦指无檐针织帽（knit cap 针织帽）在某些语境。
[例] ① He wore a baseball cap to shade his eyes. 他戴棒球帽遮阳。 ② Players must not wear caps with metal buckles on the field. 场上不得戴带金属扣的帽子。
[搭] baseball cap 棒球帽, bottle cap 瓶盖, knit cap 针织帽, cap and gown 学位帽袍, wear a cap 戴帽子
[联] hat 帽子, helmet 头盔, brim 帽檐, visor 帽舌, sunglasses 太阳镜, uniform 制服, logo 标志
[记] 可数；a cap。cap 动词表「限制、覆盖」（cap the price 限价），不同义。

**scarf**
英/美 /skɑːrf/
n. 围巾；披巾
（复数 scarves /skɑːrvz/ 或 scarfs）
[义] A piece of cloth worn around the neck, head, or shoulders for warmth or decoration. 围在颈、头或肩上的织物，保暖或装饰（wool scarf 羊毛围巾，silk scarf 丝巾）。可数。复数 scarves 更常见。
[例] ① She wrapped a wool scarf around her neck. 她用羊毛围巾围住脖子。 ② He bought a silk scarf as a gift for his mother. 他买了一条丝巾送给母亲。
[搭] wool scarf 羊毛围巾, silk scarf 丝巾, winter scarf 冬用围巾, wrap a scarf 围围巾, lightweight scarf 薄围巾
[联] hat 帽子, glove 手套, coat 大衣, wool 羊毛, silk 丝绸, neck 脖子, warm 保暖的, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；复数 scarves 或 scarfs。动词 scarf 俚语指「猛吃」，不同义。

**glove**
英/美 /ɡlʌv/
n. 手套
[义] A covering for the hand and wrist, usually with separate parts for each finger. 覆盖手掌与手腕、通常分指的手部外罩（leather gloves 皮手套，winter gloves 冬用手套）。可数。boxing glove 拳击手套为加厚专用款。
[例] ① She pulled on her gloves before stepping into the snow. 踏进雪地前她戴上了手套。 ② He lost one glove and couldn't find a matching pair. 他丢了一只手套，找不到配对的了。
[搭] a pair of gloves 一副手套, leather gloves 皮手套, winter gloves 冬用手套, boxing gloves 拳击手套, fit like a glove 非常合身（喻）
[联] mitten 连指手套, hand 手, finger 手指, scarf 围巾, coat 大衣, warm 保暖的, leather 皮革
[记] 可数；a pair of gloves。hand in glove 短语表「密切勾结」。

**belt**
英/美 /belt/
n. 腰带；皮带
v. 用带系紧；猛击
[义] A strip of leather or other material worn around the waist to hold up trousers or as decoration. 绕腰佩戴以固定裤子或作装饰的带条（leather belt 皮带，seat belt 安全带为不同义）。可数。动词 belt 表系紧或猛击（belt out a song 高声唱），与名词搭配不同。
[例] ① He tightened his belt after losing weight. 瘦下来后他把腰带收紧了。 ② The dress came with a matching fabric belt. 这条连衣裙配有一条同料腰带。
[搭] leather belt 皮带, seat belt 安全带, belt buckle 皮带扣, tighten one's belt 收紧腰带/节衣缩食, belt loop 裤耳
[联] buckle 扣, trousers 长裤, pants 裤子, jeans 牛仔裤, waist 腰, strap 带条, loop 环
[记] 可数；a belt。tighten one's belt 亦喻「削减开支」。

**tie**
英/美 /taɪ/
n. 领带；联系
v. 系；捆绑
[义] A long narrow piece of cloth worn knotted around the neck, especially by men with formal clothes. 绕颈打结的狭长织物，尤指男士正装（silk tie 丝绸领带，bow tie 领结）。可数。动词 tie 表捆绑；名词 tie 亦指联系或平局（family ties 家庭纽带）。
[例] ① He chose a blue tie to match his suit. 他选了一条蓝领带配西装。 ② She learned to tie a bow tie for the formal dinner. 她学会了为正式晚宴打领结。
[搭] silk tie 丝绸领带, bow tie 领结, tie and suit 领带配西装, loosen one's tie 松开领带, family ties 家庭纽带（喻）
[联] suit 套装, collar 衣领, shirt 衬衫, formal 正式的, knot 结, blazer 西装外套, business 商务
[记] 可数；a tie。tie 表「平局」为体育语境（the game ended in a tie）。

**watch**
英/美 /wɑːtʃ/ 美 /wɑːtʃ/
n. 手表；监视
v. 观看；注视
[义] A small clock worn on the wrist or carried in a pocket. 戴于腕上或置于口袋的小钟表（wrist watch 手表，smart watch 智能手表）。可数。动词 watch 表观看，与配饰名词同形需辨义。
[例] ① He glanced at his watch and realized he was late. 他瞥了眼手表，发现迟到了。 ② She received a smart watch that tracks her daily steps. 她收到一块记录每日步数的智能手表。
[搭] wrist watch 手表, smart watch 智能手表, pocket watch 怀表, watch strap 表带, check one's watch 看表
[联] clock 钟, time 时间, bracelet 手镯, ring 戒指, jewelry 珠宝, strap 表带, accessory 配饰
[记] 可数；a watch。watch out 当心；watch over 照看，均为动词短语。

**ring**
英/美 /rɪŋ/
n. 戒指；环
v. 响铃；打电话
[义] A small circular band worn on the finger as jewellery. 戴于指上的环形首饰（wedding ring 婚戒，engagement ring 订婚戒）。可数。动词 ring 表铃响或打电话，与首饰义同形。
[例] ① She wears a simple gold ring on her left hand. 她左手戴着一枚素金戒指。 ② He proposed with a diamond engagement ring. 他用钻石订婚戒求婚。
[搭] wedding ring 婚戒, engagement ring 订婚戒, diamond ring 钻戒, gold ring 金戒指, ring size 戒指尺码
[联] necklace 项链, bracelet 手镯, earring 耳环, jewelry 珠宝, finger 手指, gold 金, diamond 钻石, gift 礼物
[记] 可数；a ring。ring 亦指拳击台、电话铃等，需看语境。

**necklace**
英/美 /ˈnekləs/
n. 项链
[义] A piece of jewellery worn around the neck. 绕颈佩戴的首饰（gold necklace 金项链，pearl necklace 珍珠项链）。可数。
[例] ① She inherited a pearl necklace from her grandmother. 她从祖母那里继承了一条珍珠项链。 ② The necklace clasp broke and the beads scattered. 项链扣坏了，珠子散落一地。
[搭] gold necklace 金项链, pearl necklace 珍珠项链, diamond necklace 钻石项链, necklace clasp 项链扣, wear a necklace 戴项链
[联] bracelet 手镯, ring 戒指, earring 耳环, jewelry 珠宝, chain 链, pendant 吊坠, pearl 珍珠
[记] 可数；a necklace。neck + lace 复合，指「颈上的链饰」。

**bracelet**
英/美 /ˈbreɪslət/
n. 手镯；手链
[义] A piece of jewellery worn around the wrist or arm. 绕腕或臂佩戴的首饰（silver bracelet 银手镯，charm bracelet 串饰手链）。可数。
[例] ① She stacked several thin gold bracelets on her wrist. 她在腕上叠戴了几只细金手镯。 ② The charm bracelet held tiny pendants from each trip. 那条串饰手链挂着每次旅行的微型吊坠。
[搭] gold bracelet 金手镯, silver bracelet 银手镯, charm bracelet 串饰手链, leather bracelet 皮手链, friendship bracelet 友谊手链
[联] necklace 项链, ring 戒指, earring 耳环, watch 手表, wrist 手腕, jewelry 珠宝, charm 吊坠
[记] 可数；a bracelet。brace 动词表「支撑」，bracelet 为「臂上的环饰」。

**earring**
英/美 /ˈɪrɪŋ/
n. 耳环；耳饰
[义] A piece of jewellery worn on the earlobe or ear. 佩戴在耳垂或耳部的首饰（stud earrings 耳钉，hoop earrings 圈形耳环）。可数；常用复数 earrings。
[例] ① She lost one earring and couldn't find its pair. 她丢了一只耳环，找不到配对的了。 ② Large hoop earrings framed her face. 大圈形耳环衬着她的脸。
[搭] stud earrings 耳钉, hoop earrings 圈形耳环, drop earrings 坠式耳环, a pair of earrings 一副耳环, gold earrings 金耳环
[联] necklace 项链, ring 戒指, bracelet 手镯, jewelry 珠宝, ear 耳朵, pierce 穿洞（ear piercing 打耳洞）
[记] 可数；常用复数 earrings。a pair of earrings。

**handbag**
英/美 /ˈhændbæɡ/
n. 手提包（尤英式）
[义] A small bag used by women for carrying money and personal items. 女性用以装钱与个人物品的小包。可数。美式更常说 purse 指同类；handbag 在美式亦通用。
[例] ① She left her handbag on the train by mistake. 她误把手提包落在火车上。 ② The handbag matched her shoes and belt. 手提包与她的鞋和腰带相配。
[搭] leather handbag 皮手提包, designer handbag 名牌手提包, handbag strap 包带, leave one's handbag 落下手提包, match one's handbag 包与……相配
[联] purse 钱包/女包（美式）, wallet 钱包, bag 包, shoulder bag 肩包, clutch 手拿包, accessory 配饰, leather 皮革
[记] 可数；a handbag。hand + bag 复合词。

**wallet**
英/美 /ˈwɒlɪt/ 美 /ˈwɑːlɪt/
n. 钱包；皮夹
[义] A small flat case for carrying paper money, cards, and sometimes coins, usually used by men. 装纸币、卡片、有时也装硬币的扁平小夹，男士更常用（leather wallet 皮夹，digital wallet 数字钱包）。可数。
[例] ① He pulled a credit card from his wallet. 他从钱包里抽出一张信用卡。 ② I keep my ID and driver's licence in my wallet. 我把身份证和驾照放在钱包里。
[搭] leather wallet 皮夹, slim wallet 薄款钱包, digital wallet 数字钱包, pull out one's wallet 掏出钱包, empty wallet 空钱包
[联] purse 钱包（美式女式小包亦称 purse）, handbag 手提包, money 钱, card 卡片, pocket 口袋, cash 现金, steal 偷窃
[记] 可数；a wallet。purse 在美式可指女式小包，与 wallet 有重叠，英式 purse 多指装硬币的小袋。

**sunglasses**
英/美 /ˈsʌnɡlæsɪz/
n. 太阳镜
[义] Glasses with dark lenses worn to protect the eyes from bright sunlight. 深色镜片眼镜，用于遮挡强光保护眼睛。复数形；a pair of sunglasses。可数。
[例] ① She put on her sunglasses as they walked out into the glare. 他们走进强光里时她戴上了太阳镜。 ② Polarized sunglasses reduce glare from water and snow. 偏光太阳镜能减少水面和雪面的眩光。
[搭] a pair of sunglasses 一副太阳镜, polarized sunglasses 偏光太阳镜, designer sunglasses 设计师款太阳镜, wear sunglasses 戴太阳镜, sunglasses case 太阳镜盒
[联] glasses 眼镜, lens 镜片, sun 太阳, hat 帽子, cap 便帽, beach 海滩, glare 眩光
[记] 复数；a pair of sunglasses。单数 sunglass 极少用。

**umbrella**
英/美 /ʌmˈbrelə/
n. 雨伞；伞形结构
[义] A device for protection against rain, consisting of a circular canopy on a folding metal frame. 防雨用具，圆形伞面加折叠金属骨架（umbrella stand 伞架）。可数。亦指「伞形覆盖」（umbrella term 总括术语），比喻义。
[例] ① Don't forget your umbrella—it's going to rain. 别忘了伞——要下雨了。 ② She left her umbrella on the bus again. 她又把伞落在公交车上了。
[搭] folding umbrella 折叠伞, umbrella stand 伞架, beach umbrella 沙滩遮阳伞, carry an umbrella 带伞, forget one's umbrella 忘带伞
[联] rain 雨, raincoat 雨衣, wet 湿的, hat 帽子, parasol 阳伞, shade 遮阳, weather 天气
[记] 可数；an umbrella。umbrella organization 指「伞式」上级机构，比喻义。

**cotton**
英/美 /ˈkɒtən/ 美 /ˈkɑːtən/
n. 棉；棉布
adj. 棉制的
[义] A soft white substance that grows around the seeds of the cotton plant, used to make cloth and thread. 棉花植株种子外的柔软白色纤维，用于织布与纺线（cotton fabric 棉布，100% cotton 纯棉）。不可数/可数。形容词表棉质（cotton shirt 棉衬衫）。
[例] ① This T-shirt is made of 100% cotton. 这件 T 恤是百分百纯棉的。 ② Cotton is breathable and comfortable in hot weather. 棉布透气，天热时穿着舒适。
[搭] 100% cotton 纯棉, cotton fabric 棉布, cotton shirt 棉衬衫, cotton field 棉田, organic cotton 有机棉
[联] wool 羊毛, silk 丝绸, linen 亚麻, fabric 布料, denim 丹宁, thread 线, breathable 透气的
[记] 不可数/可数。cotton on 俚语表「领会」（英式），与布料义不同。

**wool**
英/美 /wʊl/
n. 羊毛；毛线
[义] The soft hair of sheep and some other animals, used for making cloth or yarn. 绵羊等动物的柔软毛发，用于织布或纺线（pure wool 纯羊毛，wool sweater 羊毛衫）。不可数。形容词 woolen/woollen 表羊毛制的。
[例] ① The scarf is knitted from soft merino wool. 这条围巾用柔软的美利奴羊毛编织。 ② Wool keeps you warm even when it gets damp. 羊毛即使受潮也能保暖。
[搭] pure wool 纯羊毛, merino wool 美利奴羊毛, wool sweater 羊毛衫, wool blanket 羊毛毯, pull wool 拉羊毛（字面）
[联] cotton 棉, silk 丝绸, sweater 毛衣, scarf 围巾, knit 编织, warm 保暖的, sheep 绵羊
[记] 不可数。a ball of wool 一团毛线。woolen（美式）/ woollen（英式）形容词。

**silk**
英/美 /sɪlk/
n. 丝绸；丝线
adj. 丝绸的
[义] A fine smooth cloth made from the fibres produced by silkworms. 蚕丝纤维织成的细腻光滑织物（silk scarf 丝巾，silk dress 丝绸连衣裙）。不可数。形容词 silk 表丝绸质地（silk tie 丝绸领带）。
[例] ① The blouse is made of pure silk from Italy. 这件女衫是意大利纯丝绸的。 ② Silk feels cool and smooth against the skin. 丝绸贴肤凉爽顺滑。
[搭] pure silk 纯丝绸, silk scarf 丝巾, silk dress 丝绸连衣裙, silk tie 丝绸领带, silk fabric 丝绸面料
[联] cotton 棉, wool 羊毛, fabric 布料, smooth 光滑的, luxury 奢侈, blouse 女式衬衫, scarf 围巾
[记] 不可数。silky 形容词表「丝般柔滑」。silk road 丝绸之路为历史文化词。

**leather**
英/美 /ˈleðər/
n. 皮革
adj. 皮革的
[义] Animal skin treated for use in making shoes, bags, and other goods. 经加工用于制鞋、包等物的动物皮（genuine leather 真皮，leather jacket 皮夹克）。不可数。形容词表皮革制（leather belt 皮带）。
[例] ① The boots are made of genuine leather. 这双靴子是真皮的。 ② Leather ages beautifully if you care for it properly. 皮革保养得当会越用越好看。
[搭] genuine leather 真皮, leather jacket 皮夹克, leather belt 皮带, leather shoes 皮鞋, leather bag 皮包
[联] suede 麂皮, fur 毛皮, skin 皮, shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, belt 腰带, wallet 钱包, durable 耐用的
[记] 不可数。a leather 指「皮制品」较少见，常说 leather goods。leathery 形容词表「皮革般坚韧」。

**denim**
英/美 /ˈdenɪm/
n. 丹宁布；牛仔布
[义] A strong cotton cloth, usually blue, used especially for making jeans. 结实棉织物，通常为蓝色，尤用于牛仔裤（denim jacket 牛仔夹克，denim fabric 丹宁面料）。不可数。
[例] ① She wore a denim jacket over a white T-shirt. 她白 T 恤外穿牛仔夹克。 ② The dress is cut from soft stretch denim. 这条连衣裙用柔软弹力丹宁裁制。
[搭] denim jacket 牛仔夹克, denim jeans 牛仔裤, stretch denim 弹力丹宁, denim fabric 丹宁面料, dark denim 深色丹宁
[联] jeans 牛仔裤, cotton 棉, fabric 布料, jacket 夹克, casual 休闲的, blue 蓝色, sturdy 结实的
[记] 不可数。denim 源自法语 Nîmes（尼姆）产粗布名 serge de Nîmes。

**fabric**
英/美 /ˈfæbrɪk/
n. 织物；布料；结构
[义] Cloth or other material produced by weaving or knitting fibres. 纤维编织或针织而成的布或其他材料（cotton fabric 棉布，synthetic fabric 化纤面料）。不可数/可数。亦指「结构」（the fabric of society 社会结构），比喻义。
[例] ① Choose a breathable fabric for summer clothes. 夏装选透气面料。 ② The fabric of the sofa is starting to wear thin. 沙发面料开始磨薄了。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *fabrica*（工艺、制作），与 forge（锻造）同族。
[族] fabricate 捏造, fabrication 捏造
[搭] cotton fabric 棉布, synthetic fabric 化纤面料, breathable fabric 透气面料, fabric store 布料店, stretch fabric 弹力面料
[联] cloth 布, textile 纺织品, cotton 棉, wool 羊毛, silk 丝绸, thread 线, weave 编织
[记] 不可数/可数。fabric 社会结构义为抽象比喻，与布料义同形。

**lace**
英/美 /leɪs/
n. 蕾丝；鞋带
v. 系鞋带；掺入
[义] A delicate cloth made of threads in an open pattern, used for decoration on clothes. 镂空纹样织成的精致织物，用于衣物装饰（lace trim 蕾丝边，lace collar 蕾丝领）。不可数/可数。亦指鞋带（shoe lace 鞋带），动词 lace 表系鞋带或掺入（lace up your shoes 系鞋带）。
[例] ① The wedding dress was trimmed with handmade lace. 婚纱饰有手工蕾丝。 ② He bent down to lace up his running shoes. 他弯腰系跑步鞋带。
[搭] lace trim 蕾丝边, lace collar 蕾丝领, shoe lace 鞋带, lace up 系上（鞋带）, delicate lace 精致蕾丝
[联] silk 丝绸, fabric 布料, collar 衣领, dress 连衣裙, gown 礼服, delicate 精致的, trim 饰边
[记] lace 与 shoe lace 同词；lace up 系紧。lace 动词「掺烈酒」为另一义。

**collar**
英/美 /ˈkɒlər/ 美 /ˈkɑːlər/
n. 衣领；颈圈
v. 抓住；拦住
[义] The part of a shirt, jacket, or coat that fits around the neck. 衬衫、夹克或大衣绕颈的部分（shirt collar 衬衫领，turn-down collar 翻领）。可数。动词 collar 俚语表抓住某人（collar a suspect 抓住嫌疑人），与服装义不同。
[例] ① He adjusted his collar before entering the meeting room. 进会议室前他整了整衣领。 ② The dog wore a collar with its name on it. 狗戴着刻有名字的颈圈。
[搭] shirt collar 衬衫领, turn-down collar 翻领, collar size 领围尺码, pop one's collar 竖衣领, blue-collar 蓝领（喻）
[联] sleeve 袖子, cuff 袖口, shirt 衬衫, jacket 夹克, neck 脖子, tie 领带, button 纽扣
[记] 可数；a collar。blue-collar/white-collar 蓝领/白领为比喻义。

**sleeve**
英/美 /slɪːv/
n. 袖子
[义] The part of a garment that covers the arm. 衣物遮盖手臂的部分（long sleeves 长袖，short sleeves 短袖，roll up one's sleeves 卷起袖子）。可数。
[例] ① The shirt has short sleeves for summer wear. 这件衬衫是短袖，适合夏天穿。 ② She rolled up her sleeves and started washing the dishes. 她卷起袖子开始洗碗。
[搭] long sleeves 长袖, short sleeves 短袖, roll up one's sleeves 卷起袖子, sleeve length 袖长, shirt sleeves 衬衫袖
[联] collar 衣领, cuff 袖口, arm 手臂, shirt 衬衫, jacket 夹克, sweater 毛衣, button 纽扣
[记] 可数；a sleeve。have sth. up one's sleeve 喻「藏有锦囊妙计」。

**pocket**
英/美 /ˈpɒkɪt/ 美 /ˈpɑːkɪt/
n. 口袋
v. 把……装入口袋；私吞
[义] A small bag sewn into or on clothing for carrying small items. 缝在或附在衣物上的小袋（trouser pocket 裤袋，pocket money 零花钱）。可数。动词 pocket 表装入口袋或私吞款项（pocket the change 把零钱装入口袋）。
[例] ① He kept his keys in his jacket pocket. 他把钥匙放在夹克口袋里。 ② She reached into her pocket and pulled out her phone. 她伸手进口袋掏出手机。
[搭] trouser pocket 裤袋, jacket pocket 夹克口袋, pocket money 零花钱, empty one's pockets 掏空口袋, deep pockets 财力雄厚（喻）
[联] wallet 钱包, key 钥匙, phone 手机, trousers 长裤, jacket 夹克, sew 缝, button 纽扣
[记] 可数；a pocket。pocket 形容词表「袖珍的」（pocket knife 折叠小刀）。

**button**
英/美 /ˈbʌtən/
n. 纽扣；按钮
v. 扣纽扣
[义] A small disk sewn on clothing to fasten it by pushing through a hole or loop. 缝在衣物上、通过扣入孔或环来固定的小圆片（shirt button 衬衫扣，button up 扣上）。可数。亦指机器按钮（press the button 按按钮），与服装扣不同义。
[例] ① One button on his coat was missing. 他大衣上掉了一颗扣子。 ② Button up your coat—it's cold outside. 把外套扣好——外面冷。
[搭] shirt button 衬衫扣, button up 扣上, lose a button 掉扣子, gold button 金扣, button hole 扣眼
[联] zip 拉链, collar 衣领, sleeve 袖子, pocket 口袋, fasten 扣紧, sew 缝, blouse 女式衬衫
[记] 可数；a button。button 与 zip 为两种常见扣合方式。

**zip**
英/美 /zɪp/
n. 拉链
v. 拉上/拉开拉链
[义] A device with two rows of metal or plastic teeth that lock together when pulled. 两排金属或塑料齿互锁的扣合装置（zipper 美式更常说，zip 英式亦作名词）。可数。动词 zip up 拉上拉链；zip 计算机语境表「压缩文件」。
[例] ① The zip on my jacket got stuck halfway. 我夹克上的拉链卡在一半不动了。 ② She zipped up her hoodie before going outside. 出门前她拉上了连帽衫的拉链。
[搭] zip up 拉上拉链, zip fastener 拉链, broken zip 坏拉链, zip pocket 拉链口袋, full zip 全拉链
[联] button 纽扣, jacket 夹克, hoodie 连帽衫, pocket 口袋, fasten 扣紧, stuck 卡住的
[记] 可数；a zip。英式 zip，美式 zipper 更常见。zip file 压缩文件为计算机义。

**size**
英/美 /saɪz/
n. 尺码；大小
v. 按大小分类
[义] One of a series of standard measurements for clothes and shoes. 服装与鞋类的标准尺码之一（What size do you wear? 你穿多大号？，size medium 中号）。可数/不可数。动词 size 表按大小分类或估量（size up 打量）。
[例] ① This sweater is one size too small for me. 这件毛衣我穿小一号。 ② Shoe sizes vary between countries—check the conversion chart. 各国鞋码不同——查换算表。
[搭] What size? 什么尺码？, size medium 中号, shoe size 鞋码, one size fits all 均码, try a larger size 试大一号的
[联] fit 合身, small 小的, large 大的, medium 中号, tailor 裁缝, try on 试穿, measurement 尺寸
[记] 可数/不可数。size up 俚语指「打量、评估」某人。

**fit**
英/美 /fɪt/
v. 合身；适合
adj. 合身的；健康的
n. 合身；发作
[义] To be the right size for someone; to be suitable. 尺码适合某人；合适（The dress fits perfectly 裙子非常合身，fit for work 适合上班穿）。形容词 fit 表合身或健康（stay fit 保持健康）。名词 a good fit 指合身度。
[例] ① These jeans fit me perfectly after I hemmed them. 改裤边后这条牛仔裤非常合身。 ② The jacket is a good fit—neither too tight nor too loose. 这件夹克很合身——既不紧也不松。
[形] fit / fitted / fitted / fitting（英式过去式常为 fit）
[搭] fit perfectly 非常合身, a good fit 合身, tight fit 紧身, loose fit 宽松, fit for 适合于
[联] size 尺码, tailor 裁缝, try on 试穿, tight 紧的, loose 宽松的, alter 修改, hem 改边
[记] 形容词 fit 表「健康」与「合身」同形。fit in 表「融入」；fit out 装备，不同义。

**fashion**
英/美 /ˈfæʃən/
n. 时尚；时装
v. 制作；塑造
[义] A popular style of clothes, hair, etc., at a particular time. 特定时期流行的服装、发型等风格（in fashion 流行，out of fashion 过时，fashion industry 时尚业）。不可数/可数。动词 fashion 表制作（fashion a dress 缝制连衣裙），较少用。
[例] ① Wide-leg trousers are back in fashion this year. 今年阔腿裤又流行回来了。 ② She works in the fashion industry as a designer. 她在时尚业做设计师。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *faction-*（做、制作），与 fact（事实）同族 → 「做出来的样式」→ 时尚。
[族] fashionable 时尚的, unfashionable 过时的
[搭] in fashion 流行, out of fashion 过时, fashion industry 时尚业, fashion show 时装秀, high fashion 高级时装
[联] style 风格, trend 趋势, designer 设计师, clothes 衣服, outfit 一套搭配, model 模特, trendy 时髦的
[记] 不可数/可数。fashionable 形容词；old-fashioned 过时的。

**style**
英/美 /staɪl/
n. 风格；款式
v. 设计款式；造型
[义] A particular way of designing, making, or wearing clothes; a distinctive appearance. 设计、制作或穿着的特定方式；独特外观（personal style 个人风格，a classic style 经典款式）。可数/不可数。动词 style 表造型（style one's hair 做发型）。
[例] ① Her personal style mixes vintage pieces with modern basics. 她的个人风格把复古单品与现代基础款混搭。 ② The jacket comes in several different styles and colours. 这款夹克有多种款式和颜色。
[搭] personal style 个人风格, classic style 经典款式, in style 时髦, out of style 过时, style icon 时尚偶像
[联] fashion 时尚, trend 趋势, design 设计, outfit 一套搭配, elegant 优雅的, casual 休闲的, unique 独特的
[记] 不可数/可数。stylish 形容词表「有格调的」。life style 生活方式为另一常用义。

**laundry**
英/美 /ˈlɒndri/ 美 /ˈlɑːndri/
n. 待洗衣物；洗衣店
[义] Clothes, sheets, etc., that need to be washed or have just been washed; a place where clothes are washed commercially. 需要洗或刚洗好的衣物；商业洗衣场所（do the laundry 洗衣服，laundry basket 洗衣篮，laundry service 洗衣服务）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① I have a pile of laundry waiting to be folded. 我有一堆洗好的衣服等着叠。 ② The hotel offers a laundry service for guests. 酒店为客人提供洗衣服务。
[搭] do the laundry 洗衣服, laundry basket 洗衣篮, laundry detergent 洗衣液, laundry room 洗衣房, laundry service 洗衣服务
[联] wash 洗, clothes 衣服, soap 肥皂, detergent 洗涤剂, dry clean 干洗, iron 熨, fold 叠
[记] 不可数/可数。laundromat（美式）/ launderette（英式）指自助洗衣店。

**iron**
英/美 /ˈaɪən/
n. 熨斗；铁
v. 熨烫
[义] A device with a flat metal base that is heated and used to make clothes smooth. 带加热金属底板的器具，用于熨平衣物（steam iron 蒸汽熨斗，iron a shirt 熨衬衫）。可数。名词亦指金属铁；动词 iron 专指熨烫。
[例] ① She plugged in the iron and waited for it to heat up. 她插上熨斗等着加热。 ② Could you iron this blouse before the meeting? 开会前你能熨一下这件女衫吗？
[搭] steam iron 蒸汽熨斗, iron a shirt 熨衬衫, ironing board 烫衣板, iron out wrinkles 熨平褶皱, hot iron 热熨斗
[联] laundry 洗衣, wrinkle 褶皱, crease 折痕, press 压平, clothes 衣服, collar 衣领, sleeve 袖子
[记] 可数；an iron。iron out 短语表「解决小问题」；cast-iron 铸铁的，不同义。

**fold**
英/美 /foʊld/
v. 折叠；叠好
n. 折痕；羊栏
[义] To bend something, especially cloth or paper, so that one part lies on top of another. 把织物或纸张等弯折使一部分叠在另一部分上（fold clothes 叠衣服，fold a shirt 叠衬衫）。可数名词表折痕。亦指羊栏（sheep fold），罕用。
[形] folded / folded / folding
[例] ① She folded the clean laundry and put it in the drawer. 她把洗好的衣服叠好放进抽屉。 ② Fold the shirt neatly along the seams. 沿接缝把衬衫整齐叠好。
[搭] fold clothes 叠衣服, fold a shirt 叠衬衫, fold neatly 整齐叠好, fold up 折起, fold in half 对折
[联] laundry 洗衣, iron 熨, crease 折痕, drawer 抽屉, closet 衣柜, neat 整齐的, unfold 展开
[记] 规则动词。fold 名词「信徒群体」（the fold）为宗教比喻义。

**stain**
英/美 /steɪn/
n. 污渍；染色
v. 弄脏；染色
[义] A dirty mark on something that is difficult to remove. 难以去除的污迹（coffee stain 咖啡渍，remove a stain 去污渍）。可数。动词 stain 表弄脏或染色（stain wood 给木材上色）。
[例] ① There's a grease stain on your shirt collar. 你衬衫领子上有一块油渍。 ② She tried to remove the wine stain with cold water. 她试着用冷水去掉酒渍。
[搭] coffee stain 咖啡渍, grease stain 油渍, remove a stain 去污渍, stain remover 去污剂, stubborn stain 顽固污渍
[联] dirt 污垢, spot 斑点, wash 洗, laundry 洗衣, bleach 漂白, clean 清洁, spill 洒出
[记] 可数；a stain。stain 动词「染色」用于木材、玻璃等，与衣物污渍义相关但用法不同。

**put on**
英/美 /pʊt ɒn/ 美 /pʊt ɑːn/
v. 穿上；戴上
[义] To place a piece of clothing or an accessory on your body. 把衣物或配饰穿戴到身上（put on your coat 穿上外套，put on makeup 化妆为延伸义）。与 wear 不同：put on 强调「穿上」这一动作，wear 强调「穿着」的状态。
[形] put on / put on / putting on
[例] ① Put on your boots—there's ice on the steps. 穿上靴子——台阶上有冰。 ② She put on a scarf before opening the door. 开门前她围上了围巾。
[搭] put on clothes 穿衣服, put on a coat 穿外套, put on shoes 穿鞋, put on weight 体重增加（不同义）, hurry up and put on 快点穿上
[联] take off 脱下, wear 穿着, dress 穿衣, try on 试穿, layer 分层穿着, coat 外套, shoe 鞋
[记] 短语动词；宾语为代词时须后置（put it on，不说 × put on it）。put on 亦指「上演、增加体重」，需看语境。

**take off**
英/美 /teɪk ɒf/ 美 /teɪk ɔːf/
v. 脱下；起飞
[义] To remove a piece of clothing or an accessory from your body. 从身上取下衣物或配饰（take off your shoes 脱鞋，take off your hat 脱帽）。亦指飞机起飞（The plane took off），与服装义同形。
[形] took off / taken off / taking off
[例] ① Please take off your shoes before entering the house. 进屋前请脱鞋。 ② He took off his jacket and hung it on the chair. 他脱下夹克挂在椅背上。
[搭] take off your shoes 脱鞋, take off a coat 脱外套, take off jewelry 摘下首饰, take off quickly 迅速脱下, help sb. take off 帮某人脱下
[联] put on 穿上, remove 移除, undress 脱衣, shoe 鞋, coat 外套, hat 帽子, layer 分层穿着
[记] 短语动词；宾语为代词时后置（take them off）。take-off 名词指起飞或（口语）模仿秀。

**try on**
英/美 /traɪ ɒn/ 美 /traɪ ɑːn/
v. 试穿；试戴
[义] To put on a piece of clothing or an accessory to see if it fits or suits you before buying. 购买前穿上试试是否合身或合适（try on a dress 试连衣裙，try on shoes 试鞋）。可数宾语；代词须后置（try it on）。
[形] tried on / tried on / trying on
[例] ① Can I try on this jacket in a larger size? 我能试大一号的这件夹克吗？ ② She tried on three pairs of jeans before choosing one. 她试了三条牛仔裤才选定一条。
[搭] try on clothes 试穿衣服, try on a dress 试连衣裙, try on shoes 试鞋, try on in the fitting room 在试衣间试穿, try sth. on 试穿某物
[联] fit 合身, size 尺码, fitting room 试衣间, mirror 镜子, buy 购买, return 退货, tailor 裁缝
[记] 短语动词；商店常用 May I try this on? fitting room（美式）/ changing room（英式）指试衣间。

**raincoat**
英/美 /ˈreɪnkəʊt/
n. 雨衣
[义] A coat made of waterproof material to keep you dry in rain. 防水材质制成的外套，雨天穿着（lightweight raincoat 轻便雨衣，packable raincoat 可折叠雨衣）。可数。与 trench coat 风衣有重叠，raincoat 更强调防雨功能。
[例] ① Don't forget your raincoat—the forecast says showers all day. 别忘了雨衣——预报说全天有阵雨。 ② She folded her raincoat into her bag when the sun came out. 出太阳后她把雨衣折进包里。
[搭] lightweight raincoat 轻便雨衣, waterproof raincoat 防水雨衣, packable raincoat 可折叠雨衣, wear a raincoat 穿雨衣, yellow raincoat 黄色雨衣
[联] coat 外套, umbrella 雨伞, waterproof 防水的, wet 湿的, rain 雨, jacket 夹克, hood 兜帽
[记] 可数；a raincoat。rain + coat 复合词，功能直白。

**overcoat**
英/美 /ˈəʊvəkəʊt/
n. 长大衣；厚外套
[义] A long warm coat worn over other clothes in cold weather. 寒冷天气穿在其他衣服外的长款保暖外套（wool overcoat 羊毛大衣，heavy overcoat 厚大衣）。可数。比 coat 更强调「长、厚、套在最外层」。
[例] ① He buttoned his overcoat up to the collar against the wind. 他把长大衣扣到领子挡风。 ② The overcoat reached almost to his knees. 这件长大衣几乎及膝。
[搭] wool overcoat 羊毛大衣, heavy overcoat 厚大衣, long overcoat 长大衣, wear an overcoat 穿长大衣, overcoat season 穿大衣的季节
[联] coat 外套, jacket 夹克, wool 羊毛, warm 保暖的, winter 冬天, layer 分层穿着, collar 衣领
[记] 可数；an overcoat。over- 表「在外层」，与 undercoat（底漆/内层）不同领域。

**robe**
英/美 /roʊb/
n. 长袍；浴袍
[义] A long loose garment, especially one worn for ceremonies or at home after bathing. 宽松长衣，尤指典礼用袍或居家浴袍（bathrobe 浴袍，dressing gown 晨衣为近义）。可数。与 gown 相比，robe 更强调「袍、裹身」，gown 偏礼服或病号服。
[例] ① She wrapped herself in a soft bathrobe after the shower. 淋浴后她裹上柔软的浴袍。 ② The judge entered the courtroom in a black robe. 法官身着黑袍进入法庭。
[搭] bathrobe 浴袍, dressing robe 晨衣, silk robe 丝绸长袍, tie a robe 系浴袍带, hotel robe 酒店浴袍
[联] gown 长袍/礼服, pajamas 睡衣, towel 毛巾, shower 淋浴, belt 腰带, lace 蕾丝
[记] 可数；a robe。disrobe 动词表「脱衣」，较正式。

**nightgown**
英/美 /ˈnaɪtɡaʊn/
n. 睡袍；睡裙
[义] A loose dress worn in bed, especially by women and girls. 睡觉时穿的宽松睡裙，尤指女式（cotton nightgown 棉质睡袍）。可数。亦作 nightdress；与 pajamas（上下套装）不同。
[例] ① She changed into a cotton nightgown before reading in bed. 睡前她换上棉质睡袍在床上看书。 ② The nightgown had lace trim at the collar. 睡袍领口有蕾丝饰边。
[搭] cotton nightgown 棉质睡袍, silk nightgown 丝绸睡裙, wear a nightgown 穿睡袍, long nightgown 长睡袍, lace-trimmed nightgown 蕾丝边睡袍
[联] pajamas 睡衣, robe 浴袍, sleep 睡觉, cotton 棉, lace 蕾丝, bedroom 卧室, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 可数；a nightgown。night + gown 复合；英式亦说 nightdress。

**bra**
英/美 /brɑː/
n. 胸罩；文胸
[义] A piece of women's underwear worn to support the breasts. 女性用以支撑胸部的内衣（sports bra 运动文胸，wireless bra 无钢圈文胸）。可数。全称 brassiere /brəˈzɪər/，日常极常说 bra。
[例] ① She bought a new sports bra for the gym. 她为健身房买了新的运动文胸。 ② The bra strap was digging into her shoulder. 文胸肩带勒得她肩膀生疼。
[搭] sports bra 运动文胸, wireless bra 无钢圈文胸, bra strap 文胸肩带, bra size 文胸尺码, padded bra 有垫文胸
[联] underwear 内衣, panties 女式内裤, strap 肩带, size 尺码, fit 合身, cotton 棉, lace 蕾丝
[记] 可数；a bra。口语 bra；正式场合或标签可见 brassiere。

**leggings**
英/美 /ˈleɡɪŋz/
n. 紧身裤；打底裤
[义] Tight trousers made of stretchy material, worn by women and girls, often for sport or casual wear. 弹性面料制成的紧身外裤或打底裤，女性穿着，常用于运动或休闲（yoga leggings 瑜伽裤，black leggings 黑色打底裤）。复数形；a pair of leggings。
[例] ① She wore leggings and a long sweater to the coffee shop. 她穿打底裤配长毛衣去咖啡店。 ② These leggings are see-through when you bend over—wear something longer on top. 这条打底裤一弯腰就透——上面配长一点的上衣。
[搭] a pair of leggings 一条打底裤, yoga leggings 瑜伽裤, black leggings 黑色打底裤, stretch leggings 弹力打底裤, high-waisted leggings 高腰打底裤
[联] pants 裤子, yoga 瑜伽, stretch 弹力, tight 紧的, sweater 毛衣, sneakers 运动鞋, sport 运动
[记] 复数；a pair of leggings。legging 单数较少单独用。

**tights**
英/美 /taɪts/
n. 连裤袜；紧身袜
[义] A piece of clothing made of thin material that covers the legs and hips, worn by women and girls. 薄质连腿连臀的贴身袜裤（black tights 黑色连裤袜，sheer tights 透明丝袜）。复数形；a pair of tights。英式 tights 常指连裤袜；美式 pantyhose 同义。
[例] ① She wore black tights with her winter skirt. 她冬裙配黑色连裤袜。 ② I snagged my tights on the edge of the desk. 连裤袜被桌角勾丝了。
[搭] a pair of tights 一条连裤袜, black tights 黑色连裤袜, sheer tights 透明丝袜, thick tights 厚连裤袜, run in one's tights 连裤袜抽丝
[联] stocking 长筒袜, skirt 裙子, dress 连衣裙, sheer 透明的, snags 勾丝, pantyhose 连裤袜（美式）, winter 冬天
[记] 复数；a pair of tights。run（名词）指丝袜「抽丝」：I've got a run in my tights。

**stocking**
英/美 /ˈstɒkɪŋ/ 美 /ˈstɑːkɪŋ/
n. 长筒袜
[义] A close-fitting covering for the foot and leg, usually ending at or above the knee. 紧贴脚与腿的袜套，通常及膝或膝上（Christmas stocking 圣诞袜为不同义）。可数；复数 stockings。与 tights（连裤）不同：stockings 为分腿长袜，常需吊袜带。
[例] ① She hung stockings by the fireplace for the children. 她在壁炉旁为孩子们挂圣诞袜。 ② She wore silk stockings with her formal dress. 她正装配丝绸长筒袜。
[搭] silk stockings 丝绸长筒袜, Christmas stocking 圣诞袜, a pair of stockings 一双长筒袜, nylon stockings 尼龙长筒袜, knee-high stockings 及膝长袜
[联] sock 短袜, tights 连裤袜, silk 丝绸, nylon 尼龙, knee 膝盖, formal 正式的, sheer 透明的
[记] 可数；复数 stockings。Christmas stocking 指圣诞礼物袜，与日常穿着义不同。

**mitten**
英/美 /ˈmɪtən/
n. 连指手套
[义] A type of glove with a separate section for the thumb but not for the other fingers, keeping them together for warmth. 拇指单独、其余四指合在一起的保暖手套（wool mittens 羊毛连指手套，pair of mittens 一副连指手套）。可数。比 glove 更保暖，儿童与严寒地区常见。
[例] ① The toddler's mittens were attached to a string through her coat sleeves. 幼儿的连指手套用绳穿在大衣袖里。 ② My fingers are warmer in mittens than in gloves. 连指手套比分手指的手套更暖和。
[搭] wool mittens 羊毛连指手套, a pair of mittens 一副连指手套, baby mittens 婴儿连指手套, waterproof mittens 防水连指手套, knit mittens 编织连指手套
[联] glove 分手指手套, hand 手, finger 手指, wool 羊毛, winter 冬天, warm 保暖的, snow 雪
[记] 可数；a mitten。mitt 为口语简称；boxing mitt 指拳击手套，不同义。

**flip-flop**
英/美 /ˈflɪp flɒp/ 美 /ˈflɪp flɑːp/
n. 人字拖；夹脚凉鞋
[义] A type of sandal with a thong between the toes and a flat sole, held on by a Y-shaped strap. 趾间有带、Y 形带固定的平底夹脚凉鞋。可数。亦作 flip flop；美式极口语，海滩与居家高频。
[例] ① He kicked off his flip-flops before walking on the hot sand. 走上烫脚沙子前他踢掉了人字拖。 ② Flip-flops are fine for the pool but not for long walks. 人字拖泳池边穿可以，长途步行不行。
[搭] a pair of flip-flops 一双人字拖, beach flip-flops 沙滩人字拖, rubber flip-flops 橡胶人字拖, wear flip-flops 穿人字拖, cheap flip-flops 便宜人字拖
[联] sandal 凉鞋, slipper 拖鞋, beach 海滩, summer 夏天, toe 脚趾, sole 鞋底, casual 休闲的
[记] 可数；a pair of flip-flops。flip-flop 动词表「改变立场」，政治语境常见，与鞋义不同。

**trainer**
英 /ˈtreɪnə(r)/
n. 运动鞋（英式）；教练
[义] 英式：a sports shoe with a rubber sole（rubber-soled trainers 橡胶底运动鞋）；美式更常说 sneaker。可数；复数 trainers。亦指教练（fitness trainer 健身教练），须看语境。
[例] ① He bought a new pair of trainers for the gym. 他为健身房买了双新运动鞋。 ② In the UK, "trainers" means sneakers, not someone who coaches you. 在英国，trainers 指运动鞋，不是教练。
[搭] a pair of trainers 一双运动鞋, gym trainers 健身运动鞋, white trainers 白色运动鞋, running trainers 跑步鞋, leather trainers 皮面运动鞋
[联] sneaker 运动鞋（美式）, shoe 鞋, rubber 橡胶, gym 健身房, running 跑步, casual 休闲的, sport 运动
[记] 可数；复数 trainers。英美差异：英式 trainer = 美式 sneaker；trainer 作「教练」为高频另一义。

**heel**
英/美 /hiːl/
n. 鞋跟；脚后跟
[义] The raised part at the back of a shoe under the heel of the foot; the back part of the foot. 鞋后部垫高部分；脚的后跟（high heels 高跟鞋，stiletto heel 细高跟）。可数。动词 heel 指狗跟随、/list heel 航海，与鞋义不同。
[例] ① These heels are too high for walking on cobblestones. 这双鞋跟太高，走石板路受不了。 ② The rubber heel on my boot wore down. 我靴子上的橡胶鞋跟磨平了。
[搭] high heels 高跟鞋, stiletto heel 细高跟, low heel 低跟, heel height 鞋跟高度, at one's heels 紧跟着（喻）
[联] sole 鞋底, shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, toe 脚趾, foot 脚, ankle 踝, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 可数；a heel。achilles heel 喻「致命弱点」；head over heels 神魂颠倒。

**sole**
英/美 /soʊl/
n. 鞋底；脚底；灵魂
[义] The bottom surface of a shoe that touches the ground; the underside of the foot. 鞋与地面接触的底面；脚掌底部（rubber sole 橡胶底，leather sole 皮底）。可数。亦指灵魂（soul 同音异拼），与鞋义同形需辨义。
[例] ① The soles of my sneakers are worn smooth. 我运动鞋的鞋底已经磨平了。 ② She felt a pebble through the thin sole of her sandal. 她透过凉鞋薄底感觉到一颗小石子。
[搭] rubber sole 橡胶底, leather sole 皮底, sole of the shoe 鞋底, worn sole 磨平的鞋底, non-slip sole 防滑底
[联] heel 鞋跟, shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, foot 脚, ground 地面, grip 抓地力, tread 花纹底
[记] 可数；a sole。sole 与 soul /soʊl/ 同音；fish sole 指「鳎鱼」，不同义。

**purse**
英/美 /pɜːs/
n. 钱包；女式小包（美式）
[义] 美式：a small bag used by women for money and personal items（与 handbag 常互换）；英式：装硬币的小袋或女式钱包。可数。地域差异：美式 purse 可指整个手提包，英式 purse 多指装钱的小包。
[例] ① She rummaged through her purse for her keys. 她在包里翻找钥匙。 ② In British English, a purse often means a small wallet for coins. 在英式英语里，purse 常指装硬币的小钱包。
[搭] leather purse 皮钱包/女包, coin purse 零钱包, designer purse 名牌女包, rummage through one's purse 翻包, purse strings 财权（喻）
[联] handbag 手提包, wallet 钱包, bag 包, money 钱, clutch 手拿包, leather 皮革, keys 钥匙
[记] 可数；a purse。hold the purse strings 掌管开支。英美义有重叠，国际交流须留意。

**clutch**
英/美 /klʌtʃ/
n. 手拿包；离合器
v. 紧握
[义] A small handbag without handles, carried in the hand, often for formal occasions. 无提手、手持的小包，多用于正式场合（evening clutch 晚宴手拿包）。可数。亦指汽车离合器；动词 clutch 表紧抓，与包义同形。
[例] ① She carried a gold clutch to the wedding reception. 她带着金色手拿包赴婚宴。 ② The clutch matched her heels and bracelet. 手拿包与她的高跟鞋和手镯相配。
[搭] evening clutch 晚宴手拿包, gold clutch 金色手拿包, clutch bag 手拿包, carry a clutch 拿手拿包, small clutch 小手包
[联] handbag 手提包, purse 女包, formal 正式的, wedding 婚礼, elegant 优雅的, bracelet 手镯, heel 鞋跟
[记] 可数；a clutch。clutch 动词「紧抓」与名词包义同形；in the clutch 体育喻「关键时刻」。

**parasol**
英/美 /ˈpærəsɒl/ 美 /ˈpærəsɔːl/
n. 阳伞；遮阳伞
[义] A lightweight umbrella used to give shade from the sun, not rain. 轻便遮阳伞，用于遮挡阳光而非雨水（lace parasol 蕾丝阳伞，beach parasol 沙滩遮阳伞）。可数。与 umbrella（雨伞）区分：parasol 强调遮阳、常更装饰性。
[例] ① She opened a white parasol against the afternoon sun. 她撑开一把白色阳伞遮挡午后阳光。 ② Victorian ladies often carried lace parasols as fashion accessories. 维多利亚时代女士常持蕾丝阳伞作时尚配饰。
[搭] lace parasol 蕾丝阳伞, beach parasol 沙滩遮阳伞, open a parasol 撑阳伞, white parasol 白色阳伞, sun parasol 遮阳伞
[联] umbrella 雨伞, sun 太阳, shade 遮阳, hat 帽子, beach 海滩, summer 夏天, accessory 配饰
[记] 可数；a parasol。para-（防）+ sol（太阳）；现代日常 umbrella 常兼指晴雨两用。

**jewelry**
英 /ˈdʒuːəlri/ 美 /ˈdʒuːəlri/
n. 珠宝；首饰（总称）
[义] Objects such as rings, necklaces, and bracelets worn for decoration, usually made of precious metals and stones. 戒指、项链、手镯等装饰性佩戴物，通常由贵金属与宝石制成。不可数。英式拼写 jewellery。
[例] ① She keeps her jewelry in a small box on the dresser. 她把首饰放在梳妆台的小盒里。 ② He bought jewelry as a gift for their anniversary. 他买了首饰作周年礼物。
[搭] gold jewelry 金首饰, costume jewelry 人造首饰, fine jewelry 高档珠宝, jewelry box 首饰盒, piece of jewelry 一件首饰
[联] ring 戒指, necklace 项链, bracelet 手镯, earring 耳环, gold 金, diamond 钻石, accessory 配饰, watch 手表
[记] 不可数；一件说 a piece of jewelry。英式 jewellery；jeweler（美式）/ jeweller（英式）珠宝商。

**accessory**
英/美 /əkˈsesəri/
n. 配饰；附件
[义] An extra item of clothing or decoration that completes an outfit, such as a belt, scarf, or bag. 完善整体穿搭的额外衣物或装饰，如腰带、围巾、包（fashion accessories 时尚配饰，hair accessories 发饰）。可数。
[例] ① The store sells shoes and accessories on the second floor. 商店二楼卖鞋和配饰。 ② She chose simple accessories so the dress would stand out. 她选了简洁配饰以突出连衣裙。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *accessorius*（附加的），*accedere*（接近）→ 附加在主体上的物品。
[族] access 接近, accessible 可进入的
[搭] fashion accessories 时尚配饰, hair accessories 发饰, matching accessories 配套配饰, accessory department 配饰部, belt and accessories 腰带与配饰
[联] scarf 围巾, belt 腰带, handbag 手提包, jewelry 珠宝, hat 帽子, outfit 一套搭配, complete 完善
[记] 可数；an accessory。accessory 法律义「从犯」与配饰义同形，需看语境。

**wardrobe**
英/美 /ˈwɔːdrəʊb/
n. 衣橱；全部衣物
[义] A tall cupboard for storing clothes; the clothes that a person owns. 存放衣物的立柜；某人拥有的全部衣物（built-in wardrobe 嵌入式衣橱，capsule wardrobe 胶囊衣橱）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① She organized her wardrobe by colour and season. 她按颜色和季节整理衣橱。 ② He has a small wardrobe—just a few shirts and two suits. 他衣物不多——几件衬衫和两套西装。
[搭] built-in wardrobe 嵌入式衣橱, walk-in wardrobe 步入式衣帽间, capsule wardrobe 胶囊衣橱, wardrobe malfunction 着装意外（媒体用语）, update one's wardrobe 更新衣橱
[联] closet 衣柜（美式）, clothes 衣服, hanger 衣架, drawer 抽屉, outfit 一套搭配, fashion 时尚, organize 整理
[记] 可数/不可数。ward + robe 原指「放袍子的房间」。wardrobe malfunction 指公开场合衣着意外走光。

**closet**
英/美 /ˈklɒzɪt/ 美 /ˈklɑːzɪt/
n. 衣柜；壁橱
[义] A small room or cupboard for storing clothes and other items. 存放衣物等物品的小房间或柜（walk-in closet 步入式衣帽间，bedroom closet 卧室衣柜）。可数。美式 closet 常指嵌入式衣柜；英式更常说 wardrobe 或 cupboard。
[例] ① Hang the coat in the closet by the door. 把外套挂在门边衣柜里。 ② She sorted through her closet and donated clothes she never wore. 她整理衣柜，捐了从不穿的衣服。
[搭] walk-in closet 步入式衣帽间, bedroom closet 卧室衣柜, closet door 柜门, come out of the closet 出柜（喻, 不同义）, clean out the closet 清理衣柜
[联] wardrobe 衣橱, hanger 衣架, clothes 衣服, drawer 抽屉, shelf 架子, organize 整理, donate 捐赠
[记] 可数；a closet。come out of the closet 指「公开性取向」，与衣柜本义无关。

**hanger**
英/美 /ˈhæŋə(r)/
n. 衣架
[义] A shaped piece of wood, plastic, or metal used for hanging clothes in a closet. 用于在衣柜中挂衣物的木质、塑料或金属架（coat hanger 衣架，wire hanger 铁丝衣架）。可数。
[例] ① There weren't enough hangers for all the shirts. 衣架不够挂所有衬衫。 ② She slipped the dress onto a padded hanger. 她把连衣裙套在软垫衣架上。
[搭] coat hanger 衣架, wire hanger 铁丝衣架, padded hanger 软垫衣架, clothes hanger 晾衣架, hang on a hanger 挂在衣架上
[联] closet 衣柜, wardrobe 衣橱, shirt 衬衫, dress 连衣裙, hang 悬挂, wrinkle 褶皱, iron 熨
[记] 可数；a hanger。hang 动词与 hanger 名词；hanger 亦指「悬挂物、挂钩」。

**linen**
英/美 /ˈlɪnən/
n. 亚麻布；亚麻制品
[义] Cloth made from the fibres of the flax plant; sheets, tablecloths, and sometimes summer clothing. 亚麻纤维织成的布；亦指床单、桌布及夏装（linen shirt 亚麻衬衫，pure linen 纯亚麻）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① A linen shirt stays cooler than cotton in humid weather. 潮湿天气里亚麻衬衫比棉的更凉爽。 ② The hotel provides fresh linen every day. 酒店每天更换干净床品。
[搭] pure linen 纯亚麻, linen shirt 亚麻衬衫, linen fabric 亚麻面料, table linen 餐桌布艺, fresh linen 干净床品
[联] cotton 棉, silk 丝绸, fabric 布料, summer 夏天, breathable 透气的, shirt 衬衫, sheet 床单
[记] 不可数/可数。linen 原指「亚麻」，现常泛指「床品桌布」。line（线）与 linen 不同词。

**polyester**
英/美 /ˈpɒliestə(r)/ 美 /ˈpɑːliestər/
n. 聚酯纤维；涤纶
[义] A man-made material used to make cloth, often blended with cotton or wool. 人造合成纤维，常用于混纺（polyester blend 涤棉混纺，100% polyester 纯涤纶）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The dress is a cotton-polyester blend that resists wrinkles. 这条连衣裙是棉涤混纺，不易皱。 ② Pure polyester can feel hot in summer. 纯涤纶夏天穿着可能闷热。
[搭] polyester blend 混纺, 100% polyester 纯涤纶, polyester fabric 涤纶面料, cotton-polyester 棉涤, wrinkle-resistant polyester 抗皱涤纶
[联] cotton 棉, nylon 尼龙, fabric 布料, synthetic 合成的, blend 混纺, wrinkle 褶皱, wash 洗
[记] 不可数/可数。poly- + ester（化学酯）→ 合成纤维名。易洗快干，但透气性通常不如天然纤维。

**nylon**
英/美 /ˈnaɪlɒn/ 美 /ˈnaɪlɑːn/
n. 尼龙
[义] A strong synthetic material used for stockings, ropes, and various fabrics. 强韧合成材料，用于丝袜、绳索及多种织物（nylon stockings 尼龙袜，nylon jacket 尼龙夹克）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Nylon stockings were once an everyday item for office wear. 尼龙丝袜曾是日常通勤标配。 ② The backpack is made of lightweight nylon. 这款背包由轻量尼龙制成。
[搭] nylon stockings 尼龙袜, nylon fabric 尼龙面料, lightweight nylon 轻量尼龙, nylon blend 尼龙混纺, sheer nylon 透明尼龙
[联] stocking 长筒袜, tights 连裤袜, synthetic 合成的, strong 强韧的, fabric 布料, sheer 透明的
[记] 不可数/可数。20 世纪 30 年代杜邦商标名入英语。nylon 亦指「尼龙弦」乐器配件，不同义。

**suede**
英/美 /sweɪd/
n. 麂皮；绒面革
adj. 麂皮制的
[义] Soft leather with a slightly rough surface on the inside, used for shoes, jackets, and bags. 内面略粗糙的软皮革，用于鞋、夹克、包（suede jacket 麂皮夹克，suede shoes 麂皮鞋）。不可数。形容词表麂皮质地。
[例] ① Suede shoes need special cleaner—don't use water. 麂皮鞋需专用清洁剂——别用水。 ② She wore a brown suede jacket over a white T-shirt. 她白 T 恤外搭棕色麂皮夹克。
[搭] suede jacket 麂皮夹克, suede shoes 麂皮鞋, suede bag 麂皮包, suede cleaner 麂皮清洁剂, soft suede 柔软麂皮
[联] leather 皮革, jacket 夹克, shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, soft 柔软的, brush 刷子, stain 污渍
[记] 不可数。源自法语 Suède（瑞典），因瑞典革工艺得名。遇水易留渍，保养须注意。

**fur**
英/美 /fɜː(r)/
n. 毛皮；皮草
adj. 毛皮的
[义] The thick soft hair that covers the bodies of some animals; clothing made from this. 某些动物身上的厚软毛；以此制成的服装（fake fur 人造皮草，fur coat 皮草大衣）。不可数/可数。现代消费语境中 faux fur / fake fur 更常见。
[例] ① She wore a faux fur collar on her winter coat. 她冬大衣配人造毛领。 ② Real fur has become controversial in many fashion markets. 真毛皮在许多时尚市场已引发争议。
[搭] fake fur 人造皮草, fur coat 皮草大衣, fur collar 毛领, faux fur 仿毛皮, fur trim 毛皮饰边
[联] leather 皮革, wool 羊毛, coat 大衣, collar 衣领, warm 保暖的, animal 动物, ethical 伦理的
[记] 不可数/可数。fur 亦指动物「软毛」（the cat's fur），与「皮草业」义相关。真毛皮在多国受伦理与法规限制。

**fleece**
英/美 /fliːs/
n. 抓绒；羊毛
[义] A soft warm synthetic fabric that feels like sheep's wool, used for jackets and pullovers. 仿羊毛触感的保暖合成绒布，用于夹克与套头衫（polar fleece 摇粒绒，fleece jacket 抓绒夹克）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① A fleece jacket is light but keeps you warm on chilly mornings. 抓绒夹克轻便，清晨却够暖和。 ② The pullover is made of recycled fleece. 这件套头衫由再生抓绒制成。
[搭] polar fleece 摇粒绒, fleece jacket 抓绒夹克, fleece pullover 抓绒套头衫, soft fleece 柔软抓绒, recycled fleece 再生抓绒
[联] wool 羊毛, sweater 毛衣, jacket 夹克, warm 保暖的, synthetic 合成的, lightweight 轻量的, outdoor 户外
[记] 不可数/可数。fleece 原义「羊毛剪下的一层」；现多指 synthetic fleece。fleece 动词表「欺诈搜刮」，不同义。

**tailor**
英/美 /ˈteɪlə(r)/
n. 裁缝
v. 量身定制；调整
[义] A person whose job is making or altering clothes to fit a particular customer. 按顾客身材制作或修改衣物的人（bespoke tailor 定制裁缝，tailor-made 量身定做）。可数。动词 tailor 表定制或调整以适应（tailor a suit 定制西装，tailor sth. to 调整以适应）。
[例] ① The tailor took his measurements for a new suit. 裁缝为他量体做新西装。 ② She had the dress tailored to fit her perfectly. 她把连衣裙改到完全合身。
[搭] bespoke tailor 定制裁缝, tailor-made 量身定做, tailor a suit 定制西装, visit the tailor 去裁缝店, master tailor 大师傅
[联] fit 合身, alter 修改, hem 改边, suit 套装, measurement 尺寸, sew 缝, fabric 布料
[记] 可数；a tailor。tailor 动词「量身定制」可喻指调整方案（tailor a plan）。

**alter**
英/美 /ˈɔːltə(r)/
v. 修改；改变
[义] To change the size or shape of a piece of clothing so that it fits better. 改变衣物尺寸或形状以改善合身度（alter a dress 改连衣裙，alter the hem 改裤边）。广义亦指改变（alter plans 改变计划）。
[形] altered / altered / altering
[例] ① The seamstress can alter the waist so the trousers fit. 女裁缝可以改腰围让裤子合身。 ② They altered the sleeves to make them shorter. 他们把袖子改短了。
[搭] alter a dress 改连衣裙, alter the fit 改合身度, alter the hem 改裤边, have sth. altered 请人改衣, minor alterations 小改动
[联] tailor 裁缝, hem 改边, fit 合身, sew 缝, seam 接缝, size 尺码, shorten 缩短
[记] 规则动词。alter 与 altar（祭坛）同音异义；alteration 名词指改动。

**hem**
英/美 /hem/
n. 衣边；褶边
v. 缝边；改短
[义] The edge of a piece of cloth that is folded and sewn; to sew or adjust this edge. 布料折边缝制的边缘；给……缝边或改短（hem a skirt 给裙子缝边，take up the hem 把裙边改短）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The dress was too long, so she had the hem taken up. 裙子太长，她把裙边改短了。 ② Check that the hem is even before you sew it. 缝之前确认衣边是否齐整。
[搭] take up the hem 改短衣边, hem a skirt 给裙子缝边, uneven hem 不齐的衣边, rolled hem 卷边, let out the hem 放长衣边
[联] alter 修改, tailor 裁缝, sew 缝, skirt 裙子, dress 连衣裙, seam 接缝, length 长度
[记] 可数/动词。hem and haw 短语表「支支吾吾」，与衣边义无关。

**dry clean**
英/美 /draɪ kliːn/
v. 干洗
n. 干洗
[义] To clean clothes using chemicals instead of water. 用化学溶剂而非水洗清洁衣物（dry-clean only 只能干洗，take to the dry cleaner's 送去干洗店）。名词 dry cleaning 指干洗服务或过程。
[形] dry-cleaned / dry-cleaned / dry-cleaning
[例] ① This wool coat must be dry-cleaned—do not wash it at home. 这件羊毛大衣必须干洗——别在家水洗。 ② She picked up her dry cleaning on the way home. 她回家路上取了干洗的衣服。
[搭] dry-clean only 只能干洗, take to the dry cleaner's 送干洗, dry cleaning service 干洗服务, dry-clean a suit 干洗西装, professional dry cleaning 专业干洗
[联] laundry 洗衣, wash 洗, wool 羊毛, suit 套装, stain 污渍, iron 熨, delicate 娇贵的
[记] 复合动词；标签常见 Dry clean only。dry cleaner 指干洗店或干洗工。

**bleach**
英/美 /bliːtʃ/
n. 漂白剂
v. 漂白；使褪色
[义] A chemical used to make cloth white or remove colour; to treat fabric with this chemical. 使织物变白或去色的化学品；用漂白剂处理（chlorine bleach 氯漂，bleach whites 漂白白色衣物）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① Don't use bleach on coloured clothes—it will fade the fabric. 别在彩色衣物上用漂白剂——会把面料褪色。 ② The sun had bleached his hair lighter over the summer. 一夏天太阳把他的头发晒浅了。
[搭] chlorine bleach 氯漂, bleach whites 漂白白色衣物, color-safe bleach 护色漂白, bleach stain 漂白渍, avoid bleach 避免使用漂白剂
[联] stain 污渍, laundry 洗衣, white 白色的, fade 褪色, detergent 洗涤剂, wash 洗, fabric 布料
[记] 不可数/动词。bleach 亦指头发漂色剂；sun-bleached 指日晒褪色。

**swimsuit**
英/美 /ˈswɪmsuːt/
n. 泳衣
[义] A piece of clothing worn for swimming, especially by women and girls. 游泳时穿着的衣物，尤指女式（one-piece swimsuit 连体泳衣，bathing suit 美式同义）。可数。男士常用 swim trunks；竞技用 swimwear 为总称。
[例] ① She packed a swimsuit for the hotel pool. 她为酒店泳池备了泳衣。 ② The swimsuit dried quickly in the sun. 泳衣在阳光下干得很快。
[搭] one-piece swimsuit 连体泳衣, bathing suit 泳衣（美式）, try on a swimsuit 试泳衣, swim trunks 男式泳裤, swimwear 泳装（总称）
[联] beach 海滩, pool 泳池, summer 夏天, sandal 凉鞋, towel 毛巾, waterproof 防水的, sport 运动
[记] 可数；a swimsuit。swim + suit 复合；bikini 比基尼为两-piece 款式。

**tracksuit**
英/美 /ˈtræksuːt/
n. 运动套装
[义] A loose matching jacket and trousers worn for sports or casual comfort. 宽松配套的夹克与长裤，用于运动或休闲（cotton tracksuit 棉质运动套装，tracksuit bottoms 运动裤）。可数。美式亦说 sweat suit 或 jogging suit。
[例] ① He changed into a tracksuit before heading to the gym. 去健身房前他换上了运动套装。 ② She lived in a grey tracksuit during exam week. 考试周她整天穿灰色运动套装。
[搭] cotton tracksuit 棉质运动套装, tracksuit bottoms 运动裤, matching tracksuit 配套运动服, wear a tracksuit 穿运动套装, zip-up tracksuit 拉链运动套装
[联] hoodie 连帽衫, sneakers 运动鞋, gym 健身房, casual 休闲的, sweatpants 运动裤, sport 运动, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 可数；a tracksuit。track + suit；英式高频，美式 sweat suit 亦常见。

**tuxedo**
英/美 /tʌkˈsiːdəʊ/
n. 男士晚礼服；无尾礼服
[义] A man's formal black or white jacket worn with a bow tie for evening events. 男士正式晚宴穿着的黑/白礼服上装，配领结（black-tie tuxedo 黑领结礼服，rent a tuxedo 租礼服）。可数。英式 dinner jacket 同义；全称 tuxedo jacket。
[例] ① He rented a tuxedo for the charity gala. 他为慈善晚宴租了无尾礼服。 ② The invitation said black tie, so everyone wore tuxedos. 请柬写 black tie，大家都穿了礼服。
[搭] black-tie tuxedo 黑领结礼服, rent a tuxedo 租礼服, tuxedo jacket 礼服上装, white tuxedo 白色礼服, bow tie 领结
[联] suit 套装, tie 领带, bow tie 领结, formal 正式的, wedding 婚礼, gala 晚宴, elegant 优雅的
[记] 可数；a tuxedo。源自纽约 Tuxedo Park；英式 dinner jacket / DJ 同指。

**costume**
英/美 /ˈkɒstjuːm/ 美 /ˈkɑːstuːm/
n. 戏服；化装服
[义] A set of clothes worn by an actor or at a party to look like a particular character or period. 演员或派对上模仿特定人物或时代的服装（Halloween costume 万圣节化装服，national costume 民族服装）。可数。
[例] ① The children wore pirate costumes for the school play. 孩子们为学校戏剧穿了海盗戏服。 ② She spent weeks designing costumes for the film. 她花数周为电影设计戏服。
[源] 源自意大利语 *costume*（习俗、打扮），与 custom（习俗）同族。
[族] customary 习惯的
[搭] Halloween costume 万圣节化装服, national costume 民族服装, costume party 化装派对, wear a costume 穿戏服, costume designer 服装设计师
[联] uniform 制服, outfit 一套搭配, dress 穿着, theatre 戏剧, party 派对, mask 面具, character 角色
[记] 可数；a costume。swimming costume 英式指「泳衣」，与美式 swimsuit 同义。

**apron**
英/美 /ˈeɪprən/
n. 围裙
[义] A piece of clothing worn over the front of other clothes to keep them clean while cooking or working. 烹饪或劳作时罩在其他衣服前部以防弄脏的衣物（kitchen apron 厨房围裙，leather apron 皮围裙）。可数。
[例] ① She tied on an apron before starting to bake. 开始烘焙前她系上围裙。 ② The barista wiped his hands on a striped apron. 咖啡师在条纹围裙上擦了擦手。
[搭] kitchen apron 厨房围裙, leather apron 皮围裙, tie on an apron 系围裙, striped apron 条纹围裙, waist apron 腰围裙
[联] cook 烹饪, kitchen 厨房, stain 污渍, protect 保护, belt 腰带, pocket 口袋, uniform 制服
[记] 可数；an apron。apron 亦指机场停机坪（tarmac apron），航空义。

**seam**
英/美 /siːm/
n. 接缝；线缝
[义] A line where two pieces of cloth are sewn together in a garment. 衣物中两块布料缝合处的线（side seam 侧缝，seam ripper 拆线器）。可数。动词 seam 表「产生裂缝/接缝」，日常少用。
[例] ① The seam on my pocket came undone. 我口袋上的线缝开了。 ② Fold the fabric along the seam before ironing. 熨之前沿接缝把布料折好。
[搭] side seam 侧缝, seam ripper 拆线器, split a seam 接缝裂开, along the seam 沿接缝, reinforced seam 加固缝
[联] sew 缝, stitch 针脚, fabric 布料, alter 修改, hem 衣边, thread 线, tailor 裁缝
[记] 可数；a seam。seam 亦指煤矿层、球缝（baseball seam），需看语境。

**sew**
英/美 /soʊ/
v. 缝；缝纫
[义] To join or repair cloth by making stitches with a needle and thread. 用针线在布料上缝合或修补（sew a button 缝扣子，sew by hand 手缝）。不规则变化。
[形] sewed / sewn / sewing（英式过去分词亦用 sewed）
[例] ① Can you sew this button back on my shirt? 你能把这颗扣子缝回我衬衫上吗？ ② She learned to sew her own clothes in high school. 她高中学会了给自己做衣服。
[搭] sew a button 缝扣子, sew by hand 手缝, sew a seam 缝线, sewing machine 缝纫机, sew sth. up 缝好
[联] thread 线, needle 针, seam 接缝, stitch 针脚, tailor 裁缝, fabric 布料, button 纽扣
[记] 不规则：sew / sewed / sewn / sewing。sew 与 sow（播种）/ so（所以）同音异义。

**knit**
英/美 /nɪt/
v. 编织；针织
[义] To make cloth by interlocking loops of yarn with needles; to join closely. 用针钩织线圈成布；亦指密切结合（knit a sweater 织毛衣，knit together 紧密结合）。不规则变化。
[形] knit / knitted / knitting（美式过去式亦用 knit）
[例] ① Her grandmother taught her to knit scarves in winter. 祖母教她冬天织围巾。 ② The fabric is tightly knit and holds its shape well. 这种面料织得密，不易变形。
[搭] knit a sweater 织毛衣, hand-knit 手织的, knit together 紧密结合, knitting needle 织针, knitwear 针织品
[联] wool 羊毛, sweater 毛衣, scarf 围巾, yarn 毛线, stitch 针脚, thread 线, warm 保暖的
[记] 不规则：knit / knitted / knitting。knit 形容词（knit dress 针织连衣裙）指针织结构。

**textile**
英/美 /ˈtekstaɪl/
n. 纺织品；纺织品
[义] A type of cloth or fabric, especially one made by weaving or knitting; the industry that produces such materials. 织物或面料，尤指机织或针织品；纺织业（textile industry 纺织业，textile factory 纺织厂）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The region depends on textile exports for much of its income. 该地区收入很大程度依赖纺织品出口。 ② Organic textiles are becoming more popular in fashion. 有机面料在时尚界越来越受欢迎。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *texere*（编织），与 texture（质地）同族。
[族] texture 质地, textile 纺织的（形容词）
[搭] textile industry 纺织业, textile factory 纺织厂, organic textile 有机面料, textile design 纺织设计, textile export 纺织品出口
[联] fabric 布料, cotton 棉, wool 羊毛, silk 丝绸, weave 编织, industry 工业, fashion 时尚
[记] 可数/不可数。textile 偏行业/材料总称；fabric 偏具体布种。

**casual**
英/美 /ˈkæʒuəl/
adj. 休闲的；非正式的
[义] Not formal or dressy; suitable for everyday relaxed wear. 非正式、非盛装；适合日常放松穿着（casual clothes 休闲装，casual Friday 休闲星期五）。形容词。名词 casual 指「临时工/便鞋」较少见。
[例] ① The office allows casual clothes on Fridays. 公司周五允许穿休闲装。 ② Jeans and a T-shirt are perfect for a casual dinner with friends. 牛仔裤配 T 恤，朋友便餐刚好。
[搭] casual clothes 休闲装, casual wear 休闲穿着, casual Friday 休闲星期五, smart casual 便正装, dress casual 穿着随意
[联] formal 正式的, jeans 牛仔裤, T-shirt T 恤, sneakers 运动鞋, outfit 一套搭配, comfortable 舒适的, relaxed 放松的
[记] 形容词。smart casual 指「整洁但不必西装」的便正装；casual 亦指「偶然、临时」，需看语境。

**formal**
英/美 /ˈfɔːml/
adj. 正式的；正装场合的
[义] Suitable for official or serious occasions requiring dress clothes. 适合需着正装的正式或庄重场合（formal wear 正装，formal dress code 正装规定）。形容词。名词 a formal 美式指正式舞会。
[例] ① The invitation specified formal attire—suits or evening gowns. 请柬要求正装——西装或晚礼服。 ② Black shoes are more formal than white sneakers. 黑皮鞋比白运动鞋更正式。
[搭] formal wear 正装, formal dress code 正装规定, formal occasion 正式场合, formal shoes 正装皮鞋, black-tie formal 黑领结正式
[联] casual 休闲的, suit 套装, tie 领带, tuxedo 礼服, gown 礼服, elegant 优雅的, ceremony 典礼
[记] 形容词。formal 亦指「格式化的、官方的」；formal education Formal education 与服装义无关。

**waterproof**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtəpruːf/
adj. 防水的
v. 使防水
[义] Not allowing water to pass through; treated to resist water. 不透水；经处理可防水的（waterproof jacket 防水夹克，waterproof boots 防水靴）。形容词/动词。
[例] ① Invest in a good waterproof coat if you hike in the rain. 雨天徒步值得买件好防水外套。 ② These boots are waterproof up to the ankle. 这双靴子到踝部都防水。
[搭] waterproof jacket 防水夹克, waterproof boots 防水靴, waterproof fabric 防水面料, fully waterproof 完全防水, waterproof coating 防水涂层
[联] raincoat 雨衣, boot 靴子, jacket 夹克, wet 湿的, rain 雨, outdoor 户外, fabric 布料
[记] 形容词。water + proof（防）复合；water-resistant 防泼水（程度较弱）与之近义有别。

**trend**
英/美 /trend/
n. 趋势；流行风向
v. 趋向；成为热门
[义] A general direction in which fashion or style is developing. 时尚或风格发展的大方向（fashion trend 时尚趋势，set a trend 引领潮流）。可数。动词 trend 指成为热门话题（ trending on social media），与服装名词义相关。
[例] ① Wide shoulders were a big trend in eighties fashion. 宽肩是八十年代时尚大潮流。 ② Neutral colours are the current trend this season. 本季流行中性色。
[搭] fashion trend 时尚趋势, set a trend 引领潮流, follow the trend 跟风, current trend 当前潮流, trend forecast 趋势预测
[联] fashion 时尚, style 风格, popular 流行的, designer 设计师, season 季节, outdated 过时的, vintage 复古的
[记] 可数；a trend。trendy 形容词表「时髦的」；trend 亦指数据/社会趋势，不限于服装。

**designer**
英/美 /dɪˈzaɪnə(r)/
n. 设计师
adj. 设计师品牌的
[义] A person who creates fashion, clothes, or accessories; made by a famous fashion designer. 设计服装或配饰的人；著名设计师品牌出品的（fashion designer 时装设计师，designer clothes 名牌设计师款）。可数。形容词 designer 指设计师品牌（designer handbag 名牌包）。
[例] ① She works as a fashion designer in Milan. 她在米兰做时装设计师。 ② Designer labels charge more for the name as much as the quality. 名牌卖的不只是质量，还有名字。
[搭] fashion designer 时装设计师, designer clothes 设计师品牌服装, designer label 名牌, designer handbag 名牌包, costume designer 服装设计师
[联] fashion 时尚, style 风格, trend 趋势, brand 品牌, model 模特, elegant 优雅的, expensive 昂贵的
[记] 可数；a designer。design 动词表「设计」；designer 形容词须看是否真为设计师品牌，勿滥用。

**loafer**
英/美 /ˈləʊfə(r)/
n. 乐福鞋；便鞋
[义] A flat leather shoe without laces, that slips onto the foot. 无鞋带、套脚穿的扁平皮鞋（leather loafers 皮乐福，penny loafer 便士乐福）。可数。商务休闲与日常皆宜，比 sneaker 更正式。
[例] ① He wore brown loafers with chinos for the client lunch. 见客户午餐他穿卡其裤配棕色乐福鞋。 ② Loafers are easy to slip on when you're in a hurry. 赶时间时乐福鞋一蹬就走。
[搭] leather loafers 皮乐福, penny loafer 便士乐福, slip-on loafers 套脚乐福, brown loafers 棕色乐福, wear loafers 穿乐福鞋
[联] shoe 鞋, sneaker 运动鞋, leather 皮革, formal 正式的, casual 休闲的, slipper 拖鞋, heel 鞋跟
[记] 可数；a loafer。loafer 亦指「懒人、游荡者」，与鞋义同形需辨义。

**sweatshirt**
英/美 /ˈswetʃɜːrt/
n. 运动卫衣；卫衣
[义] A warm casual top made of thick cotton, usually with long sleeves and no buttons. 厚棉休闲上装，通常长袖、无扣（grey sweatshirt 灰色卫衣，crew-neck sweatshirt 圆领卫衣）。可数。与 hoodie 区别：sweatshirt 无帽；hoodie 有连帽。
[例] ① She threw on a sweatshirt over her gym clothes. 她在健身服外套了件卫衣。 ② University sweatshirts are popular campus souvenirs. 大学卫衣是热门校园纪念品。
[搭] grey sweatshirt 灰色卫衣, crew-neck sweatshirt 圆领卫衣, oversized sweatshirt 宽松卫衣, cotton sweatshirt 棉质卫衣, university sweatshirt 大学卫衣
[联] hoodie 连帽衫, sweater 毛衣, tracksuit 运动套装, cotton 棉, casual 休闲的, fleece 抓绒, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a sweatshirt。sweat + shirt；sweatpants 常成套出现。

**sweatpants**
英/美 /ˈswetpænts/
n. 运动裤
[义] Loose warm trousers made of thick cotton, worn for sport or relaxing at home. 厚棉宽松长裤，运动或居家穿着（grey sweatpants 灰色运动裤，joggers 慢跑裤为近义款式）。复数形；a pair of sweatpants。
[例] ① After work he changed into sweatpants and a T-shirt. 下班后他换上运动裤和 T 恤。 ② These sweatpants have pockets—most gym pairs don't. 这条运动裤有口袋——多数健身款没有。
[搭] a pair of sweatpants 一条运动裤, grey sweatpants 灰色运动裤, cotton sweatpants 棉质运动裤, elastic waist 松紧腰, jogger sweatpants 束脚运动裤
[联] sweatshirt 卫衣, tracksuit 运动套装, hoodie 连帽衫, leggings 打底裤, casual 休闲的, gym 健身房, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 复数；a pair of sweatpants。亦说 joggers / track pants，款式略有差异。

**polo shirt**
英/美 /ˈpəʊləʊ ʃɜːrt/
n. polo 衫；Polo 领 T 恤
[义] A casual shirt with a collar, a few buttons at the neck, and short sleeves. 有领、颈下几颗纽扣、短袖的休闲上衣（striped polo 条纹 polo，golf polo 高尔夫 polo 衫）。可数。比 T-shirt 更整洁，比 dress shirt 更休闲。
[例] ① The office dress code allows polo shirts on casual Fridays. 公司休闲星期五允许穿 polo 衫。 ② He tucked his polo shirt into his chinos for a neat look. 他把 polo 衫塞进卡其裤，显得利落。
[搭] striped polo 条纹 polo, golf polo 高尔夫 polo 衫, cotton polo shirt 棉质 polo, short-sleeve polo 短袖 polo, tuck in a polo 把 polo 塞进裤腰
[联] shirt 衬衫, T-shirt T 恤, collar 衣领, button 纽扣, casual 休闲的, sleeve 袖子, neat 整洁的
[记] 可数；a polo shirt。名字来自马球（polo）运动穿搭，今已日常化。

**tank top**
英/美 /tæŋk tɒp/ 美 /tæŋk tɑːp/
n. 背心；无袖衫
[义] A sleeveless shirt with wide shoulder straps, worn in hot weather or for sport. 宽肩带无袖上衣，天热或运动时穿（cotton tank top 棉质背心，workout tank 健身背心）。可数。英式亦说 vest top；美式 vest 义不同。
[例] ① She wore a tank top and shorts to the beach. 她穿背心和短裤去海滩。 ② Layer a tank top under a sheer blouse. 在透视女衫里搭一件背心。
[搭] cotton tank top 棉质背心, workout tank 健身背心, sleeveless tank 无袖背心, ribbed tank top 罗纹背心, wear a tank top 穿背心
[联] T-shirt T 恤, sleeve 袖子, sleeveless 无袖的, shorts 短裤, summer 夏天, layer 分层穿着, sport 运动
[记] 可数；a tank top。tank 原指「水箱、坦克」，此处为口语服饰名。

**windbreaker**
英/美 /ˈwɪndbreɪkə(r)/
n. 防风夹克
[义] A light jacket designed to protect against wind, usually made of nylon or polyester. 防风用轻便夹克，多为尼龙或涤纶（packable windbreaker 可折叠防风夹克）。可数。英式亦说 windcheater；比 raincoat 更强调防风而非防大雨。
[例] ① Pack a windbreaker—the coastal trail can get breezy. 带件防风夹克——海岸步道可能风大。 ② The windbreaker folds into its own pocket. 这件防风夹克能折进自带口袋里。
[搭] packable windbreaker 可折叠防风夹克, lightweight windbreaker 轻便防风夹克, nylon windbreaker 尼龙防风夹克, zip-up windbreaker 拉链防风夹克, wear a windbreaker 穿防风夹克
[联] jacket 夹克, raincoat 雨衣, nylon 尼龙, waterproof 防水的, outdoor 户外, layer 分层穿着, zip 拉链
[记] 可数；a windbreaker。wind + breaker 复合，功能直白。

**trench coat**
英/美 /trentʃ kəʊt/
n. 风衣； trench 大衣
[义] A long belted raincoat, typically in beige or khaki, with a double-breasted front. 长款系带防雨大衣，常为米色或卡其，双排扣（classic trench coat 经典款风衣）。可数。一战中 trench warfare 得名，后成都市经典外套。
[例] ① She belted her trench coat against the spring drizzle. 春雨中她把风衣腰带系紧。 ② A classic trench coat never really goes out of style. 经典款风衣永不过时。
[搭] classic trench coat 经典风衣, belted trench coat 系带风衣, beige trench coat 米色风衣, double-breasted trench coat 双排扣风衣, wear a trench coat 穿风衣
[联] coat 大衣, raincoat 雨衣, belt 腰带, collar 衣领, formal 正式的, elegant 优雅的, waterproof 防水的
[记] 可数；a trench coat。trench 本义「战壕」，一战军官常穿此类大衣。

**waistcoat**
英 /ˈweɪskəʊt/ 美 /ˈweɪskɔːt/
n. 马甲；西装背心（英式）
[义] A sleeveless garment worn over a shirt and under a jacket, matching a suit. 穿在衬衫外、夹克内的无袖上装，与西装配套（three-piece suit waistcoat 三件套马甲）。可数。美式 vest 同指西装马甲；英式 vest 却常指内衣。
[例] ① He wore a grey waistcoat with his wedding suit. 他婚礼西装配灰色马甲。 ② In the UK, a waistcoat is part of a three-piece suit. 在英国，waistcoat 是三件套的一部分。
[搭] suit waistcoat 西装马甲, three-piece waistcoat 三件套马甲, wool waistcoat 羊毛马甲, waistcoat and tie 马甲配领带, formal waistcoat 正式马甲
[联] vest 背心（美式义不同）, suit 套装, blazer 西装外套, tie 领带, formal 正式的, button 纽扣, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a waistcoat。英美：英式 waistcoat = 美式 suit vest；勿与美式 undershirt vest 混淆。

**bow tie**
英/美 /bəʊ taɪ/
n. 领结
[义] A men's tie in the form of a bow, worn for formal events. 男士打结式领带，用于正式场合（black bow tie 黑领结，self-tie bow tie 手打领结）。可数。与 long tie（长领带）相对；black tie 指正式着装要求。
[例] ① He struggled to tie his bow tie before the gala. 晚宴前他笨手笨脚地打领结。 ② The waiters wore black bow ties and white shirts. 服务员白衬衫配黑领结。
[搭] black bow tie 黑领结, self-tie bow tie 手打领结, clip-on bow tie 夹式领结, bow tie and tuxedo 领结配礼服, tie a bow tie 打领结
[联] tie 领带, tuxedo 礼服, formal 正式的, suit 套装, collar 衣领, shirt 衬衫, elegant 优雅的

**panties**
英/美 /ˈpæntiz/
n. 女式内裤
[义] A piece of women's underwear covering the area between the waist and the tops of the legs. 女性穿于腰至腿根之间的内裤（cotton panties 棉质内裤，lace panties 蕾丝内裤）。复数形；a pair of panties。美式常用；英式亦说 knickers。
[例] ① She packed clean panties for the weekend trip. 周末旅行她备了干净内裤。 ② Cotton panties are more comfortable in hot weather. 天热时棉质内裤更舒服。

[记] 复数；a pair of panties。英式 knickers 同义；panty 单数作定语（panty line 内裤痕）。

**briefs**
英/美 /briːfs/
n. 男式三角内裤
[义] Men's underpants that are tight-fitting and cover the area from the waist to the top of the thighs. 男式贴身三角内裤（cotton briefs 棉质三角裤，boxer briefs 平角内裤为混合款）。复数形；a pair of briefs。
[例] ① He prefers cotton briefs for everyday wear. 日常他更穿棉质三角内裤。 ② Pack enough briefs for a week-long trip. 一周旅行要备够内裤。

[记] 复数；a pair of briefs。brief 形容词「简短」与 briefs 名词不同义。

**boxers**
英/美 /ˈbɒksəz/ 美 /ˈbɑːksərz/
n. 平角内裤；拳击短裤
[义] Loose men's underpants, or shorts worn for boxing. 男式宽松平角内裤；或拳击运动短裤（cotton boxers 棉质平角裤，boxer shorts 拳击短裤）。复数形；a pair of boxers。与 briefs 相比更宽松。
[例] ① He sleeps in an old T-shirt and boxers. 他穿旧 T 恤和平角裤睡觉。 ② Boxers are looser than briefs but can bunch under trousers. 平角裤比三角裤松，但穿外裤时可能打褶。

[联] briefs 三角内裤, underwear 内衣, shorts 短裤, cotton 棉, comfortable 舒适的, sport 运动, boxing 拳击
[记] 复数；a pair of boxers。boxer briefs 为介于 briefs 与 boxers 之间的款式。

**undershirt**
英/美 /ˈʌndərʃɜːrt/
n. 汗衫；打底背心
[义] A thin shirt worn under other clothes, next to the skin. 贴身穿在其他衣服内的薄打底衫（cotton undershirt 棉质汗衫，sleeveless undershirt 无袖打底背心）。可数。美式常用；英式 vest 常指同类内衣。
[例] ① He wore an undershirt beneath his dress shirt in winter. 冬天他在正装衬衫里穿汗衫。 ② The undershirt kept sweat off his good shirt. 汗衫把汗挡在好衬衫外面。

[联] shirt 衬衫, underwear 内衣, vest 背心（英式内衣义）, layer 分层穿着, cotton 棉, sweat 汗, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 可数；an undershirt。under + shirt 复合；英式常称 vest（与 waistcoat 不同）。

**bikini**
英/美 /bɪˈkiːni/
n. 比基尼

[例] ① She bought a new bikini for the beach holiday. 她为海滩度假买了新比基尼。 ② Bikini styles range from sporty to very minimal. 比基尼款式从运动到极简都有。

**veil**
英/美 /veɪl/
n. 面纱；头纱
v. 遮盖
[义] A piece of fine material worn by a bride to cover the face or head, or for religious/cultural dress. 新娘遮面或遮头的薄纱（wedding veil 婚纱头纱）；亦指宗教/文化头纱。可数。动词 veil 表遮盖（veiled criticism 含沙射影的批评），比喻义。
[例] ① She lifted her veil for the first kiss. 初吻时她掀起了头纱。 ② The bride wore a long lace veil over her hair. 新娘长发上覆长款蕾丝头纱。

[联] gown 礼服, wedding 婚礼, lace 蕾丝, bride 新娘, dress 连衣裙, ceremony 典礼, elegant 优雅的

英/美 /kʌf/
n. 袖口；手铐
v. 给……装袖口
[义] The end part of a sleeve at the wrist; the turned-back fold on a trouser leg. 袖子腕部；裤脚翻边（French cuffs 法式叠袖，cufflinks 袖扣）。可数。亦指手铐（in cuffs），与服装义同形。
[例] ① He adjusted his cufflinks before shaking hands. 握手前他整了整袖扣。 ② Roll the cuffs to show off your new watch. 卷起袖口露出手表。

**cufflink**
英/美 /ˈkʌflɪŋk/
n. 袖扣
[义] A small decorative object used to fasten the cuffs of a dress shirt instead of buttons. 固定正装衬衫叠袖的小装饰扣，替代纽扣（gold cufflinks 金袖扣，pair of cufflinks 一副袖扣）。可数；常用复数 cufflinks。
[例] ① He wore silver cufflinks with his wedding suit. 婚礼西装他配银袖扣。 ② Cufflinks are required with French cuffs—buttons won't work. 法式叠袖必须用袖扣——纽扣不行。

v. 覆盖

英/美 /plæd/
n. 格子呢；格子图案
adj. 格子图案的

**pattern**

n. 图案；花样；版型

[例] ① The dress has a small floral pattern that looks elegant. 这条裙子是小碎花，显得雅致。 ② She bought a sewing pattern to make her own skirt. 她买了纸样自己裁裙子。

**thread**
英/美 /θred/
n. 线；线索
v. 穿线
[义] A long thin strand of cotton, nylon, or silk used for sewing. 缝纫用的棉、尼龙或 silk 细线（spool of thread 线轴，match the thread to the fabric 线色配布）。不可数/可数。动词 thread 表穿线或 threading needle。

**yarn**
英/美 /jɑːn/
n. 纱线；毛线

[例] ① She bought soft yarn to knit a winter scarf. 她买了柔软毛线织冬围巾。 ② Don't pull too hard—you'll break the yarn. 别猛拉——会把线拉断。

**needle**

n. 针
[义] A small thin piece of polished metal with a point at one end and a hole for thread, used for sewing. 一端尖、一端有针眼的细金属缝纫针（sewing needle 缝衣针，needle and thread 针和线）。可数。亦指注射针、唱针，需看语境。
[例] ① Thread the needle before you start hemming the skirt. 给裙边缝边前先穿针。 ② She pricked her finger on the needle. 她被针扎了手指。
