**stitch**
英/美 /stɪtʃ/
n. 针脚；一针
v. 缝；缝合
[义] One loop of thread in sewing, or the action of sewing. 缝纫中的一针或针脚（drop a stitch 漏一针，stitches 缝线/伤口缝针为不同义）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Check that every stitch is even along the hem. 检查衣边针脚是否均匀。 ② She stitched a patch onto the torn jeans. 她在破牛仔裤上缝了块补丁。
[搭] drop a stitch 漏一针, even stitches 均匀针脚, hand-stitched 手缝的, stitch up 缝好, running stitch 平针
[联] sew 缝, thread 线, needle 针, seam 接缝, hem 衣边, fabric 布料, tailor 裁缝
[记] 可数/动词。stitch up 英式俚语指「陷害」；in stitches 喻「笑破肚皮」。

**wrinkle**
英/美 /ˈrɪŋkl/
n. 褶皱
v. 起皱
[义] A small fold or line in cloth caused by crushing or age; to form such folds. 布料因挤压或久穿形成的小折痕（iron out wrinkles 熨平褶皱，wrinkle-resistant fabric 抗皱面料）。可数/动词。
[例] ① This linen shirt wrinkles easily in a suitcase. 这件亚麻衬衫放行李箱里容易皱。 ② She ironed the wrinkles out of the dress. 她把连衣裙的褶皱熨平了。
[搭] iron out wrinkles 熨平褶皱, wrinkle-resistant 抗皱的, deep wrinkles 深褶, smooth out wrinkles 抚平褶皱, wrinkle-free 免烫的
[联] iron 熨, crease 折痕, fold 折叠, laundry 洗衣, fabric 布料, stain 污渍, neat 整洁的
[记] 可数/动词。wrinkle 亦指皮肤皱纹（facial wrinkles），与布料义相关。

**crease**
英/美 /kriːs/
n. 折痕；裤线
v. 弄皱；压出折痕
[义] A line made by folding or pressing cloth; the sharp line pressed into trouser legs. 折叠或压烫形成的线；西裤裤线（sharp crease 清晰裤线，crease-resistant 不易起皱）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He wanted a sharp crease in his dress trousers. 他想要正装裤有清晰裤线。 ② Don't sit too long or you'll crease your suit. 别坐太久——会把西装坐皱。
[搭] sharp crease 清晰裤线, trouser crease 裤线, crease-resistant 不易起皱, iron a crease 烫出裤线, remove creases 去掉折痕
[联] wrinkle 褶皱, iron 熨, fold 折叠, trousers 长裤, suit 套装, neat 整洁的, formal 正式的
[记] 可数/动词。crease 亦指「转折点」（a crease in the game），体育喻义。

**detergent**
英/美 /dɪˈtɜːdʒənt/
n. 洗涤剂
[义] A liquid or powder used for washing clothes and removing dirt. 洗衣用液体或粉末清洁剂（laundry detergent 洗衣液，mild detergent 温和洗涤剂）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Use a mild detergent for wool sweaters. 羊毛衫要用温和洗涤剂。 ② She measured detergent into the washing machine drawer. 她把洗衣液量进洗衣机抽屉。
[搭] laundry detergent 洗衣液, mild detergent 温和洗涤剂, liquid detergent 洗衣液, powder detergent 洗衣粉, high-efficiency detergent 高效洗涤剂
[联] laundry 洗衣, wash 洗, bleach 漂白, soap 肥皂, fabric 布料, stain 污渍, machine 机器
[记] 不可数/可数。detergent 亦指「去垢剂」用于其他清洁，不限洗衣。

**shrink**
英/美 /ʃrɪŋk/
v. 缩水；收缩
[义] To become smaller when washed, especially in hot water; of cloth losing size. 洗涤后变小，尤指热水洗（This sweater shrank 这件毛衣缩水了，preshrunk cotton 预缩棉）。不规则变化。
[形] shrank / shrunk / shrinking（过去分词英式亦用 shrunken）
[例] ① Always check the label—wool can shrink in hot water. 务必看标签——羊毛热水会缩水。 ② The T-shirt shrank after I put it in the dryer. T 恤进烘干机后缩水了。
[搭] shrink in the wash 洗后缩水, preshrunk 预缩的, shrink to fit 缩至合身, avoid shrinking 避免缩水, cold wash to prevent shrinkage 冷水防缩
[联] wash 洗, laundry 洗衣, wool 羊毛, cotton 棉, sweater 毛衣, size 尺码, fit 合身
[记] 不规则：shrink / shrank / shrunk / shrinking。shrink 亦指「退缩、缩小规模」，需看语境。

**fade**
英/美 /feɪd/
v. 褪色
[义] To lose colour when washed often or exposed to sun. 多次洗涤或日晒后颜色变淡（fade in the sun 日晒褪色，colours fade 颜色褪去）。规则动词。名词 fade 指渐变发型（a fade haircut），不同义。
[例] ① Black jeans fade faster if you wash them often. 黑牛仔裤洗勤了褪色更快。 ② Hang dark clothes inside out to stop them fading. 深色衣服翻面晾可减缓褪色。
[搭] fade in the sun 日晒褪色, colours fade 颜色褪去, fade-resistant 不易褪色的, prevent fading 防褪色, faded jeans 褪色牛仔裤
[联] bleach 漂白, wash 洗, colour 颜色, denim 丹宁, jeans 牛仔裤, sun 太阳, laundry 洗衣
[记] 规则动词。fade 亦指「逐渐消失」（hope fades），与颜色义相关。

**fitting room**
英/美 /ˈfɪtɪŋ ruːm/
n. 试衣间
[义] A small room in a shop where customers try on clothes before buying. 商店内供顾客试穿的小隔间（try on in the fitting room 在试衣间试穿）。可数。美式 fitting room；英式亦说 changing room。
[例] ① She took three dresses into the fitting room. 她拿三条裙子进了试衣间。 ② The fitting room mirror was unflattering—bring your own judgment. 试衣间镜子不讨好——还得靠自己判断。
[搭] try on in the fitting room 在试衣间试穿, fitting room mirror 试衣镜, fitting room attendant 试衣间服务员, queue for the fitting room 排队等试衣间, three items per fitting room 试衣间限三件
[联] try on 试穿, size 尺码, fit 合身, mirror 镜子, buy 购买, return 退货, shop 商店
[记] 可数；a fitting room。英式 changing room 亦用于公共泳池更衣室，语境不同。

**footwear**
英/美 /ˈfʊtweə(r)/
n. 鞋类（总称）
[义] Things worn on the feet, such as shoes, boots, and sandals. 穿在脚上的物品总称，如鞋、靴、凉鞋。不可数。比 shoes 更正式、涵盖更广。
[例] ① The store has a large footwear section on the ground floor. 商店一楼有大型鞋类区。 ② Waterproof footwear is essential for the hike. 徒步必备防水鞋类。
[搭] footwear section 鞋类区, waterproof footwear 防水鞋类, casual footwear 休闲鞋类, athletic footwear 运动鞋类, footwear brand 鞋类品牌
[联] shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, sandal 凉鞋, sneaker 运动鞋, sock 袜子, size 鞋码, leather 皮革
[记] 不可数。foot + wear 复合；headwear 指「头饰总称」，对称结构。

**sportswear**
英/美 /ˈspɔːtsweə(r)/
n. 运动装（总称）
[义] Clothes designed for sport or casual athletic wear. 为运动或休闲运动穿着设计的服装总称（sportswear brand 运动品牌，sportswear line 运动系列）。不可数。
[例] ① The brand expanded from footwear into full sportswear. 该品牌从鞋类扩展到完整运动装。 ② Sportswear is acceptable on many campuses as daily dress. 许多校园日常可穿运动装。
[搭] sportswear brand 运动品牌, sportswear line 运动系列, casual sportswear 休闲运动装, athletic sportswear 竞技运动装, sportswear collection 运动系列
[联] tracksuit 运动套装, sneaker 运动鞋, hoodie 连帽衫, leggings 打底裤, gym 健身房, casual 休闲的, brand 品牌
[记] 不可数。sport + wear 复合；与 activewear、athleisure 近义，后者更偏「运动风日常穿搭」。

**vintage**
英/美 /ˈvɪntɪdʒ/
adj. 复古的；老款的
n. 复古款；佳酿年份
[义] Referring to clothes from a past era, especially from the 1920s–1990s, that are worn again for style. 指过去年代（尤 1920–1990 年代）因风格而再度穿着的旧衣（vintage dress 复古连衣裙，vintage store 古着店）。形容词/名词。亦指葡萄酒年份，不同领域。
[例] ① She found a vintage leather jacket at the flea market. 她在跳蚤市场淘到一件复古皮夹克。 ② Vintage pieces mix well with modern basics. 复古单品与现代基础款很好搭。
[搭] vintage dress 复古连衣裙, vintage store 古着店, vintage fashion 复古时尚, vintage find 淘到的复古好物, mix vintage and modern 复古与现代混搭
[联] fashion 时尚, style 风格, trend 趋势, second-hand 二手的, thrift store 古着店, unique 独特的, outdated 过时的
[记] 形容词/名词。vintage 原指葡萄酒「年份」；时尚语境指「有年代感的旧款」，不等于 merely old。

**brand**
英/美 /brænd/
n. 品牌
v. 打烙印
[义] A type of product made by a particular company, especially in fashion (luxury brand 奢侈品牌，brand name 品牌名). 某公司生产的商品类型，时尚语境尤常见。可数。动词 brand 表烙印或塑造品牌。
[例] ① She prefers lesser-known brands with better quality control. 她更偏好小众但品控更好的品牌。 ② Luxury brands charge a premium for their labels. 奢侈品牌靠标签溢价。
[搭] luxury brand 奢侈品牌, brand name 品牌名, fashion brand 时尚品牌, own-brand 自有品牌, brand loyalty 品牌忠诚度
[联] designer 设计师, fashion 时尚, label 标签, expensive 昂贵的, quality 质量, logo 标志, trendy 时髦的
[记] 可数；a brand。brand-new 全新的；branding 指品牌营销。

**elegant**
英/美 /ˈelɪɡənt/
adj. 优雅的；雅致的
[义] Graceful and stylish in appearance or manner; attractive and showing good taste in dress. 外观或举止优雅有品；穿着显品位（elegant dress 雅致连衣裙，elegant simplicity 简约雅致）。形容词。
[例] ① She chose an elegant black dress for the awards ceremony. 颁奖典礼她选了一条雅致黑裙。 ② Less is more—an elegant outfit doesn't need many accessories. 少即是多——优雅穿搭不必堆砌配饰。
[源] 源自拉丁语 *elegans*（精选的、讲究的），*eligere*（挑选）→ 精挑细选出的品位。
[族] elegance 优雅
[搭] elegant dress 雅致连衣裙, elegant style 优雅风格, elegant simplicity 简约雅致, look elegant 显得优雅, elegant evening wear 优雅晚装
[联] formal 正式的, style 风格, gown 礼服, sophisticated 精致的, classy 有品位的, casual 休闲的, sloppy 邋遢的
[记] 形容词。elegant 偏「品位与线条」；fancy 偏「花哨装饰」。

**fasten**
英/美 /ˈfɑːsn/ 美 /ˈfæsn/
v. 扣紧；系牢
[义] To close or attach clothing by doing up buttons, zips, belts, or buckles. 通过扣纽扣、拉拉链、系腰带或扣扣来闭合或固定衣物（fasten your coat 扣好外套，fasten a belt 系紧腰带）。规则动词。
[形] fastened / fastened / fastening
[例] ① Fasten your seatbelt—oh, and button your coat too. 系安全带——哦，外套也扣好。 ② The dress fastens at the back with a hidden zip. 这条裙子背后隐形拉链开合。
[搭] fasten a coat 扣好外套, fasten a belt 系紧腰带, fasten buttons 扣纽扣, fasten a zip 拉拉链, securely fastened 系牢的
[联] button 纽扣, zip 拉链, belt 腰带, buckle 扣, tie 领带, unfasten 解开, secure 牢固的
[记] 规则动词。fasten 与 hasten（加快）勿混；fastener 指「扣件、紧固件」。

**match**
英/美 /mætʃ/
v. 相配；搭配
n. 比赛；火柴
[义] To look good together when worn; to choose colours or styles that go well. 穿着在一起好看；选择协调的颜色或款式（match your shoes to your belt 鞋配腰带，matching outfits 配套穿搭）。动词高频于穿搭。名词 match 指比赛或火柴，不同义。
[形] matched / matched / matching
[例] ① Do these socks match my tie? 这袜子配我的领带吗？ ② She always matches her handbag to her shoes. 她总让包和鞋颜色相配。
[搭] match colours 配色, matching outfits 配套穿搭, match shoes and belt 鞋配腰带, mix and match 混搭, perfectly matched 完美搭配
[联] outfit 一套搭配, colour 颜色, accessory 配饰, belt 腰带, shoe 鞋, coordinate 协调, clash 冲突（颜色）
[记] 动词穿搭义高频。mix and match 指「混搭多件单品」；match 亦指「比赛、火柴」。

**apparel**
英/美 /əˈpærəl/
n. 服装（正式）
[义] Clothes, especially when sold or described in a formal or commercial context. 衣物，尤指商业或正式语境（women's apparel 女装，apparel industry 服装业）。不可数。比 clothes 正式，零售与行业常用。
[例] ① The trade fair featured apparel from over thirty countries. 展销会有三十多国服装。 ② Athletic apparel sales grew during the pandemic. 疫情期间运动装销售增长。
[搭] women's apparel 女装, men's apparel 男装, apparel industry 服装业, athletic apparel 运动装, apparel manufacturer 服装制造商
[联] clothing 衣服, garment 一件衣, fashion 时尚, textile 纺织, retail 零售, sportswear 运动装, brand 品牌
[记] 不可数。apparel 偏商业/行业用语；日常口语多说 clothes。

**attire**
英/美 /əˈtaɪə(r)/
n. 着装（正式）
[义] Clothes, especially of a particular type or for a particular occasion. 衣物，尤指特定类型或场合（formal attire 正装，business attire 商务着装，dress attire 礼服要求）。不可数。比 clothes 正式，邀请函、 dress code 常用。
[例] ① The invitation specified formal attire. 请柬要求正装。 ② Business attire usually means a suit, not jeans. 商务着装通常指西装而非牛仔裤。
[搭] formal attire 正装, business attire 商务着装, casual attire 休闲装, dress attire 礼服要求, appropriate attire 得体着装
[联] formal 正式的, casual 休闲的, suit 套装, dress code 着装规定, clothes 衣服, elegant 优雅的, wedding 婚礼
[记] 不可数。attire 较书面；casual attire 在美式职场语境指「不必西装但仍整洁」。

**slacks**
英/美 /slæks/
n. 西裤；休闲长裤（美式）
[义] Trousers worn for work or smart casual occasions, especially in American English. 美式中指上班或 smart casual 场合穿的长裤（dress slacks 正装西裤，pair of slacks 一条西裤）。复数形；a pair of slacks。比 pants 略正式，常指非牛仔的整洁长裤。
[例] ① He wore grey slacks and a blazer to the presentation. 汇报时他穿灰色西裤配西装外套。 ② These slacks need hemming—they're too long. 这条西裤得改短——太长了。
[搭] a pair of slacks 一条西裤, dress slacks 正装西裤, grey slacks 灰色西裤, pleated slacks 褶裥西裤, press slacks 烫西裤
[联] pants 裤子, trousers 长裤, chinos 卡其裤, blazer 西装外套, formal 正式的, crease 裤线, hem 改边
[记] 复数；a pair of slacks。slack 形容词「松弛的」与 slacks 名词不同义。

**chinos**
英/美 /ˈtʃiːnəʊz/
n. 卡其裤；休闲长裤
[义] Casual trousers made from a cotton twill fabric, usually khaki-coloured. 斜纹棉制成的休闲长裤，常为卡其色（khaki chinos 卡其裤，pair of chinos 一条卡其裤）。复数形；a pair of chinos。比 jeans 更整洁，比 dress slacks 更休闲。
[例] ① Chinos are a safe choice between jeans and a full suit. 卡其裤介于牛仔裤与全套西装之间的稳妥选择。 ② He paired navy chinos with a white polo shirt. 他海军蓝卡其裤配白色 polo 衫。
[搭] a pair of chinos 一条卡其裤, khaki chinos 卡其裤, navy chinos 海军蓝卡其裤, slim-fit chinos 修身卡其裤, cotton chinos 棉质卡其裤
[联] slacks 西裤, pants 裤子, polo shirt polo 衫, casual 休闲的, blazer 西装外套, loafers 乐福鞋, smart casual 便正装
[记] 复数；a pair of chinos。名字源自中文「中国」产斜纹棉，属词源趣闻。

**jumpsuit**
英/美 /ˈdʒʌmpsuːt/
n. 连体裤；连身衣
[义] A one-piece garment combining a top and trousers, worn by women for fashion or by workers for practicality. 上装与长裤连成一体的单件服装，女性时尚或工装均用（denim jumpsuit 牛仔连体裤，silk jumpsuit 丝绸连身衣）。可数。
[例] ① She wore a black jumpsuit to the gallery opening. 画廊开幕她穿黑色连体裤。 ② The mechanic's jumpsuit was covered in oil stains. 技师的连体工装沾满油渍。
[搭] denim jumpsuit 牛仔连体裤, silk jumpsuit 丝绸连身衣, wide-leg jumpsuit 阔腿连体裤, belted jumpsuit 系带连体裤, wear a jumpsuit 穿连体裤
[联] romper 连身短裤, outfit 一套搭配, dress 连衣裙, trousers 长裤, belt 腰带, casual 休闲的, elegant 优雅的
[记] 可数；a jumpsuit。jump + suit 原指飞行员/跳伞连体服，后进入日常与时尚。

**overalls**
英/美 /ˈəʊvərɔːlz/
n. 工装连体裤；背带裤
[义] A loose piece of clothing with a top part and trousers in one, held up by straps over the shoulders. 上裤连体、肩带吊挂的宽松服装（denim overalls 牛仔背带裤，work overalls 工装连体裤）。复数形；a pair of overalls。英式 dungarees 常指牛仔背带裤。
[例] ① The painter put on overalls before starting on the walls. 刷墙前他套上工装连体裤。 ② Denim overalls are back as a casual fashion item. 牛仔背带裤又流行作休闲单品。
[搭] a pair of overalls 一条背带裤/工装裤, denim overalls 牛仔背带裤, work overalls 工装连体裤, painter's overalls 油漆工连体裤, strap overalls 肩带背带裤
[联] jumpsuit 连体裤, jeans 牛仔裤, apron 围裙, work 工作, strap 肩带, casual 休闲的, denim 丹宁
[记] 复数；a pair of overalls。overall 形容词「总体的」与 overalls 名词不同形义。

**romper**
英/美 /ˈrɒmpə(r)/
n. 连身短裤；连体短装
[义] A one-piece garment combining a top and short trousers, worn by babies, children, or women in summer. 上装与短裤连成一体的单件服装，婴儿、儿童或女性夏装（baby romper 婴儿连身衣，floral romper 碎花连体短裤）。可数。比 jumpsuit 短，更偏夏季与幼童。
[例] ① The baby wore a cotton romper in the park. 宝宝在公园穿棉质连身短裤。 ② She packed a lightweight romper for the beach trip. 海滩行她带了一件轻便连体短裤。
[搭] baby romper 婴儿连身衣, floral romper 碎花连体短裤, cotton romper 棉质连身短裤, summer romper 夏季连体短裤, wear a romper 穿连身短裤
[联] jumpsuit 连体裤, shorts 短裤, summer 夏天, beach 海滩, baby 婴儿, casual 休闲的, dress 连衣裙
[记] 可数；a romper。romp 动词「嬉戏」与 romper 同族，指便于活动的短连身装。

**dress code**
英/美 /dres kəʊd/
n. 着装规定
[义] A set of rules about what clothes people should wear in a particular place or situation. 特定场所或场合的着装规则（business dress code 商务着装规定，casual dress code 休闲着装，black-tie dress code 黑领结正装要求）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① The restaurant has a smart casual dress code—no flip-flops. 餐厅要求 smart casual——不能穿人字拖。 ② Check the dress code before you pack for the conference. 参会打包前先查着装规定。
[搭] business dress code 商务着装规定, casual dress code 休闲着装, dress code policy 着装政策, violate the dress code 违反着装规定, strict dress code 严格着装要求
[联] formal 正式的, casual 休闲的, attire 着装, suit 套装, appropriate 得体的, office 办公室, wedding 婚礼
[记] 可数/不可数。smart casual 指整洁但不必西装；black tie 指礼服级别。

**undress**
英/美 /ʌnˈdres/
v. 脱衣
[义] To remove one's clothes, or to remove clothes from someone else. 脱掉自己或他人的衣服（undress for bed 睡前脱衣，get undressed 脱光衣服）。与 dress 相对；比 take off 更指「脱到只剩内衣或全裸」的过程。
[形] undressed / undressed / undressing
[例] ① She undressed and hung her clothes in the closet. 她脱下衣服挂进衣柜。 ② The child learned to undress himself before bath time. 孩子学会洗澡前自己脱衣服。
[搭] undress for bed 睡前脱衣, get undressed 脱衣服, partially undressed 半裸, undress quickly 快速脱衣, help sb. undress 帮某人脱衣
[联] dress 穿衣, take off 脱下, put on 穿上, pajamas 睡衣, closet 衣柜, bathroom 浴室, change 更换
[记] 规则动词。undressed 形容词表「裸体的、未穿衣的」；dressing room 可指「更衣室」。

**wash**
英/美 /wɒʃ/ 美 /wɑːʃ/
v. 洗涤
n. 洗涤；待洗衣物
[义] To clean clothes with water and detergent. 用水和洗涤剂清洗衣物（wash your clothes 洗衣服，machine wash 机洗，hand wash 手洗）。名词 a wash 指一次洗涤或待洗的一堆。
[形] washed / washed / washing
[例] ① Wash dark colours separately to avoid fading. 深色单独洗以防褪色。 ② This sweater needs a gentle wash in cold water. 这件毛衣需冷水轻柔洗涤。
[搭] wash clothes 洗衣服, machine wash 机洗, hand wash 手洗, cold wash 冷水洗, wash separately 分开洗
[联] laundry 洗衣, detergent 洗涤剂, bleach 漂白, shrink 缩水, fade 褪色, dry clean 干洗, stain 污渍
[记] 规则动词。care label 常见 Machine wash cold；wash 亦指「冲洗、冲走」，需看语境。

**flannel**
英/美 /ˈflænl/
n. 法兰绒
[义] A soft woven wool or cotton cloth, used for shirts, pyjamas, and sheets. 柔软机织羊毛或棉布，用于衬衫、睡衣、床单（flannel shirt 法兰绒衬衫， brushed flannel 磨毛法兰绒）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① A flannel shirt keeps you warm on cool autumn days. 法兰绒衬衫在秋日凉爽时保暖。 ② He wore plaid flannel pyjamas in winter. 冬天他穿格子法兰绒睡衣。
[搭] flannel shirt 法兰绒衬衫, plaid flannel 格子法兰绒, brushed flannel 磨毛法兰绒, cotton flannel 棉法兰绒, soft flannel 软法兰绒
[联] plaid 格子, cotton 棉, wool 羊毛, shirt 衬衫, pajamas 睡衣, warm 保暖的, fabric 布料
[记] 不可数/可数。英式 flannel 亦指「洗脸毛巾」，与美式 washcloth 近义，需看语境。

**tartan**
英/美 /ˈtɑːtən/
n. 苏格兰格纹；格子呢
[义] A pattern of coloured checks and lines crossing each other, associated with Scottish clans and kilts. 彩色横竖格纹，与苏格兰氏族与 kilt 相关（tartan scarf 苏格兰格围巾，tartan pattern 苏格兰格图案）。不可数/可数。比 plaid 更强调苏格兰传统；美式 plaid 用法更宽。
[例] ① He wore a tartan scarf with his winter coat. 他冬大衣配苏格兰格围巾。 ② Each clan traditionally had its own tartan colours. 每个氏族传统上有自己的 tartan 配色。
[搭] tartan scarf 苏格兰格围巾, tartan pattern 苏格兰格图案, tartan kilt 苏格兰格 kilt, clan tartan 氏族格纹, red tartan 红格
[联] plaid 格子, kilt 苏格兰裙, Scotland 苏格兰, scarf 围巾, pattern 图案, wool 羊毛, traditional 传统的
[记] 不可数/可数。tartan 与 plaid 在美式常混用；严格说 tartan 常指苏格兰 registered 图案。

**check**
英/美 /tʃek/
n. 格子图案；支票
v. 检查
[义] A pattern of squares, usually in two colours; checked cloth. 通常两色的方格图案；格子布（check shirt 格子衬衫，gingham check 方格布）。可数。动词 check 表检查；名词 check 亦指支票（美式），与图案义同形。
[例] ① He wore a blue-and-white check shirt to the picnic. 野餐他穿蓝白格子衬衫。 ② Small checks look quieter than bold plaid. 小格比大 plaid 更低调。
[搭] check shirt 格子衬衫, gingham check 方格布, checked pattern 格子图案, bold check 大格, subtle check 细格
[联] plaid 格子, stripe 条纹, pattern 图案, shirt 衬衫, gingham 方格布, square 方块, colour 颜色
[记] 可数；a check。checked 形容词（a checked shirt 格子衬衫）；check 亦指「支票、核对」。

**floral**
英/美 /ˈflɔːrəl/
adj. 花卉图案的
[义] Decorated with a pattern of flowers. 饰有花卉图案（floral dress 碎花连衣裙，floral print 印花）。形容词。名词 floral 指「花饰、鲜花布置」，与服装图案义相关。
[例] ① She chose a floral dress for the garden party. 花园派对她选了碎花连衣裙。 ② Floral prints are popular in spring collections. 花卉印花在春季系列中很常见。
[搭] floral dress 碎花连衣裙, floral print 花卉印花, floral pattern 花卉图案, bold floral 大朵印花, subtle floral 小碎花
[联] pattern 图案, dress 连衣裙, spring 春天, print 印花, elegant 优雅的, vintage 复古的, colour 颜色
[记] 形容词。floral 亦指「与花有关的」（floral arrangement 插花），服装语境多指印花。

**tight**
英/美 /taɪt/
adj. 紧身的；紧的
[义] Fitting closely to the body; not loose. 贴身、不宽松（tight jeans 紧身牛仔裤，too tight 太紧，tight fit 紧身）。形容词。比 slim fit 更强调「勒身」；过紧会不舒服。
[例] ① These shoes are too tight—I need a larger size. 这双鞋太紧——要大一号。 ② The dress was tight around the waist but fine elsewhere. 裙子腰紧，别处还好。
[搭] tight jeans 紧身牛仔裤, too tight 太紧, tight fit 紧身, feel tight 觉得紧, skin-tight 紧身的
[联] loose 宽松的, fit 合身, size 尺码, stretch 弹力, leggings 打底裤, comfortable 舒适的, alter 修改
[记] 形容词。tight 亦指「严格的、没钱的」（money is tight），需看语境。

**loose**
英/美 /luːs/
adj. 宽松的
[义] Not fitting tightly; with extra room in the garment. 不贴身、有余量（loose fit 宽松版型， loose trousers 宽松长裤）。形容词。与 tight 相对；loose 亦指「松散的、未固定的」，需看语境。
[例] ① She prefers loose clothing in hot weather. 天热她更爱宽松衣服。 ② The shirt hangs loose because he lost weight. 他瘦了，衬衫显得松垮。
[搭] loose fit 宽松版型, loose trousers 宽松长裤, hang loose 松垮, loose clothing 宽松衣物, loose around the waist 腰部宽松
[联] tight 紧身的, fit 合身, comfortable 舒适的, casual 休闲的, baggy 肥大的, size 尺码, alter 修改
[记] 形容词。loose 亦指「松开的、零散的」（a loose button 快掉的扣子），与版型义相关。

**sleeveless**
英/美 /ˈsliːvləs/
adj. 无袖的
[义] Without sleeves. 没有袖子的（sleeveless dress 无袖连衣裙，sleeveless top 无袖上衣）。形容词。与 tank top 近义，sleeveless 作定语更正式。
[例] ① She wore a sleeveless blouse under her blazer. 她西装外套里穿无袖女衫。 ② The dress code bans sleeveless shirts in the main office. 主办公区着装规定禁止无袖衬衫。
[搭] sleeveless dress 无袖连衣裙, sleeveless top 无袖上衣, sleeveless blouse 无袖女衫, sleeveless jacket 无袖夹克, sleeveless design 无袖设计
[联] sleeve 袖子, tank top 背心, dress 连衣裙, blouse 女式衬衫, summer 夏天, formal 正式的, layer 分层穿着
[记] 形容词。sleeve + -less；cap-sleeve 指「盖肩短袖」，与完全无袖不同。

**turtleneck**
英/美 /ˈtɜːrtlnek/
n. 高领；高领毛衣
[义] A high, close-fitting collar that folds over; a sweater with such a collar. 高而贴颈、可翻折的领子；此类领子的毛衣（black turtleneck 黑色高领衫，turtleneck sweater 高领毛衣）。可数。英式 polo neck 同义。
[例] ① A black turtleneck looks sharp under a blazer. 黑色高领衫配西装外套很利落。 ② She rolled the turtleneck down when she got warm. 热了她把高领翻下来。
[搭] black turtleneck 黑色高领衫, turtleneck sweater 高领毛衣, roll-neck 翻领高领, slim turtleneck 修身高领, cotton turtleneck 棉质高领
[联] sweater 毛衣, collar 衣领, neckline 领口, warm 保暖的, layer 分层穿着, elegant 优雅的, winter 冬天
[记] 可数；a turtleneck。英式 polo neck；turtle 与「龟」同形，因领部折叠形似龟壳得名。

**shoelace**
英/美 /ˈʃuːleɪs/
n. 鞋带
[义] A long thin cord used to fasten a shoe by threading it through holes or eyelets. 穿入鞋孔用以系鞋的细长绳带（tie your shoelaces 系鞋带， untied shoelace 松开的鞋带）。可数；常用复数 shoelaces。
[例] ① One shoelace broke during the hike. 徒步中一根鞋带断了。 ② Double-knot your shoelaces so they don't come undone. 鞋带打双结以免松开。
[搭] tie shoelaces 系鞋带, untied shoelace 松开的鞋带, shoelace hole 鞋眼, replace shoelaces 换鞋带, colourful shoelaces 彩色鞋带
[联] shoe 鞋, sneaker 运动鞋, boot 靴子, lace 系带, tie 系, knot 结, lace-up 系带的
[记] 可数；常用复数 shoelaces。shoe + lace 复合；lace up your shoes 系鞋。

**suspenders**
英/美 /səˈspendəz/
n. 吊裤带（美式）；吊袜带
[义] 美式：straps worn over the shoulders to hold up trousers（英式 braces）；亦指女性吊袜带。复数形。英式 braces 指吊裤带；美式 braces 指牙箍或支架，易混淆。
[例] ① He wore suspenders with his dress trousers for a vintage look. 他为复古风正装裤配了吊裤带。 ② In the UK, ask for braces—not suspenders—if you mean trouser straps. 在英国，吊裤带要说 braces，不是 suspenders。
[搭] wear suspenders 穿吊裤带, leather suspenders 皮吊裤带, clip-on suspenders 夹式吊裤带, dress suspenders 正装吊裤带, trouser suspenders 吊裤带
[联] belt 腰带, trousers 长裤, formal 正式的, vintage 复古的, strap 带条, waist 腰, suit 套装
[记] 复数形。美式 suspenders = 英式 braces（吊裤带）；美式 suspenders 亦指吊袜带。

**brooch**
英/美 /brəʊtʃ/ 美 /broʊtʃ/
n. 胸针；别针
[义] A piece of jewellery with a pin at the back, worn fastened to clothing on the chest. 背面有针、别在胸前衣物上的首饰（gold brooch 金胸针，vintage brooch 复古胸针）。可数。
[例] ① She pinned a pearl brooch to her coat lapel. 她把珍珠胸针别在大衣领上。 ② The brooch was a gift from her grandmother. 胸针是祖母送的礼物。
[搭] gold brooch 金胸针, pearl brooch 珍珠胸针, vintage brooch 复古胸针, pin a brooch 别胸针, coat brooch 大衣胸针
[联] pin 别针, jewelry 珠宝, necklace 项链, lapel 翻领, coat 大衣, elegant 优雅的, gift 礼物
[记] 可数；a brooch。brooch 与 broach（提出话题）同音异拼；pin 为动词/广义别针。

**pendant**
英/美 /ˈpendənt/
n. 吊坠；垂饰
[义] A piece of jewellery hung from a chain around the neck. 挂在颈链上的垂饰（gold pendant 金吊坠，heart pendant 心形吊坠）。可数。与 necklace 区别：pendant 指坠体，necklace 指整条链饰。
[例] ① The necklace had a small silver pendant shaped like a star. 项链坠着一颗星形小银吊坠。 ② She chose a simple pendant instead of a busy necklace. 她选了简洁吊坠而非复杂项链。
[搭] gold pendant 金吊坠, heart pendant 心形吊坠, silver pendant 银吊坠, pendant necklace 吊坠项链, wear a pendant 戴吊坠
[联] necklace 项链, chain 链, bracelet 手镯, jewelry 珠宝, charm 小吊坠, gift 礼物, elegant 优雅的
[记] 可数；a pendant。pendant 亦指「悬挂物」（light pendant 吊灯），需看语境。

**bangle**
英/美 /ˈbæŋɡl/
n. 手镯；刚性镯
[义] A rigid bracelet that slips over the hand without a clasp. 无扣、直接套入手腕的硬镯（gold bangles 金手镯，glass bangles 玻璃镯）。可数。与 chain bracelet 相对，bangle 不可弯折开合。
[例] ① She wore several thin gold bangles on one wrist. 她一只腕上戴几只细金镯。 ② Glass bangles clinked when she moved her arm. 她动手臂时玻璃镯叮当作响。
[搭] gold bangles 金手镯, glass bangles 玻璃镯, thin bangle 细镯, stack bangles 叠戴手镯, rigid bangle 硬镯
[联] bracelet 手镯, wrist 手腕, jewelry 珠宝, gold 金, ring 戒指, elegant 优雅的, gift 礼物
[记] 可数；a bangle。bangle 在南亚婚礼文化中常见；与 flexible bracelet 材质感不同。

**backpack**
英/美 /ˈbækpæk/
n. 双肩背包
v. 背包旅行
[义] A bag carried on the back with two straps over the shoulders. 双背带背在背上的包（school backpack 书包，leather backpack 皮背包）。可数。动词 backpack 指背包旅行。
[例] ① He slung his backpack over one shoulder on the way to class. 去上课他把书包甩到一侧肩上。 ② Leave bulky backpacks in the locker—they block the aisle. 大背包放储物柜——会挡过道。
[搭] school backpack 书包, leather backpack 皮背包, backpack strap 背包带, carry a backpack 背背包, lightweight backpack 轻量背包

**tote bag**
英/美 /təʊt bæɡ/
n. 托特包；大手袋
[义] A large bag with two handles, open at the top, used for shopping or everyday carry. 双提手、顶部敞开的大袋，购物或日常携带（canvas tote 帆布托特， reusable tote 环保袋）。可数。
[例] ① She carried groceries home in a canvas tote bag. 她用帆布托特袋把杂货带回家。 ② Conference swag often comes in a branded tote bag. 会议伴手礼常装在品牌托特袋里。
[搭] canvas tote bag 帆布托特包, reusable tote bag 环保托特袋, branded tote 品牌托特袋, carry a tote 提托特包, large tote 大托特
[联] handbag 手提包, purse 女包, shopping 购物, canvas 帆布, strap 提手, casual 休闲的, eco-friendly 环保的

**bandana**
英/美 /bænˈdænə/
n. 头巾；方巾
[义] A square piece of coloured cloth worn on the head or around the neck. 系在头上或颈间的彩色方巾（red bandana 红头巾， wear a bandana 包头巾）。可数。亦作 bandanna。
[例] ① He tied a bandana around his forehead to keep sweat out of his eyes. 他在额上系头巾挡汗。 ② Bandanas work as neck scarves in a pinch. 应急时头巾也可当颈巾。

[联] scarf 围巾, head 头, hat 帽子, cap 便帽, casual 休闲的, summer 夏天, sweat 汗

**headband**
英/美 /ˈhedbænd/
n. 发带；头带
[义] A narrow band worn around the head to hold hair back or as decoration. 绕头佩戴以束发或装饰的窄带（elastic headband 弹性发带， sports headband 运动头带）。可数。
[例] ① She pushed her hair back with a simple black headband. 她用黑色发带把头发往后拢。 ② The athlete wore a sweat-wicking headband during the match. 运动员比赛时戴吸汗头带。

**velvet**
英/美 /ˈvelvɪt/
n. 天鹅绒
adj. 天鹅绒的
[义] A soft thick fabric with a short dense pile on one side, used for evening wear and winter clothes. 一侧有短密绒毛的柔软厚织物，用于晚装与冬装（velvet dress 丝绒连衣裙，black velvet 黑丝绒）。不可数/形容词。
[例] ① The theatre curtains were made of deep red velvet. 剧院幕布是深红丝绒的。 ② She wore a velvet blazer for the holiday party. 假日派对她穿丝绒西装外套。

**satin**
英/美 /ˈsætɪn/
n. 缎；缎面
adj. 缎面的
[义] A smooth shiny fabric, usually silk or polyester, used for formal dresses and linings. 光滑亮泽的织物，通常丝或涤纶，用于礼服与衬里（satin dress 缎面连衣裙，satin lining 缎衬）。不可数/形容词。
[例] ① The bridesmaid dresses were pale blue satin. 伴娘裙是淡蓝缎面的。 ② Satin catches the light—great for evening wear. 缎面反光——适合晚装。
[搭] satin dress 缎面连衣裙, satin lining 缎衬, silk satin 丝绸缎, satin finish 缎面光泽, smooth satin 光滑缎面
[联] silk 丝绸, velvet 天鹅绒, gown 礼服, elegant 优雅的, formal 正式的, shiny 闪亮的, fabric 布料
[记] 不可数/形容词。sateen 指「棉缎」；satin 与 silk 常搭配但并非同义。

**corduroy**
英/美 /ˈkɔːdərɔɪ/
n. 灯芯绒
[义] A strong cotton cloth with raised parallel lines (ribs), used for trousers and jackets. 有凸起平行条纹（ rib）的结实棉布，用于长裤与夹克（corduroy trousers 灯芯绒裤，wide-wale corduroy 粗条灯芯绒）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Corduroy trousers are warm and hard-wearing in autumn. 灯芯绒裤秋天保暖耐穿。 ② The jacket was soft brown corduroy. 夹克是软棕色灯芯绒的。
[搭] corduroy trousers 灯芯绒裤, wide-wale corduroy 粗条灯芯绒, corduroy jacket 灯芯绒夹克, brown corduroy 棕色灯芯绒, ribbed corduroy 罗纹灯芯绒
[联] cotton 棉, trousers 长裤, jacket 夹克, fabric 布料, warm 保暖的, rib 条纹, casual 休闲的
[记] 不可数/可数。wale 指灯芯绒「条距」；fine-wale 细条更正式。

**mesh**
英/美 /meʃ/
n. 网眼织物；网格
[义] Material made of a network of wire or thread with small spaces between, used in sportswear and shoes for breathability. 线或丝织成、留小孔的网络状材料，用于运动装与鞋面透气（mesh panel 网眼拼接，mesh sneakers 网面运动鞋）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The running shoes have mesh panels for ventilation. 跑鞋有网眼拼接透气。 ② Mesh lining helps the jacket dry faster after rain. 网眼内衬帮夹克雨后更快干。
[搭] mesh panel 网眼拼接, mesh fabric 网眼织物, mesh sneakers 网面运动鞋, breathable mesh 透气网眼, mesh lining 网眼内衬
[联] fabric 布料, sneaker 运动鞋, breathable 透气的, sportswear 运动装, nylon 尼龙, lightweight 轻量的, ventilate 通风
[记] 不可数/可数。mesh 亦指「网、网格」（wire mesh），与服装义同形。

**spandex**
英/美 /ˈspændeks/
n. 氨纶；弹性纤维
[义] A synthetic fibre known for exceptional elasticity, used in stretch clothing and sportswear. 以极高弹性著称的合成纤维，用于弹力衣与运动装（spandex blend 氨纶混纺，contains spandex 含氨纶）。不可数。英式常标 lycra（商标名，通用指同类纤维）。
[例] ① These leggings contain spandex for stretch and recovery. 这条打底裤含氨纶，有弹性且回弹。 ② A little spandex in denim adds comfort. 牛仔加一点氨纶更舒适。
[搭] spandex blend 氨纶混纺, contains spandex 含氨纶, spandex fibre 氨纶纤维, stretch spandex 弹力氨纶, lycra blend Lycra 混纺
[联] stretch 弹力, leggings 打底裤, polyester 涤纶, elastic 弹性的, fit 合身, sportswear 运动装, fabric 布料
[记] 不可数。Lycra 为杜邦商标，欧美标签常见；spandex 为通用化学名。

**knitwear**
英/美 /ˈnɪtweə(r)/
n. 针织品（总称）
[义] Clothes made by knitting, such as sweaters, cardigans, and scarves. 针织制成的衣物总称，如毛衣、开衫、围巾。不可数。比 sweater 更偏品类/零售用语（knitwear department 针织部）。
[例] ① The store's knitwear sale starts next week. 店里针织品下周开始促销。 ② Pack light knitwear for layering in spring. 春天带轻薄针织品方便叠穿。
[搭] knitwear department 针织部, light knitwear 轻薄针织, wool knitwear 羊毛针织, knitwear collection 针织系列, hand-knitwear 手织针织
[联] sweater 毛衣, cardigan 开衫, knit 编织, wool 羊毛, scarf 围巾, layer 分层穿着, winter 冬天
[记] 不可数。knit + wear 复合；knitwear 零售与行业常用，口语多说 sweaters。

**loungewear**
英/美 /ˈlaʊndʒweə(r)/
n. 居家服（总称）
[义] Comfortable clothes worn at home for relaxing, such as pyjamas, sweatpants, and soft robes. 在家放松时穿的舒适衣物总称，如睡衣、运动裤、软浴袍。不可数。疫情后零售与时尚常用 category。
[例] ① Sales of loungewear surged when more people worked from home. 居家办公增多时居家服销量激增。 ② Loungewear should feel soft but still look presentable on video calls. 居家服要软，视频会议还得看得过去。
[搭] comfortable loungewear 舒适居家服, loungewear set 居家套装, soft loungewear 柔软居家服, loungewear brand 居家服品牌, lounge in loungewear 穿居家服放松
[联] pajamas 睡衣, sweatpants 运动裤, robe 浴袍, casual 休闲的, comfortable 舒适的, home 家, hoodie 连帽衫
[记] 不可数。lounge + wear 复合；与 sleepwear 近义，loungewear 更强调「醒着在家穿」。

**model**
英/美 /ˈmɒdl/ 美 /ˈmɑːdl/
n. 模特；模型
v. 当模特；展示
[义] A person whose job is to wear clothes for photographs, fashion shows, or advertising. 为拍照、时装秀或广告展示服装的人（fashion model 时装模特， runway model 走秀模特）。可数。动词 model 指当模特或展示（model the new collection 展示新系列）。
[例] ① She worked as a fashion model in her twenties. 她二十多岁做过时装模特。 ② The dress was modeled by a well-known face at the show. 这条裙子由知名模特在秀上展示。
[搭] fashion model 时装模特, runway model 走秀模特, model the collection 展示系列, aspiring model 准模特, fit model 试衣模特
[联] designer 设计师, fashion 时尚, runway T 台, photographer 摄影师, elegant 优雅的, brand 品牌, trend 趋势
[记] 可数；a model。model 亦指「模型、型号」（role model 榜样），需看语境。

**mannequin**
英/美 /ˈmænɪkɪn/
n. 人体模特；假人
[义] A life-sized dummy used to display clothes in shop windows. 橱窗展示服装的等身假人（shop mannequin 橱窗模特， mannequin display 假人陈列）。可数。英式亦拼 manikin；与 fashion model（真人）不同。
[例] ① The mannequin in the window wore the new winter coat. 橱窗假人穿着新款冬大衣。 ② Staff dressed the mannequins for the seasonal display. 员工为 seasonal 陈列给假人穿衣。
[搭] shop mannequin 橱窗模特, window mannequin 橱窗假人, dress a mannequin 给假人穿衣, mannequin display 假人陈列, headless mannequin 无头假人
[联] shop 商店, display 陈列, fashion 时尚, retail 零售, boutique 精品店, clothes 衣服, window 橱窗
[记] 可数；a mannequin。来自荷兰语「小男人」；勿与 manikin（医学训练假人）完全等同。

**boutique**
英/美 /buːˈtiːk/
n. 精品店；小型时装店
[义] A small shop selling fashionable clothes, accessories, or luxury items. 售卖时尚服装、配饰或奢侈品的小型店铺（fashion boutique 时装精品店， boutique hotel 精品酒店为延伸义）。可数。
[例] ① She opened a boutique selling handmade jewellery and scarves. 她开了家卖手工首饰与围巾的精品店。 ② The dress came from a local boutique, not a chain store. 这条裙子来自本地精品店，不是连锁店。
[搭] fashion boutique 时装精品店, local boutique 本地精品店, boutique shopping 精品店购物, independent boutique 独立精品店, boutique owner 精品店老板
[联] shop 商店, retail 零售, designer 设计师, fashion 时尚, brand 品牌, elegant 优雅的, accessory 配饰
[记] 可数；a boutique。boutique 形容词表「小批量、精品式」（boutique brand 小众品牌）。

**label**
英/美 /ˈleɪbl/
n. 标签；品牌
v. 贴标签
[义] A piece of cloth or paper sewn or stuck on clothing giving care instructions, size, or brand. 缝或贴在衣物上的布/纸标，含洗护说明、尺码或品牌（care label 洗护标， designer label 设计师品牌标）。可数。动词 label 表贴标签。
[例] ① Always read the care label before washing wool. 洗羊毛前务必看洗护标。 ② The label says dry clean only. 标签写着只能干洗。
[搭] care label 洗护标, designer label 设计师品牌, clothing label 服装标签, read the label 看标签, size label 尺码标
[联] brand 品牌, wash 洗, dry clean 干洗, shrink 缩水, size 尺码, fabric 布料, designer 设计师
[记] 可数；a label。label 亦指「唱片公司、刻板印象」，需看语境。

**second-hand**
英/美 /ˌsekənd ˈhænd/
adj. 二手的
adv. 间接地
[义] Not new; previously owned by someone else. 非全新、经他人使用过的（second-hand clothes 二手衣服， second-hand shop 旧货店）。形容词/副词。名词 the second-hand 指二手市场。
[例] ① She finds unique jackets at second-hand shops. 她在旧货店淘到独特夹克。 ② Buying second-hand reduces waste and saves money. 买二手减少浪费也省钱。
[搭] second-hand clothes 二手衣服, second-hand shop 旧货店, buy second-hand 买二手, second-hand market 二手市场, gently used second-hand 九成新二手
[联] vintage 复古的, thrift store 古着店, used 用过的, affordable 负担得起的, sustainable 可持续的, clothes 衣服, donate 捐赠
[记] 形容词。second-hand 与 secondhand 拼写均可；hand-me-down 指「兄姐传下来的」。

**trim**
英/美 /trɪm/
n. 饰边；装饰
v. 修剪
[义] Decorative material such as lace or ribbon sewn along the edge of a garment. 缝在衣物边缘的装饰材料，如蕾丝或丝带（lace trim 蕾丝边， gold trim 金饰边）。不可数/可数。动词 trim 表修剪（trim the hem 修边），与名词相关。
[例] ① The coat has fur trim around the hood. 大衣兜帽一圈毛皮饰边。 ② Delicate lace trim makes the blouse look expensive. 精致蕾丝边让女衫显贵。
[搭] lace trim 蕾丝边, fur trim 毛皮饰边, gold trim 金饰边, decorative trim 装饰边, trim detail 饰边细节
[联] lace 蕾丝, fur 毛皮, hem 衣边, collar 衣领, elegant 优雅的, dress 连衣裙, blouse 女式衬衫
[记] 不可数/可数。trim 亦指「修剪、削减」（trim the budget），需看语境。

**embroidery**
英/美 /ɪmˈbrɔɪdəri/
n. 刺绣
[义] Decorative patterns sewn onto cloth with coloured thread. 用彩色线在布上绣出的装饰图案（hand embroidery 手工刺绣， floral embroidery 花卉刺绣）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① The collar had fine embroidery in gold thread. 衣领有金线细绣。 ② Traditional embroidery takes hours of careful work. 传统刺绣需数小时细致劳作。
[搭] hand embroidery 手工刺绣, floral embroidery 花卉刺绣, gold embroidery 金线刺绣, embroidery detail 刺绣细节, embroidered collar 刺绣领
[联] thread 线, stitch 针脚, sew 缝, lace 蕾丝, pattern 图案, elegant 优雅的, traditional 传统的
[记] 不可数/可数。embroidered 形容词（embroidered shirt 刺绣衬衫）；embroidery 亦喻「渲染、润色」。

**tumble dry**
英/美 /ˈtʌmbl draɪ/
v. 滚筒烘干
[义] To dry clothes in a machine that rotates and blows hot air. 在旋转并吹热风的机器中烘干衣物（do not tumble dry 不可烘干， tumble dry low 低温烘干）。动词短语；标签常见 Do not tumble dry。
[形] tumble-dried / tumble-dried / tumble-drying
[例] ① The label says tumble dry low—high heat may shrink wool. 标签写低温烘干——高温可能使羊毛缩水。 ② She tumble-dried the towels after washing them. 毛巾洗完她放进烘干机烘干了。
[搭] do not tumble dry 不可烘干, tumble dry low 低温烘干, tumble dry gentle 轻柔烘干, tumble dryer 烘干机, avoid tumble drying 避免烘干
[联] wash 洗, shrink 缩水, laundry 洗衣, dry 干燥, heat 热, wool 羊毛, care label 洗护标
[记] 短语动词。tumble dryer（英式）/ clothes dryer（美式）指烘干机。

**hand wash**
英/美 /hænd wɒʃ/ 美 /hænd wɑːʃ/
v. 手洗
n. 手洗
[义] To wash clothes by hand in a sink or basin, not in a machine. 在水槽或盆中用手洗涤，非机洗（hand wash only 只能手洗， hand wash cold 冷水手洗）。标签常见 Hand wash only。
[例] ① This silk blouse is hand wash only—don't put it in the machine. 这件丝绸女衫只能手洗——别机洗。 ② She hand-washed the sweater in lukewarm water. 她用温手水洗了毛衣。
[搭] hand wash only 只能手洗, hand wash cold 冷水手洗, hand wash gently 轻柔手洗, hand wash cycle 轻柔手洗程序（洗衣机）, hand wash silk 手洗丝绸
[联] wash 洗, laundry 洗衣, delicate 娇贵的, silk 丝绸, wool 羊毛, label 标签, shrink 缩水
[记] 短语；hand-wash 作形容词（hand-wash items 需手洗的衣物）。与 hand wash（洗手）同形，语境区分。

**drawstring**
英/美 /ˈdrɔːstrɪŋ/
n. 抽绳
[义] A cord threaded through a channel in the fabric, used to tighten the waist or hood of clothing. 穿在 fabric 通道里用以收紧腰或兜帽的绳（elastic drawstring 弹性抽绳， drawstring hood 抽绳兜帽）。可数。
[例] ① Tighten the drawstring on your sweatpants before running. 跑步前收紧运动裤抽绳。 ② The hood has a drawstring to block the wind. 兜帽有抽绳挡风。
[搭] elastic drawstring 弹性抽绳, drawstring hood 抽绳兜帽, drawstring waist 抽绳腰, tighten the drawstring 收紧抽绳, loose drawstring 松抽绳
[联] hood 兜帽, sweatpants 运动裤, waist 腰, elastic 弹性的, tighten 收紧, casual 休闲的, sport 运动
[记] 可数；a drawstring。draw + string 复合；抽绳裤 waist 可调节。

**elastic**
英/美 /ɪˈlæstɪk/
n. 松紧带；橡皮筋
adj. 有弹性的
[义] A material or band that returns to its original shape after stretching; used in waistbands and cuffs. 拉伸后回弹的材料或带条，用于腰头、袖口（elastic waist 松紧腰， elastic band 橡皮筋）。不可数/可数。形容词表有弹性。
[例] ① The skirt has an elastic waist for comfort. 这条裙子松紧腰穿着舒服。 ② Replace the elastic if the waistband feels loose. 腰头松了就换松紧带。
[搭] elastic waist 松紧腰, elastic band 橡皮筋, elastic cuff 弹性袖口, stretchy elastic 弹力松紧, replace the elastic 更换松紧带
[联] waist 腰, stretch 弹力, spandex 氨纶, fit 合身, comfortable 舒适的, sweatpants 运动裤, drawstring 抽绳
[记] 不可数/可数。elastic 亦指「灵活的」（elastic rules 灵活规则），需看语境。

**runway**
英/美 /ˈrʌnweɪ/
n. T 台；跑道
[义] The long platform on which models walk in a fashion show. 时装秀上模特走秀的狭长平台（walk the runway 走 T 台，runway show T 台秀）。可数。亦指飞机跑道，语境不同。
[例] ① Models walked the runway in the designer's latest collection. 模特穿着设计师最新系列走 T 台。 ② The finale dress closed the runway show. 压轴礼服为 T 台秀收尾。
[搭] walk the runway 走 T 台, runway show T 台秀, runway model 走秀模特, runway collection T 台系列, fashion runway 时尚 T 台
[联] model 模特, designer 设计师, fashion 时尚, fashion show 时装秀, collection 系列, elegant 优雅的, trend 趋势
[记] 可数；a runway。run + way 原义「跑的路」；航空与时尚同形异域。

**zipper**
英/美 /ˈzɪpə(r)/
n. 拉链（美式）
[义] A device with two rows of teeth that lock together, used to fasten clothes and bags. 两排齿互锁的扣合装置，用于衣物与包（zipper pull 拉链头， broken zipper 坏拉链）。可数。英式更常说 zip；美式 zipper 更口语。
[例] ① The zipper on my backpack stuck halfway. 背包拉链卡在一半。 ② Replace the zipper if it keeps splitting. 老开就换拉链。
[搭] broken zipper 坏拉链, zipper pull 拉链头, front zipper 前拉链, zip up the zipper 拉上拉链, replace the zipper 换拉链
[联] zip 拉链, button 纽扣, jacket 夹克, hoodie 连帽衫, fasten 扣紧, stuck 卡住的, pocket 口袋
[记] 可数；a zipper。zip 英式名词/动词；zipper 美式名词更常见。

**kilt**
英/美 /kɪlt/
n. 苏格兰 kilt；褶裙
[义] A knee-length skirt with pleats, traditionally worn by men in Scotland, made of tartan cloth. 及膝褶裙，传统上苏格兰男性穿着， tartan 布制成。可数。文化特异词：正式场合、风笛队、高地游戏常见；现代时尚亦借 kilt 廓形。
[例] ① He wore a formal kilt and sporran to the wedding. 婚礼他穿正式 kilt 配 sporran（前挂皮袋）。 ② The kilt's tartan pattern represents a Scottish clan. kilt 的 tartan 纹样代表苏格兰氏族。
[搭] tartan kilt 苏格兰格 kilt, formal kilt 正式 kilt, wear a kilt 穿 kilt, kilt pin kilt 别针, pleated kilt 褶裥 kilt
[联] tartan 苏格兰格, plaid 格子, Scotland 苏格兰, skirt 裙子, pleated 百褶的, traditional 传统的, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a kilt。kilt 传统为男装；勿与普通 pleated skirt 完全等同。

**anorak**
英/美 /ˈænəræk/
n. 连帽防风夹克（英式）
[义] A warm waterproof jacket with a hood, often knee-length or hip-length. 带帽保暖防水夹克，常为及膝或及臀长度。可数。英式高频；美式更常说 parka 或 windbreaker。源自因纽特语，经北极装备进入日常。
[例] ① Pack an anorak—the coastal wind can be fierce. 带件 anorak——海边风可能很大。 ② His bright yellow anorak made him easy to spot in the crowd. 亮黄色 anorak 让他在人群里很显眼。
[搭] waterproof anorak 防水 anorak, hooded anorak 连帽 anorak, packable anorak 可折叠 anorak, bright anorak 亮色 anorak, wear an anorak 穿 anorak
[联] jacket 夹克, parka 派克大衣, windbreaker 防风夹克, hood 兜帽, waterproof 防水的, raincoat 雨衣, outdoor 户外
[记] 可数；an anorak。英式 anorak 俚语亦指「怪癖极深的人」，与夹克义不同。

**poncho**
英/美 /ˈpɒntʃəʊ/
n. 斗篷；披风
[义] A piece of outer clothing without sleeves, made from a single piece of fabric with a hole for the head. 无袖外披，整块布料中间留头洞（rain poncho 雨披， wool poncho 羊毛斗篷）。可数。拉美与户外语境常见；比 coat 更易穿脱。
[例] ① She threw a rain poncho over her backpack and herself. 她把雨披罩在背包和自己身上。 ② A wool poncho keeps your shoulders warm without restricting your arms. 羊毛斗篷护肩不束缚手臂。
[搭] rain poncho 雨披, wool poncho 羊毛斗篷, waterproof poncho 防水斗篷, throw on a poncho 披上斗篷, colourful poncho 彩色斗篷
[联] coat 大衣, scarf 围巾, rain 雨, waterproof 防水的, layer 分层穿着, outdoor 户外, Latin America 拉美
[记] 可数；a poncho。源自南美西班牙语；一次性塑料 rain poncho 音乐节、徒步常见。

**kimono**
英/美 /kɪˈməʊnəʊ/
n. 和服；和式长袍
[义] A traditional Japanese garment with wide sleeves and a sash; also a loose robe worn as loungewear or fashion. 宽袖、腰带固定的日本传统服装；亦指休闲或时尚用的宽松和式长袍（silk kimono 丝绸和服， kimono robe 和式睡袍）。可数。文化特异：正式和服穿法复杂；时尚 kimono 多为简化款。
[例] ① She wore a silk kimono as a light layer over her dress. 她在连衣裙外搭丝绸和式长袍。 ② Traditional kimono require careful folding and a proper obi belt. 传统和服需仔细折叠并系好 obi 腰带。
[搭] silk kimono 丝绸和服, kimono robe 和式长袍, wear a kimono 穿和服, kimono sleeve 和服宽袖, traditional kimono 传统和服
[联] robe 长袍, silk 丝绸, sash 腰带, Japan 日本, traditional 传统的, elegant 优雅的, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a kimono。复数 kimonos 或 kimono。勿与 bathrobe 混称传统和服。

**clog**
英/美 /klɒɡ/ 美 /klɑːɡ/
n. 木屐；厚底凉拖
[义] A shoe with a thick wooden or platform sole, sometimes open at the heel. 厚木底或平台底鞋，有时后跟敞开（leather clogs 皮面木屐， nursing clogs 护士鞋）。可数。荷兰传统木鞋与时尚厚底 clogs 均用此词。
[例] ① She slipped into her garden clogs by the back door. 她在后门趿上园艺木屐。 ② Platform clogs came back in several summer collections. 厚底 clogs 在多个夏季系列回归。
[搭] leather clogs 皮面木屐, garden clogs 园艺木屐, platform clogs 厚底 clogs, slip into clogs 趿上木屐, wooden clogs 木屐
[联] shoe 鞋, sandal 凉鞋, heel 鞋跟, sole 鞋底, slipper 拖鞋, comfortable 舒适的, garden 园艺
[记] 可数；a clog。clog 动词表「堵塞」，与鞋义同形需辨义。

**mule**
英/美 /mjuːl/
n. 穆勒鞋；后空鞋
[义] A shoe or slipper that is open at the back, so the foot can slip in easily. 后跟敞开、易趿穿入的鞋或拖鞋（leather mules 皮穆勒鞋， heeled mules 高跟后空鞋）。可数。时尚语境高频；与 loafer（包跟）不同。
[例] ① She wore heeled mules to the dinner—they look elegant but are hard to walk in. 晚宴她穿高跟穆勒鞋——好看但难走。 ② Mules are easy to slip on but can come off your heel when you walk fast. 穆勒鞋易穿但走快时后跟易滑出。
[搭] leather mules 皮穆勒鞋, heeled mules 高跟后空鞋, slip-on mules 套脚穆勒, flat mules 平底穆勒, wear mules 穿穆勒鞋
[联] loafer 乐福鞋, slipper 拖鞋, heel 鞋跟, shoe 鞋, elegant 优雅的, slip-on 套脚的, backless 后空的
[记] 可数；a mule。mule 亦指「骡子、走私者」，需看语境。

**stiletto**
英/美 /stɪˈletəʊ/
n. 细高跟；尖高跟
[义] A shoe with a very thin, high heel; the heel itself. 极细高跟的鞋；该种鞋跟（stiletto heels 细高跟， pair of stilettos 一双细高跟）。可数。比 high heels 更强调「针般细跟」；正式与派对语境常见。
[例] ① She switched to flats after three hours in stilettos. 细高跟穿三小时后她换成了平底鞋。 ② Stilettos look sharp but are brutal on cobblestones. 细高跟好看，走石板路却受罪。
[搭] stiletto heels 细高跟, pair of stilettos 一双细高跟, black stilettos 黑色细高跟, wear stilettos 穿细高跟, stiletto heel height 细跟高度
[联] heel 鞋跟, high heels 高跟鞋, shoe 鞋, formal 正式的, elegant 优雅的, uncomfortable 不舒服的, party 派对
[记] 可数；a stiletto。stiletto 原指「短匕首」，因鞋跟形状得名。

**cashmere**
英/美 /ˈkæʃmɪə(r)/
n. 羊绒；开司米
[义] Fine soft wool from cashmere goats, used for expensive sweaters and scarves. 山羊绒制成的细腻软羊毛，用于高档毛衣与围巾（cashmere sweater 羊绒衫， pure cashmere 纯羊绒）。不可数。形容词 cashmere 表羊绒质地。比 wool 更软、更贵、更娇贵。
[例] ① A good cashmere sweater feels light but keeps you warm. 好羊绒衫轻而暖。 ② Store cashmere folded, not on a hanger, to avoid stretching. 羊绒应折叠存放，别挂衣架，以免拉伸。
[搭] cashmere sweater 羊绒衫, pure cashmere 纯羊绒, cashmere scarf 羊绒围巾, soft cashmere 柔软羊绒, cashmere blend 羊绒混纺
[联] wool 羊毛, merino 美利奴羊毛, sweater 毛衣, scarf 围巾, luxury 奢侈, soft 柔软的, delicate 娇贵的
[记] 不可数。名字来自克什米尔（Kashmir）地区；cashmere 需轻柔洗护。

**merino**
英/美 /məˈriːnəʊ/
n. 美利奴羊毛
[义] Very fine soft wool from merino sheep, used for base layers and quality knitwear. 美利奴羊所产极细软羊毛，用于贴身层与优质针织（merino wool 美利奴羊毛， merino base layer 美利奴打底）。不可数。户外与日常针织均常见。
[例] ① Merino wool stays warm even when damp. 美利奴羊毛受潮仍保暖。 ② She prefers merino socks for long hikes. 长途徒步她更穿美利奴袜。
[搭] merino wool 美利奴羊毛, merino sweater 美利奴毛衣, merino base layer 美利奴打底, fine merino 细美利奴, merino blend 美利奴混纺
[联] wool 羊毛, cashmere 羊绒, sweater 毛衣, sock 袜子, warm 保暖的, soft 柔软的, outdoor 户外
[记] 不可数。merino 亦指美利奴羊这一品种；merino 比 generic wool 更强调「细、软、不刺痒」。

**rayon**
英/美 /ˈreɪɒn/ 美 /ˈreɪɑːn/
n. 人造丝；粘胶纤维
[义] A smooth artificial fibre made from cellulose, used in dresses, blouses, and linings. 由纤维素制成的光滑人造纤维，用于连衣裙、女衫、衬里（viscose rayon 粘胶人造丝， rayon blend 人造丝混纺）。不可数。标签常见 viscose（英式）与 rayon（美式）指近类材料。
[例] ① The dress hangs beautifully—the fabric is rayon with a silk-like feel. 这条裙子垂感好——面料是人造丝，手感类丝。 ② Rayon wrinkles easily, so pack carefully when travelling. 人造丝易皱，旅行要仔细打包。
[搭] viscose rayon 粘胶人造丝, rayon blend 人造丝混纺, rayon fabric 人造丝面料, silk-like rayon 类丝人造丝, lightweight rayon 轻量人造丝
[联] silk 丝绸, polyester 涤纶, fabric 布料, chiffon 雪纺, wrinkle 褶皱, blouse 女式衬衫, dress 连衣裙
[记] 不可数。rayon 为再生纤维素纤维总称之一；洗护常建议轻柔、低温。

**chiffon**
英/美 /ʃɪˈfɒn/ 美 /ʃɪˈfɑːn/
n. 雪纺
[义] A light transparent fabric, used especially for evening dresses and scarves. 轻薄半透明织物，尤用于晚装与围巾（silk chiffon 真丝雪纺， chiffon dress 雪纺连衣裙）。不可数。比 silk 更轻、更飘；需防勾丝。
[例] ① The bridesmaid dresses were pale pink chiffon. 伴娘裙是淡粉雪纺的。 ② Chiffon layers give the skirt movement when she walks. 雪纺层让她走路时裙摆有动感。
[搭] silk chiffon 真丝雪纺, chiffon dress 雪纺连衣裙, chiffon scarf 雪纺围巾, layered chiffon 分层雪纺, lightweight chiffon 轻雪纺
[联] silk 丝绸, satin 缎, dress 连衣裙, sheer 透明的, elegant 优雅的, formal 正式的, delicate 娇贵的
[记] 不可数。法语 chiffe（破布）演变而来；现指高档轻透面料。

**tweed**
英/美 /twiːd/
n. 粗花呢
[义] A rough woollen cloth with mixed colours, traditionally used for jackets and coats in Britain. 混色粗羊毛呢，英国传统用于夹克与大衣（tweed jacket 花呢夹克，Harris tweed 哈里斯花呢）。不可数。比 wool 更强调「粗织、混色、英伦」。
[例] ① He wore a tweed blazer for the autumn conference. 秋季会议他穿花呢西装外套。 ② Tweed is hard-wearing and hides minor stains well. 花呢耐穿，小污渍也不显眼。
[搭] tweed jacket 花呢夹克, Harris tweed 哈里斯花呢, tweed blazer 花呢西装外套, wool tweed 粗花呢, classic tweed 经典花呢
[联] wool 羊毛, blazer 西装外套, jacket 夹克, plaid 格子, British 英国的, formal 正式的, warm 保暖的
[记] 不可数。名字据说因 clerk 误读 tweel（斜纹）为 tweed 得名；Harris Tweed 为苏格兰外赫布里底注册产地。

**khaki**
英/美 /ˈkɑːki/
n. 卡其色；卡其布
adj. 卡其色的
[义] A dull brownish-yellow colour; cotton cloth of this colour used for uniforms and casual trousers. 暗黄褐色；该色棉布，用于制服与休闲长裤（khaki pants 卡其裤， khaki uniform 卡其制服）。不可数/形容词。与 chinos 常重叠，khaki 更强调颜色。
[例] ① Khaki trousers work for both office and weekend. 卡其裤上班周末都能穿。 ② The safari jacket came in classic khaki. Safari 夹克是经典卡其色。
[搭] khaki pants 卡其裤, khaki colour 卡其色, khaki uniform 卡其制服, khaki green 卡其绿, classic khaki 经典卡其色
[联] chinos 卡其裤, beige 米色, cotton 棉, casual 休闲的, trousers 长裤, military 军事的, neutral 中性的
[记] 不可数/形容词。源自乌尔都语 khāk（尘土色）；khaki 最初为英属印度军服色。

**cargo pants**
英/美 /ˈkɑːɡəʊ pænts/
n. 工装裤；多袋裤
[义] Casual trousers with large pockets on the sides of the legs. 腿侧有大口袋的休闲长裤（cargo pants with pockets 多袋工装裤）。复数形；a pair of cargo pants。90 年代流行，现仍作实用休闲款。
[例] ① Cargo pants are handy when you need to carry your phone without a bag. 不想背包时工装裤装手机很方便。 ② He wore olive cargo pants for the outdoor shoot. 外拍他穿橄榄绿工装裤。
[搭] a pair of cargo pants 一条工装裤, cargo pants with pockets 多袋工装裤, olive cargo pants 橄榄绿工装裤, baggy cargo pants 宽松工装裤, utility cargo pants 实用工装裤
[联] pants 裤子, pocket 口袋, casual 休闲的, outdoor 户外, shorts 短裤, utility 实用的, military 军事风
[记] 复数；a pair of cargo pants。cargo 本义「货物」；口袋原为工装实用设计。

**lapel**
英/美 /ləˈpel/
n. 翻领
[义] The folded flap on the front of a jacket or coat below the collar. 夹克或大衣领下前片折出的翻领（suit lapel 西装翻领， buttonhole on the lapel 翻领驳头眼）。可数。notched / peaked lapel 为常见版型术语。
[例] ① He pinned a flower to his lapel at the wedding. 婚礼他在翻领上别了一朵花。 ② The jacket has wide lapels in a seventies style. 这件夹克宽翻领，七十年代风格。
[搭] suit lapel 西装翻领, wide lapels 宽翻领, lapel pin 翻领别针, buttonhole on the lapel 驳头眼, notched lapel 单槽翻领
[联] collar 衣领, blazer 西装外套, suit 套装, jacket 夹克, brooch 胸针, formal 正式的, tie 领带
[记] 可数；a lapel。lapel 与 collar 不同：lapel 是西装前片折出部分。

**lining**
英/美 /ˈlaɪnɪŋ/
n. 衬里；内衬
[义] An inner layer of material sewn inside a coat, dress, or bag. 缝在大衣、连衣裙或包内的内层材料（silk lining 丝绸衬里， fully lined 全衬里）。可数/不可数。动词 line 表加衬里。
[例] ① The coat has a warm quilted lining for winter. 这件大衣有夹棉内衬过冬。 ② Check that the lining isn't torn before you buy the jacket. 买夹克前检查内衬是否破损。
[搭] silk lining 丝绸衬里, quilted lining 夹棉衬里, fully lined 全衬里, lining torn 衬里破损, inner lining 内衬
[联] coat 大衣, jacket 夹克, dress 连衣裙, silk 丝绸, fabric 布料, warm 保暖的, seam 接缝
[记] 可数/不可数。a lining。unlined 指「无衬里」；lining 亦指「地铁线路内衬」等，需看语境。

**breathable**
英/美 /ˈbreθəbl/
adj. 透气的
[义] Allowing air and moisture to pass through, so the wearer stays comfortable. 允许空气与湿气通过，穿着更舒适（breathable fabric 透气面料， breathable mesh 透气网眼）。形容词。运动装、户外装、夏季服装标签高频。
[例] ① Choose breathable cotton for summer shirts. 夏天衬衫选透气棉。 ② These running shoes use breathable mesh panels. 这款跑鞋用透气网眼拼接。
[搭] breathable fabric 透气面料, breathable mesh 透气网眼, breathable cotton 透气棉, highly breathable 高透气, breathable sportswear 透气运动装
[联] cotton 棉, mesh 网眼, fabric 布料, sportswear 运动装, comfortable 舒适的, waterproof 防水的, summer 夏天
[记] 形容词。breathable 与 waterproof 常需权衡；Gore-Tex 等品牌即追求「防水且透气」。

**thrift store**
英/美 /θrɪft stɔː(r)/
n. 旧货店； thrift 店
[义] A shop that sells second-hand clothes and goods, often to raise money for charity. 售卖二手衣物与物品、常为慈善筹款的商店（shop at a thrift store 逛旧货店， thrift store find 淘到的旧货好物）。可数。英式 charity shop 同义；美式 thrift store 极口语。
[例] ① She found a vintage leather jacket at a thrift store downtown. 她在市中心旧货店淘到复古皮夹克。 ② Thrift stores are full of surprises if you dig patiently. 旧货店耐心翻能淘到惊喜。
[搭] shop at a thrift store 逛旧货店, thrift store find 旧货淘货, local thrift store 本地旧货店, charity thrift store 慈善旧货店, thrift store shopping 旧货店购物
[联] second-hand 二手的, vintage 复古的, charity 慈善, affordable 负担得起的, clothes 衣服, sustainable 可持续的, bargain 便宜货
[记] 可数；a thrift store。英式 charity shop；thrift 本义「节俭」。

**outlet**
英/美 /ˈaʊtlet/
n. 奥特莱斯；折扣店
[义] A shop or mall where brands sell goods at reduced prices, often past-season stock. 品牌以减价出售商品（常为过季库存）的商店或商场（outlet mall 奥特莱斯商场， factory outlet 工厂店）。可数。亦指「出口、插座」，需看语境。
[例] ① They drove to the outlet mall for end-of-season deals. 他们开车去奥特莱斯买季末折扣。 ② The outlet had last year's running shoes at half price. 折扣店去年款跑鞋半价。
[搭] outlet mall 奥特莱斯商场, factory outlet 工厂店, outlet store 折扣店, designer outlet 设计师折扣店, outlet shopping 奥特莱斯购物
[联] brand 品牌, discount 折扣, sale 促销, mall 商场, bargain 便宜货, retail 零售, fashion 时尚
[记] 可数；an outlet。outlet 亦指「情绪出口、电源插座」，与零售义同形。

**sequin**
英/美 /ˈsiːkwɪn/
n. 亮片
[义] A small shiny disk sewn onto clothing for decoration. 缝在衣物上作装饰的小圆亮片（sequin dress 亮片连衣裙， covered in sequins 满缀亮片）。可数；常用复数 sequins。
[例] ① Her sequin dress caught every light on the dance floor. 她的亮片裙在舞池里闪闪发光。 ② Sequins can scratch delicate skin if the lining is thin. 内衬薄时亮片可能刮皮肤。
[搭] sequin dress 亮片连衣裙, covered in sequins 满缀亮片, gold sequins 金色亮片, sequin top 亮片上衣, fall off sequins 亮片脱落
[联] glitter 闪光, elegant 优雅的, party 派对, dress 连衣裙, formal 正式的, embroidery 刺绣, shiny 闪亮的
[记] 可数；常用复数 sequins。sequined 形容词（sequined gown 亮片礼服）。

**fringe**
英/美 /frɪndʒ/
n. 流苏；穗边
[义] A decorative border of hanging threads or strips on clothing, scarves, or bags. 衣物、围巾或包上垂挂的线绳或条饰边（fringe scarf 流苏围巾， fringe detail 流苏细节）。不可数/可数。英式 fringe 亦指「刘海」，与穗边义同形。
[例] ① The suede jacket had fringe along the back and sleeves. 麂皮夹克背与袖有流苏。 ② A fringe scarf adds movement when you walk. 流苏围巾走路时有动感。
[搭] fringe scarf 流苏围巾, fringe detail 流苏细节, leather fringe 皮流苏, fringe trim 流苏饰边, western fringe 西部风流苏
[联] scarf 围巾, suede 麂皮, trim 饰边, decoration 装饰, western 西部风, jacket 夹克, vintage 复古的
[记] 不可数/可数。英式 fringe = 美式 bangs（刘海）；服装义多指「穗、流苏」。

**monogram**
英/美 /ˈmɒnəɡræm/
n. 字母组合标志；姓名首字母刺绣
[义] A design made from two or more letters, especially initials, embroidered or printed on clothing, bags, or linens. 两个以上字母（尤姓名首字母）组成的图案，绣或印在衣、包、床品上（monogrammed shirt 绣 initials 的衬衫， LV monogram  LV 字母纹）。可数。
[例] ① She monogrammed his cufflinks with his initials. 她在他的袖扣上刻了姓名首字母。 ② Monogrammed towels are a classic wedding gift. 绣 initials 的毛巾是经典婚礼礼物。
[搭] monogrammed shirt 首字母刺绣衬衫, monogram design 字母组合设计, embroidered monogram 刺绣 monogram, personal monogram 个人字母标, monogrammed bag 字母标包
[联] initials 首字母, embroidery 刺绣, logo 标志, brand 品牌, elegant 优雅的, gift 礼物, personalize 个性化
[记] 可数；a monogram。mono-（单）+ gram（写）→ 字母组合；luxury brands 常用 monogram 作识别纹。

**shawl**
英/美 /ʃɔːl/
n. 披肩；大围巾
[义] A large piece of cloth worn over the shoulders or head, often for warmth or ceremony. 披在肩或头上的大块织物，保暖或礼仪用（wool shawl 羊毛披肩， silk shawl 丝质披肩）。可数。比 scarf 更大；可披可裹。
[例] ① She wrapped a wool shawl around her shoulders in the cold church. 教堂里冷，她用羊毛披肩裹肩。 ② The bride's silk shawl matched her dress embroidery. 新娘丝质披肩与裙上刺绣相配。
[搭] wool shawl 羊毛披肩, silk shawl 丝质披肩, wrap a shawl 裹披肩, prayer shawl 祈祷披肩, fringed shawl 流苏披肩
[联] scarf 围巾, wrap 裹, shoulders 肩, wool 羊毛, silk 丝绸, warm 保暖的, elegant 优雅的
[记] 可数；a shawl。shawl 源自波斯/印度服饰传统；pashmina 为一种 shawl 类型。

**beanie**
英/美 /ˈbiːni/
n. 针织帽；便帽
[义] A close-fitting knitted hat that covers the head and ears, usually without a brim. 贴头包裹耳部的针织帽，通常无檐（wool beanie 羊毛针织帽， roll-up beanie 卷边针织帽）。可数。比 cap 更贴头、更保暖；街头与冬季高频。
[例] ① He pulled a beanie over his ears before cycling in the cold. 天冷骑车前他把针织帽拉下来盖住耳朵。 ② Beanies are easy to stash in a coat pocket. 针织帽易塞进外套口袋。
[搭] wool beanie 羊毛针织帽, roll-up beanie 卷边针织帽, wear a beanie 戴针织帽, black beanie 黑色针织帽, winter beanie 冬用针织帽
[联] hat 帽子, cap 便帽, knit 编织, wool 羊毛, warm 保暖的, winter 冬天, scarf 围巾
[记] 可数；a beanie。beanie 亦曾指「学童帽」；与 baseball cap 风格不同。

**beret**
英/美 /ˈbereɪ/
n. 贝雷帽
[义] A soft round flat cap without a brim, often made of wool. 无檐的软圆扁帽，常为羊毛（black beret 黑色贝雷帽， wool beret 羊毛贝雷帽）。可数。法式、军事、艺术气质联想强。
[例] ① She wore a red beret with her trench coat. 她风衣配红色贝雷帽。 ② The artist was rarely photographed without his beret. 那位艺术家照片里几乎总戴着贝雷帽。
[搭] black beret 黑色贝雷帽, wool beret 羊毛贝雷帽, wear a beret 戴贝雷帽, French beret 法式贝雷帽, tilt a beret 歪戴贝雷帽
[联] hat 帽子, cap 便帽, wool 羊毛, French 法国的, elegant 优雅的, military 军事的, artistic 艺术的
[记] 可数；a beret。法语借词；军事单位常以 beret 颜色区分。

**fedora**
英/美 /fɪˈdɔːrə/
n. 费多拉帽；软毡帽
[义] A soft felt hat with a creased crown and a brim that can be snapped down in front. 软毡帽，顶有褶、檐可前压（black fedora 黑色费多拉， classic fedora 经典软毡帽）。可数。复古、爵士、侦探片气质；现亦作时尚单品。
[例] ① He tipped his fedora as the elevator doors opened. 电梯门开时他碰了碰费多拉帽檐。 ② Fedoras look sharp with a tailored coat in autumn. 秋天费多拉配剪裁大衣很利落。
[搭] black fedora 黑色费多拉, classic fedora 经典软毡帽, felt fedora 毡费多拉, wear a fedora 戴费多拉, brim of a fedora 费多拉帽檐
[联] hat 帽子, brim 帽檐, felt 毡, formal 正式的, vintage 复古的, elegant 优雅的, coat 大衣
[记] 可数；a fedora。名字来自同名剧作女主角；trilby 为类似窄檐款。

**choker**
英/美 /ˈtʃəʊkə(r)/
n. 颈链；贴颈项链
[义] A close-fitting necklace worn around the neck. 贴颈佩戴的项链（pearl choker 珍珠颈链， velvet choker 丝绒颈链）。可数。90 年代与复古时尚均常见；比 standard necklace 更短、更贴颈。
[例] ① She wore a simple black velvet choker to the party. 派对她戴黑色丝绒颈链。 ② Chokers can feel tight if they're sized wrong. 尺寸不对时颈链会觉得勒。
[搭] pearl choker 珍珠颈链, velvet choker 丝绒颈链, black choker 黑色颈链, tight choker 贴颈链, wear a choker 戴颈链
[联] necklace 项链, jewelry 珠宝, pendant 吊坠, elegant 优雅的, vintage 复古的, collar 衣领, throat 颈
[记] 可数；a choker。choke 动词「窒息」与 choker 同族；choker 亦指「扼喉式防守」，体育义。

**anklet**
英/美 /ˈæŋklət/
n. 脚链
[义] A piece of jewellery worn around the ankle. 绕踝佩戴的首饰（silver anklet 银脚链， delicate anklet 细脚链）。可数。夏季、海滩、部分文化礼仪语境出现。
[例] ① She wore a thin silver anklet with her sandals. 她凉鞋配细银脚链。 ② The anklet chimed softly when she walked barefoot. 赤脚走路时脚链轻轻作响。
[搭] silver anklet 银脚链, delicate anklet 细脚链, gold anklet 金脚链, wear an anklet 戴脚链, ankle chain 脚链
[联] bracelet 手镯, jewelry 珠宝, ankle 踝, sandal 凉鞋, beach 海滩, summer 夏天, ring 戒指
[记] 可数；an anklet。ankle + -let（小饰件）；部分职场/正式场合不宜外露。

**tiara**
英/美 /tiˈɑːrə/
n. 冠状头饰；头冠
[义] A decorative half-crown worn by women on formal occasions, especially weddings. 女性在正式场合佩戴的半冠状头饰，尤婚礼（bridal tiara 新娘头冠，diamond tiara 钻石头冠）。可数。
[例] ① The bride wore a simple pearl tiara with her veil. 新娘面纱配简约珍珠头冠。 ② The tiara was borrowed from her grandmother's jewellery box. 头冠是从祖母首饰盒借来的。
[搭] bridal tiara 新娘头冠, diamond tiara 钻石头冠, pearl tiara 珍珠头冠, wear a tiara 戴头冠, crystal tiara 水晶头冠
[联] crown 王冠, veil 面纱, wedding 婚礼, jewelry 珠宝, elegant 优雅的, bride 新娘, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a tiara。源自希腊/拉丁语「头饰」；皇室 tiara 与 bridal tiara 设计不同但词同。

**dress up**
英/美 /dres ʌp/
v. 盛装；打扮
[义] To wear formal or special clothes, or to put on a costume. 穿正式或特别服装；或穿化装服（dress up for dinner 晚宴盛装， dress up as a pirate 扮海盗）。与 dress down 相对。短语动词。
[形] dressed up / dressed up / dressing up
[例] ① Do we need to dress up for the restaurant, or is casual fine? 去那家餐厅要盛装吗，还是休闲就行？ ② The kids love dressing up in their parents' old clothes. 孩子们爱穿父母旧衣服扮大人。
[搭] dress up for dinner 晚宴盛装, dress up for a party 派对盛装, dress up as 装扮成, get dressed up 打扮好, dress-up party 化装派对
[联] dress down 穿着随意, formal 正式的, outfit 一套搭配, costume 戏服, elegant 优雅的, casual 休闲的, occasion 场合
[记] 短语动词。dress up 亦指「美化、粉饰」（dress up the report 把报告包装好看）。

**dress down**
英/美 /dres daʊn/
v. 穿着随意；训斥
[义] To wear clothes that are less formal than usual. 穿着比平时更不正式（dress down Friday 休闲星期五， dress down for travel 旅行穿着随意）。短语动词。亦指「训斥」（dress sb. down），与着装义不同。
[形] dressed down / dressed down / dressing down
[例] ① The office allows staff to dress down on Fridays. 公司允许员工周五穿着随意。 ② For the long flight she dressed down in leggings and a hoodie. 长途航班她穿打底裤和连帽衫，怎么舒服怎么来。
[搭] dress down Friday 休闲星期五, dress down for travel 旅行穿着随意, casually dressed down 穿着很随意, dress-down day 便装日, dress down policy 便装政策
[联] dress up 盛装, casual 休闲的, formal 正式的, hoodie 连帽衫, jeans 牛仔裤, office 办公室, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 短语动词。dress-down 作名词/形容词（a dress-down day 便装日）；dress sb. down 指「痛骂」。

**layer**
英/美 /ˈleɪə(r)/
v. 分层穿着
n. 层
[义] To wear several pieces of clothing on top of each other for warmth or style. 为保暖或造型而多层叠穿（layer a sweater under a coat 大衣里叠毛衣， layer for cold weather 天冷分层穿）。可数名词表「层」。时尚与户外语境高频。
[形] layered / layered / layering
[例] ① Layer a thin T-shirt under your shirt for extra warmth. 衬衫里叠件薄 T 恤更暖和。 ② Good layering lets you adjust when the temperature changes. 会分层穿，气温变也好调整。
[搭] layer clothes 分层穿着, layer for warmth 为保暖叠穿, layered look 分层造型, base layer 贴身层, outer layer 外层
[联] coat 大衣, sweater 毛衣, T-shirt T 恤, warm 保暖的, cold 冷的, outfit 一套搭配, outdoor 户外
[记] 可数/动词。layer 亦指「涂层、阶层」；base layer / mid layer / outer layer 为户外三层穿衣法。

**slip-on**
英/美 /slɪp ɒn/ 美 /slɪp ɑːn/
adj. 套脚的
n. 套脚鞋
[义] Designed to be put on without laces or buckles; a shoe of this type. 无需鞋带或扣即可穿入；此类鞋（slip-on shoes 套脚鞋， slip-on sneakers 套脚运动鞋）。形容词/名词。与 lace-up 相对。
[例] ① Slip-on shoes are convenient at airport security. 套脚鞋过机场安检方便。 ② She prefers slip-ons because bending to tie laces hurts her back. 她更爱套脚鞋——弯腰系鞋带会背疼。
[搭] slip-on shoes 套脚鞋, slip-on sneakers 套脚运动鞋, slip-on loafers 套脚乐福鞋, easy slip-on 易穿套脚, slip-on design 套脚设计
[联] loafer 乐福鞋, mule 穆勒鞋, lace-up 系带的, shoe 鞋, convenient 方便的, sneaker 运动鞋, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 形容词/名词。a slip-on = a slip-on shoe。slip on 动词短语表「快速穿上」。

**lace-up**
英/美 /leɪs ʌp/
adj. 系带的
n. 系带鞋
[义] Fastened with laces; a shoe that ties with laces. 以鞋带系紧；系带鞋（lace-up boots 系带靴， lace-up dress shoes 系带正装鞋）。形容词/名词。与 slip-on 相对。
[例] ① Lace-up boots give better ankle support on rough trails. 崎岖步道系带靴更护踝。 ② He bought classic lace-up oxfords for the interview. 面试他买了经典系带牛津鞋。
[搭] lace-up boots 系带靴, lace-up shoes 系带鞋, lace-up dress shoes 系带正装鞋, lace-up sneakers 系带运动鞋, traditional lace-up 传统系带款
[联] shoelace 鞋带, boot 靴子, shoe 鞋, slip-on 套脚的, fasten 扣紧, formal 正式的, support 支撑
[记] 形容词/名词。lace up 动词短语表「系好鞋带」；lace-up 作定语（lace-up design）。

**Velcro**
英/美 /ˈvelkrəʊ/
n. 魔术贴；尼龙搭扣
[义] A fastening system of two strips that stick together when pressed, used on shoes, jackets, and bags. 两片按压即粘合的扣合系统，用于鞋、夹克、包（Velcro strap 魔术贴带， Velcro closure 魔术贴开合）。不可数；商标名通用化。儿童鞋、户外装备常见。
[例] ① The child's shoes have Velcro straps instead of laces. 孩子的鞋用魔术贴代替鞋带。 ② The jacket cuff adjusts with a Velcro tab. 夹克袖口用魔术贴调节。
[搭] Velcro strap 魔术贴带, Velcro closure 魔术贴开合, Velcro fastener 魔术贴扣, adjust with Velcro 用魔术贴调节, peel apart Velcro 撕开魔术贴
[联] fasten 扣紧, strap 带条, shoe 鞋, jacket 夹克, convenient 方便的, zip 拉链, button 纽扣
[记] 不可数；商标 Velcro，亦写 velcro。比 button/zip 更易操作，但耐久与美观通常较弱。

**stain remover**
英/美 /steɪn rɪˈmuːvə(r)/
n. 去污剂
[义] A product used to remove stains from clothing before or during washing. 洗涤前或洗涤中去除衣物污渍的产品（apply stain remover 涂去污剂， stain remover spray 去污喷雾）。可数/不可数。
[例] ① Blot the spill, then apply stain remover before washing. 吸干溢出物，洗前先涂上去污剂。 ② Keep a stain remover pen in your bag for emergencies. 包里备支去污笔应急。
[搭] apply stain remover 涂去污剂, stain remover spray 去污喷雾, stain remover pen 去污笔, pre-treat with stain remover 预涂去污剂, commercial stain remover 市售去污剂
[联] stain 污渍, wash 洗, laundry 洗衣, bleach 漂白, detergent 洗涤剂, spot 斑点, clean 清洁
[记] 可数/不可数。pre-treat 指「洗前预处理污渍」；stain remover 不保证去除所有顽固渍。

**fabric softener**
英/美 /ˈfæbrɪk ˈsɒftənə(r)/
n. 衣物柔顺剂
[义] A liquid added to the final rinse when washing clothes to make them feel softer and smell fresh. 洗衣最后一遍漂洗时加入、使织物更软更香的液体（use fabric softener 使用柔顺剂， skip fabric softener 跳过柔顺剂）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Skip fabric softener on towels—it reduces absorbency. 毛巾别用柔顺剂——会降低吸水性。 ② She added fabric softener for the bed sheets. 洗床单她加了柔顺剂。
[搭] use fabric softener 使用柔顺剂, skip fabric softener 不用柔顺剂, liquid fabric softener 液体柔顺剂, fabric softener sheet 柔顺纸, scented fabric softener 香型柔顺剂
[联] laundry 洗衣, detergent 洗涤剂, wash 洗, soft 柔软的, towel 毛巾, clothes 衣服, scent 香味
[记] 不可数/可数。towels、sportswear、microfiber 常建议不用柔顺剂，以免影响功能。

**laundry basket**
英/美 /ˈlɒndri bɑːskɪt/ 美 /ˈlɑːndri bæskɪt/
n. 洗衣篮
[义] A basket or hamper used to collect dirty clothes before washing. 洗涤前收集脏衣的篮或 hamper（empty the laundry basket 倒空洗衣篮， plastic laundry basket 塑料洗衣篮）。可数。
[例] ① The laundry basket was overflowing again by Thursday. 到周四洗衣篮又满出来了。 ② Sort lights and darks into separate laundry baskets. 把浅色和深色分放进不同洗衣篮。
[搭] empty the laundry basket 倒空洗衣篮, plastic laundry basket 塑料洗衣篮, laundry basket full 满洗衣篮, carry the laundry basket 提洗衣篮, hamper laundry basket 带盖洗衣篮

[记] 可数；a laundry basket。hamper 常指带盖大篮；laundry hamper 与 laundry basket 常互换。

**ironing board**
英/美 /ˈaɪənɪŋ bɔːrd/
n. 烫衣板
[义] A padded board, usually on legs, on which clothes are ironed. 有垫、通常带支架的熨烫用板（folding ironing board 折叠烫衣板， set up the ironing board 支起烫衣板）。可数。
[例] ① She set up the ironing board in the spare room. 她在空房间支起烫衣板。 ② Always fold the ironing board flat when you're done. 烫完把烫衣板折平收好。
[搭] folding ironing board 折叠烫衣板, set up the ironing board 支烫衣板, ironing board cover 烫衣板套, adjustable ironing board 可调高度烫衣板, put away the ironing board 收起烫衣板

n. 鞋油
[义] A substance rubbed onto leather shoes to clean, shine, and protect them. 擦在皮鞋上以清洁、上光、保护的膏体（brown shoe polish 棕色鞋油， apply shoe polish 打鞋油）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① He applied shoe polish before the job interview. 面试前他打了鞋油。 ② Good shoe polish extends the life of leather dress shoes. 好鞋油能延长皮鞋寿命。

**garment bag**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːmənt bæɡ/
n. 衣套；防尘衣袋
[义] A long bag used to cover and protect suits, dresses, or coats during travel or storage. 旅行或存放时罩住西装、连衣裙、大衣的长袋（hanging garment bag 挂式衣袋， zippered garment bag 拉链衣袋）。可数。
[例] ① He hung his suit in a garment bag for the flight. 西装挂进衣袋带上飞机。 ② Garment bags help formal clothes stay wrinkle-free. 衣袋帮正装少皱。
