**stretch**
英/美 /stretʃ/
v. 拉伸；弹性变形
n. 弹性；一段
[义] Of fabric, to become longer or wider when pulled and return partly to shape. 织物被拉时伸长、松后部分回弹（stretch denim 弹力牛仔， four-way stretch 四面弹）。动词/名词。名词 a stretch 亦指「一段距离/时间」，需看语境。
[形] stretched / stretched / stretching
[例] ① These jeans have a bit of stretch for comfort. 这条牛仔裤有点弹力更舒适。 ② Don't overstretch the collar when you pull the shirt on. 穿衬衫时别把领子拉过度。
[搭] stretch denim 弹力牛仔, four-way stretch 四面弹, stretch fabric 弹力面料, stretch to fit 拉伸以适应, a little stretch 一点弹性
[联] spandex 氨纶, elastic 弹性的, fit 合身, leggings 打底裤, fabric 布料, comfortable 舒适的, tight 紧身的
[记] 动词/名词。stretch 亦指「伸展身体、 stretch the truth 夸大」；has stretch = 有弹力。

**pill**
英/美 /pɪl/
v. 起球
n. 毛球
[义] Of fabric, to form small balls of fibre on the surface after rubbing or washing. 织物摩擦或洗涤后表面形成纤维小球（sweater pilling 毛衣起球， fabric pills 面料起球）。动词/名词。名词 pill 亦指「药丸」，需看语境。
[例] ① Cheap knits pill after just a few washes. 廉价针织洗几次就起球。 ② Use a fabric shaver to remove pills from the sweater. 用去球器去掉毛衣上的毛球。
[搭] sweater pilling 毛衣起球, fabric pills 面料起球, remove pills 去毛球, pill badly 严重起球, anti-pill finish 防起球处理
[联] sweater 毛衣, knit 编织, wool 羊毛, wash 洗, fabric 布料, quality 质量, lint 毛絮
[记] 动词/名词。pilling 名词；fabric shaver / de-piller 为去球工具。

**lint**
英/美 /lɪnt/
n. 毛絮；线头
[义] Tiny fibres that come off clothes, especially in the wash or dryer. 衣物脱落的细纤维，尤在洗烘过程中（lint trap 绒毛收集器，lint roller 粘毛滚筒）。不可数。
[例] ① Clean the dryer's lint trap after every load. 每次烘干后清理绒毛收集器。 ② Black trousers show lint easily—keep a roller handy. 黑裤子容易沾毛絮——备个粘毛滚筒。
[搭] lint trap 绒毛收集器, lint roller 粘毛滚筒, pick up lint 粘去毛絮, lint on clothes 衣服上的毛絮, dryer lint 烘干机毛絮
[联] laundry 洗衣, wash 洗, fabric 布料, pill 起球, clean 清洁, dryer 烘干机, dark clothes 深色衣物
[记] 不可数。lint 亦指「医用脱脂棉」；lint roller 粘毛滚筒为深色衣必备。

**fashion show**
英/美 /ˈfæʃn ʃəʊ/
n. 时装秀
[义] An event where models walk a runway to display a designer's new collection. 模特走 T 台展示设计师新系列的活动（Paris Fashion Show 巴黎时装秀， attend a fashion show 看时装秀）。可数。
[例] ① The fashion show opened with a dramatic black cape. 时装秀以一件黑色大披风开场。 ② Front-row guests at the fashion show included several celebrities. 时装秀前排嘉宾有多位名人。
[搭] attend a fashion show 看时装秀, Paris fashion show 巴黎时装秀, runway fashion show T 台时装秀, fashion show finale 时装秀压轴, live fashion show 现场时装秀
[联] runway T 台, model 模特, designer 设计师, collection 系列, trend 趋势, elegant 优雅的, fashion 时尚
[记] 可数；a fashion show。fashion week 指「时装周」，含多场 show。

**bespoke**
英/美 /bɪˈspəʊk/
adj. 定制的；量身定做的
[义] Made to a customer's specific measurements and requirements, especially for suits and shoes. 按顾客尺寸与要求定制，尤指西装与鞋（bespoke suit 定制西装， bespoke tailor 定制裁缝）。形容词。英式高频；美式亦说 custom-made。
[例] ① A bespoke suit takes several fittings but fits perfectly. 定制西装要试几次身，但非常合身。 ② Bespoke shoes are expensive but last for years. 定制鞋贵但可穿多年。
[搭] bespoke suit 定制西装, bespoke tailor 定制裁缝, bespoke shoes 定制鞋, bespoke service 定制服务, fully bespoke 全定制
[联] tailor 裁缝, suit 套装, fit 合身, measurement 尺寸, luxury 奢侈, custom-made 定制的, formal 正式的
[记] 形容词。bespoke 原义「已订购」；与 off-the-rack（成衣）相对。

**ready-to-wear**
英/美 /ˌredi tə ˈweə(r)/
adj. 成衣的；现货服装的
n. 成衣
[义] Made in standard sizes and sold in shops, not custom-made. 标准尺码、店内现货出售，非定制（ready-to-wear collection 成衣系列， off-the-rack 成衣）。形容词/名词。与 haute couture（高级定制）相对。
[例] ① Most people buy ready-to-wear suits from department stores. 多数人从百货买成衣西装。 ② The designer's ready-to-wear line is more affordable than couture. 设计师成衣线比高级定制买得起。
[搭] ready-to-wear collection 成衣系列, ready-to-wear fashion 成衣时尚, off-the-rack 成衣, buy ready-to-wear 买成衣, ready-to-wear brand 成衣品牌
[联] bespoke 定制的, tailor 裁缝, suit 套装, retail 零售, brand 品牌, fashion 时尚, department store 百货
[记] 形容词/名词。缩写 RTW；couture 指「高级定制时装」，价位与流程完全不同。

**hand-me-down**
英/美 /ˌhænd miː ˈdaʊn/
n. 传下来的旧衣
adj. 旧传旧用的
[义] Used clothing passed from an older person to a younger one, especially in a family. 从年长者传给年幼者的旧衣，尤家庭内（hand-me-down clothes 姐姐传下来的衣服， hand-me-down jacket 传下来的夹克）。可数/形容词。
[例] ① Most of his winter coats were hand-me-downs from his brother. 他的冬大衣多是哥哥传下来的。 ② She didn't mind hand-me-down dresses if they still looked good. 裙子只要还好看，她不介意是传下来的。
[搭] hand-me-down clothes 传下来的旧衣, hand-me-down jacket 传下来的夹克, family hand-me-down 家里传下来的, accept hand-me-downs 接受旧衣传递, gently worn hand-me-down 八成新传衣
[联] second-hand 二手的, thrift store 旧货店, sibling 兄弟姐妹, family 家庭, save money 省钱, sustainable 可持续的, clothes 衣服
[记] 可数/形容词。a hand-me-down 指「一件传下来的东西」；含情感与节俭色彩，未必贬义。

**parka**
英/美 /ˈpɑːkə/
n. 派克大衣；厚连帽外套
[义] A warm hip-length or longer coat with a hood, often padded and weatherproof. 带帽保暖外套，常为及臀或更长、夹棉、防风雨（down parka 羽绒派克，fur-lined parka 毛领派克）。可数。美式常用 parka；英式亦说 anorak，但 parka 更强调厚、防寒。
[例] ① She zipped her parka up to the chin in the snowstorm. 暴风雪中她把派克大衣拉链拉到下巴。 ② A good parka is worth the investment if you live up north. 住北方值得投资一件好派克。
[搭] down parka 羽绒派克, fur-lined parka 毛领派克, hooded parka 连帽派克, waterproof parka 防水派克, packable parka 可折叠派克
[联] anorak 防风夹克, coat 大衣, hood 兜帽, waterproof 防水的, winter 冬天, layer 分层穿着, outdoor 户外
[记] 可数；a parka。源自 Nenets 语，经俄语进入英语；极地、城市冬季均常见。

**sarong**
英/美 /səˈrɒŋ/
n. 莎笼；裹裙
[义] A large piece of cloth wrapped around the waist or body, worn in Southeast Asia and at beaches. 围在腰或裹在身上的大块布，东南亚与海滩常见（beach sarong 沙滩裹裙， silk sarong 丝绸莎笼）。可数。旅行、度假、泳池边高频。
[例] ① She tied a sarong over her swimsuit after leaving the pool. 出泳池后她在泳衣外系了条莎笼。 ② Sarongs double as light cover-ups on hot evenings. 夏夜莎笼还可当轻薄外搭。
[搭] beach sarong 沙滩裹裙, silk sarong 丝绸莎笼, tie a sarong 系莎笼, wrap a sarong 裹莎笼, colourful sarong 彩色莎笼
[联] swimsuit 泳衣, beach 海滩, skirt 裙子, wrap 裹, summer 夏天, travel 旅行, casual 休闲的
[记] 可数；a sarong。马来语借词；穿法多样，可围腰可披肩。

**earmuffs**
英/美 /ˈɪrmʌfs/
n. 耳罩；御寒耳套
[义] A pair of soft coverings joined by a band, worn over the ears to keep them warm. 以头带连接、罩在耳上的软保暖套（winter earmuffs 冬用耳罩， foldable earmuffs 可折叠耳罩）。复数形；a pair of earmuffs。
[例] ① The kids wore bright earmuffs on the sledding hill. 孩子们在滑雪坡戴亮色耳罩。 ② Foldable earmuffs fit easily in a coat pocket. 可折叠耳罩易放外套口袋。
[搭] a pair of earmuffs 一副耳罩, winter earmuffs 冬用耳罩, foldable earmuffs 可折叠耳罩, wear earmuffs 戴耳罩, fluffy earmuffs 毛绒耳罩
[联] hat 帽子, beanie 针织帽, scarf 围巾, winter 冬天, cold 冷的, warm 保暖的, snow 雪
[记] 复数；a pair of earmuffs。ear + muffs；比帽子更专护耳部。

**balaclava**
英/美 /ˌbæləˈklɑːvə/
n. 巴拉克拉法帽；全脸针织帽
[义] A close-fitting woollen hat that covers most of the head and neck, leaving only part of the face exposed. 贴头羊毛帽，覆盖大部分头颈，只露部分面部（ski balaclava 滑雪面罩帽， black balaclava 黑色面罩帽）。可数。滑雪、极寒、骑行常见；新闻中亦指蒙面装扮，语境须辨。
[例] ① He pulled on a balaclava before cycling in freezing wind. 零度大风骑车前他套上巴拉克拉法帽。 ② A thin balaclava fits under a helmet comfortably. 薄款可舒适地戴在头盔下。
[搭] ski balaclava 滑雪面罩帽, wool balaclava 羊毛面罩帽, wear a balaclava 戴面罩帽, thin balaclava 薄款, thermal balaclava 保暖面罩帽
[联] beanie 针织帽, scarf 围巾, ski 滑雪, winter 冬天, mask 面罩, hood 兜帽, cold 冷的
[记] 可数；a balaclava。克里米亚战争巴拉克拉瓦战役得名；勿与 ski mask 完全等同，但用法近。

**stole**
英/美 /stəʊl/
n. 长披肩；圣职披肩
[义] A long narrow piece of cloth worn over the shoulders, especially with formal evening dress or clergy robes. 披在肩上的长条织物，尤配晚装或神职袍（fur stole 毛皮长披肩，silk stole 丝绸长披肩）。可数。比 shawl 更窄长、更正式。
[例] ① She draped a white fur stole over her evening gown. 她把白色毛皮长披肩搭在晚礼服上。 ② The priest wore an embroidered stole during the service. 仪式中神父佩戴刺绣圣职披肩。
[搭] fur stole 毛皮长披肩, silk stole 丝绸长披肩, evening stole 晚装长披肩, drape a stole 搭长披肩, embroidered stole 刺绣披肩
[联] shawl 披肩, gown 礼服, elegant 优雅的, formal 正式的, fur 毛皮, wedding 婚礼, wrap 裹
[记] 可数；a stole。steal（偷窃）过去式也是 stole，同形异义；宗教 stole 与时尚 stole 词同。

**miniskirt**
英/美 /ˈmɪniskɜːrt/
n. 迷你裙
[义] A very short skirt, well above the knee. 远短于膝的短裙（black miniskirt 黑色迷你裙， denim miniskirt 牛仔迷你裙）。可数。亦作 mini skirt；1960 年代起进入日常时尚词汇。
[例] ① Miniskirts came back in several spring collections. 迷你裙在多个春季系列回归。 ② She paired a miniskirt with tights for warmth. 她迷你裙配连裤袜保暖。
[搭] black miniskirt 黑色迷你裙, denim miniskirt 牛仔迷你裙, wear a miniskirt 穿迷你裙, short miniskirt 超短迷你裙, pleated miniskirt 百褶迷你裙
[联] skirt 裙子, tights 连裤袜, fashion 时尚, trend 趋势, short 短的, casual 休闲的, vintage 复古的
[记] 可数；a miniskirt。mini + skirt；工作场合 dress code 可能限制过短裙。

**pencil skirt**
英/美 /ˈpensl skɜːrt/
n. 铅笔裙；包臀裙
[义] A narrow straight skirt that fits closely and ends around the knee. 窄而直、贴身、通常及膝的裙子（black pencil skirt 黑色铅笔裙， high-waisted pencil skirt 高腰铅笔裙）。可数。职场与正式场合常见，强调利落线条。
[例] ① A pencil skirt and blouse make a simple office outfit. 铅笔裙配女衫是简洁职场搭配。 ② The pencil skirt is tight—take smaller steps when you walk. 铅笔裙很紧——走路步幅要小些。
[搭] black pencil skirt 黑色铅笔裙, high-waisted pencil skirt 高腰铅笔裙, knee-length pencil skirt 及膝铅笔裙, pencil skirt suit 铅笔裙套装, wear a pencil skirt 穿铅笔裙
[联] skirt 裙子, blouse 女式衬衫, formal 正式的, office 办公室, tight 紧身的, elegant 优雅的, suit 套装
[记] 可数；a pencil skirt。因轮廓如铅笔得名；比 A-line skirt 更贴腿。

**evening wear**
英/美 /ˈiːvnɪŋ weə(r)/
n. 晚装（总称）
[义] Clothes worn for formal events in the evening, such as gowns, tuxedos, and dress shoes. 晚间正式活动穿着的衣物总称，如礼服、无尾礼服、正装鞋。不可数。比 formal wear 更强调「晚间场合」。
[例] ① The invitation calls for evening wear, not business casual. 请柬要求晚装，不是商务休闲。 ② The shop has a separate section for evening wear. 店里单独设晚装区。
[搭] formal evening wear 正式晚装, evening wear dress code 晚装着装要求, black-tie evening wear 黑领结晚装, rent evening wear 租晚装, elegant evening wear 雅致晚装
[联] gown 礼服, tuxedo 礼服, formal 正式的, elegant 优雅的, wedding 婚礼, attire 着装, dress up 盛装
[记] 不可数。evening + wear 复合；black tie / white tie 为更具体的正式级别。

**activewear**
英/美 /ˈæktɪvweə(r)/
n. 运动休闲装（总称）
[义] Clothing designed for exercise and physical activity. 为锻炼与体育活动设计的服装总称（activewear brand 运动品牌， yoga activewear 瑜伽运动装）。不可数。比 sportswear 更偏「日常健身穿搭」；与 athleisure 近义。
[例] ① The brand expanded from running shoes into full activewear. 该品牌从跑鞋扩展到完整运动装。 ② Activewear should wick sweat and allow movement. 运动装应吸汗且不妨碍动作。
[搭] activewear brand 运动品牌, yoga activewear 瑜伽运动装, women's activewear 女式运动装, activewear line 运动系列, breathable activewear 透气运动装
[联] sportswear 运动装, leggings 打底裤, sneakers 运动鞋, gym 健身房, breathable 透气的, stretch 弹力, athleisure 运动休闲风
[记] 不可数。active + wear 复合；强调功能（吸汗、弹力）而非仅外观。

**athleisure**
英/美 /ˈæθliːʒə(r)/
n. 运动休闲风； athleisure 穿搭
[义] Casual clothing designed to be suitable for both exercise and everyday wear. 既适合运动又适合日常穿着的休闲服装风格（athleisure trend 运动休闲风，athleisure wear 运动休闲穿搭）。不可数。leggings 配卫衣、跑鞋上街即典型 athleisure。
[例] ① Athleisure blurred the line between gym clothes and street fashion. 运动休闲风模糊了健身服与街头时尚的界限。 ② She lives in athleisure—leggings, hoodies, and clean sneakers. 她整天 athleisure 风——打底裤、连帽衫、干净运动鞋。
[搭] athleisure trend 运动休闲风, athleisure wear 运动休闲穿搭, athleisure brand 运动休闲品牌, athleisure look 运动休闲造型, everyday athleisure 日常运动休闲
[联] activewear 运动装, sportswear 运动装, leggings 打底裤, hoodie 连帽衫, sneakers 运动鞋, casual 休闲的, trend 趋势
[记] 不可数。athletic + leisure 混成；2010 年代后零售与媒体高频词。

**dungarees**
英/美 /ˌdʌŋɡəˈriːz/
n. 背带裤（英式）
[义] Trousers with a bib front and straps over the shoulders; British term often for denim overalls. 前胸 bib、肩带吊挂的长裤；英式常指牛仔背带裤（denim dungarees 牛仔背带裤）。复数形；a pair of dungarees。美式更常说 overalls。
[例] ① She wore dungarees and a striped T-shirt to the picnic. 野餐她穿背带裤配条纹 T 恤。 ② In the UK, dungarees usually means denim overalls, not work boilersuits. 在英国，dungarees 通常指牛仔背带裤，不是工装连体服。
[搭] a pair of dungarees 一条背带裤, denim dungarees 牛仔背带裤, children's dungarees 儿童背带裤, wear dungarees 穿背带裤, vintage dungarees 复古背带裤
[联] overalls 工装连体裤, jeans 牛仔裤, strap 肩带, casual 休闲的, denim 丹宁, vintage 复古的, children 儿童
[记] 复数；a pair of dungarees。源自印地语 dongri（孟买附近产粗棉布）；英美义有细微差别。

**oxford**
英/美 /ˈɒksfərd/
n. 牛津鞋
[义] A type of lace-up shoe with a closed lacing system, worn for formal and business dress. 封闭式鞋带系统的系带鞋，用于正式与商务穿着（black oxfords 黑色牛津鞋， leather oxfords 皮牛津鞋）。可数。与 derby / blucher（开放式鞋带）相对。
[例] ① Black oxfords are the safest choice with a dark suit. 深色西装配黑牛津鞋最稳妥。 ② He polished his oxfords the night before the interview. 面试前一晚他擦好了牛津鞋。
[搭] black oxfords 黑色牛津鞋, leather oxfords 皮牛津鞋, pair of oxfords 一双牛津鞋, cap-toe oxford 三接头牛津鞋, wear oxfords 穿牛津鞋
[联] loafer 乐福鞋, lace-up 系带的, shoe 鞋, formal 正式的, suit 套装, leather 皮革, interview 面试
[记] 可数；an oxford。Oxford 亦为城市名、牛津纺（oxford cloth 牛津布）不同义。

**wedge**
英/美 /wedʒ/
n. 坡跟鞋；楔形跟
[义] A shoe with a heel that runs from the back to the middle or front of the sole in one piece. 鞋跟自后部与鞋底连成一体、呈楔形的鞋（wedge sandals 坡跟凉鞋， wedge heels 坡跟鞋）。可数。比 stiletto 稳、比 flat 高。
[例] ① Wedge sandals are easier to walk in than stilettos. 坡跟凉鞋比细高跟好走。 ② She chose nude wedges to lengthen her legs without pain. 她选裸色坡跟鞋拉长腿部线条又不太疼。
[搭] wedge sandals 坡跟凉鞋, wedge heels 坡跟鞋, nude wedges 裸色坡跟鞋, platform wedge 厚底坡跟, wear wedges 穿坡跟鞋
[联] heel 鞋跟, stiletto 细高跟, sandal 凉鞋, shoe 鞋, comfortable 舒适的, summer 夏天, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a wedge。wedge 亦指「楔形物、楔入」，需看语境。

**flat**
英/美 /flæt/
n. 平底鞋
adj. 平的；平底的
[义] A shoe with very little or no heel. 几乎无跟或完全平底的鞋（ballet flats 芭蕾平底鞋， flat shoes 平底鞋）。可数名词。形容词 flat 表「平的、扁平的」。
[例] ① She switched to flats after three hours in heels. 高跟鞋穿三小时后她换成了平底鞋。 ② Comfortable flats are essential for long museum days. 长时间逛博物馆必备舒适平底鞋。
[搭] ballet flats 芭蕾平底鞋, flat shoes 平底鞋, comfortable flats 舒适平底鞋, pair of flats 一双平底鞋, wear flats 穿平底鞋
[联] heel 鞋跟, stiletto 细高跟, shoe 鞋, comfortable 舒适的, casual 休闲的, walk 步行, sneaker 运动鞋
[记] 可数；a flat。flat 亦指「公寓、平坦的」，需看语境。

**wellies**
英/美 /ˈweliz/
n. 惠灵顿靴；雨靴（英式）
[义] Waterproof rubber or plastic boots worn in wet or muddy conditions. 湿泥地穿着的防水橡胶或塑料靴（pair of wellies 一双雨靴， green wellies 绿色雨靴）。复数形；a pair of wellies。全称 Wellington boots；英式极口语。
[例] ① Pull your wellies on—the field is mud after the rain. 穿上雨靴——雨后田野全是泥。 ② Festival-goers lived in wellies all weekend. 音乐节观众整个周末都穿雨靴。
[搭] a pair of wellies 一双雨靴, green wellies 绿色雨靴, rubber wellies 橡胶雨靴, festival wellies 音乐节雨靴, wear wellies 穿雨靴
[联] boot 靴子, rain 雨, waterproof 防水的, mud 泥, outdoor 户外, rubber 橡胶, festival 音乐节
[记] 复数；a pair of wellies。Wellington 公爵得名；美式更常说 rain boots。

**water-resistant**
英/美 /ˈwɔːtərɪzɪstənt/
adj. 防泼水的
[义] Able to resist water to a degree, but not fully waterproof in heavy rain or submersion. 一定程度抵御水，但非大雨或浸泡下完全防水（water-resistant jacket 防泼水夹克， water-resistant watch 防泼水手表）。形容词。比 waterproof 程度弱；标签常见。
[例] ① The coat is water-resistant, fine for drizzle but not a downpour. 这件外套防泼水，毛毛雨可以，暴雨不行。 ② Water-resistant fabric sheds light rain for a while. 防泼水面料能挡一阵小雨。
[搭] water-resistant jacket 防泼水夹克, water-resistant coating 防泼水涂层, water-resistant fabric 防泼水面料, highly water-resistant 高防泼水, water-resistant vs waterproof 防泼水与防水的区别
[联] waterproof 防水的, raincoat 雨衣, jacket 夹克, fabric 布料, rain 雨, outdoor 户外, label 标签
[记] 形容词。water-resistant ≠ waterproof；户外装备须看清等级与测试标准。

**wrinkle-free**
英/美 /ˈrɪŋkl friː/
adj. 免烫的；不易皱的
[义] Treated or made from fabric that does not wrinkle easily, or that looks smooth after washing without ironing. 经处理或面料本身不易皱，洗后不必熨也平整（wrinkle-free shirt 免烫衬衫， wrinkle-resistant trousers 抗皱长裤）。形容词。亦说 wrinkle-resistant。
[例] ① Wrinkle-free shirts save time on business trips. 免烫衬衫出差省时间。 ② The blend is marketed as wrinkle-free but still needs hanging straight. 混纺宣传免烫，但仍需挂直。
[搭] wrinkle-free shirt 免烫衬衫, wrinkle-free fabric 免烫面料, wrinkle-resistant 抗皱的, stay wrinkle-free 保持平整, pack wrinkle-free clothes 打包不易皱的衣物
[联] iron 熨, wrinkle 褶皱, crease 折痕, shirt 衬衫, travel 旅行, fabric 布料, business 商务
[记] 形容词。wrinkle-free 与 wrinkle-resistant 近义；纯 linen 很难 truly wrinkle-free。

**unbutton**
英/美 /ʌnˈbʌtn/
v. 解开纽扣
[义] To undo the buttons on a piece of clothing. 解开衣物上的纽扣（unbutton your collar 解开领扣， unbutton a shirt 解开衬衫）。与 button up 相对。
[形] unbuttoned / unbuttoned / unbuttoning
[例] ① He unbuttoned his collar when he got off the plane. 下飞机后他解开了领扣。 ② She unbuttoned her coat but kept it on indoors. 室内她解开大衣扣但没脱。
[搭] unbutton your collar 解开领扣, unbutton a shirt 解开衬衫, unbutton a coat 解开外套扣, leave unbuttoned 不扣, quickly unbutton 快速解扣
[联] button 纽扣, fasten 扣紧, collar 衣领, shirt 衬衫, coat 大衣, casual 休闲的, relax 放松
[记] 规则动词。unbuttoned 形容词可表「风纪松散、不拘礼」（an unbuttoned manner）。

**button-down**
英/美 /ˈbʌtn daʊn/
adj. 纽扣领的
[义] Describing a shirt collar with small buttons that fasten the points to the shirt. 领尖以小纽扣固定在衬衫上的领型（button-down shirt 纽扣领衬衫， button-down collar 纽扣领）。形容词。美式商务休闲经典款。
[例] ① A crisp button-down shirt works with or without a tie. 挺括纽扣领衬衫系不系领带都行。 ② Button-down collars look less formal than spread collars. 纽扣领比 spread 领看起来不那么正式。
[搭] button-down shirt 纽扣领衬衫, button-down collar 纽扣领, classic button-down 经典纽扣领, cotton button-down 棉质纽扣领, wear a button-down 穿纽扣领衬衫
[联] shirt 衬衫, collar 衣领, button 纽扣, formal 正式的, casual 休闲的, tie 领带, office 办公室
[记] 形容词。button-down 亦喻「保守、循规蹈矩」（a button-down company culture）。

**V-neck**
英/美 /ˈviː nek/
n. V 领
adj. V 领的
[义] A neckline in the shape of the letter V. 呈字母 V 形的领口（V-neck sweater V 领毛衣， deep V-neck 深 V 领）。可数/形容词。与 crew neck 相对。
[例] ① A V-neck sweater layers nicely over a collared shirt. V 领毛衣配衬衫领很有层次。 ② The dress had a modest V-neck for the ceremony. 典礼连衣裙是保守 V 领。
[搭] V-neck sweater V 领毛衣, deep V-neck 深 V 领, modest V-neck 保守 V 领, V-neck T-shirt V 领 T 恤, V-neckline V 形领口
[联] neckline 领口, crew neck 圆领, sweater 毛衣, T-shirt T 恤, collar 衣领, layer 分层穿着, casual 休闲的
[记] 可数/形容词。V-neck 与 scoop neck（U 形领）不同；深 V 在 formal 场合须留意。

**crew neck**
英/美 /kruː nek/
n. 圆领
adj. 圆领的
[义] A round neckline that sits close to the base of the neck, without a collar. 贴近颈根、无领的圆形领口（crew neck sweater 圆领毛衣， crew neck T-shirt 圆领 T 恤）。可数/形容词。与 V-neck 相对；crew neck 名源自 rowing crew 毛衣款式。
[例] ① A plain crew neck T-shirt goes with almost anything. 素色圆领 T 恤几乎百搭。 ② The sweatshirt has a classic crew neck, no hood. 这件运动卫衣是经典圆领，无帽。
[搭] crew neck sweater 圆领毛衣, crew neck T-shirt 圆领 T 恤, classic crew neck 经典圆领, plain crew neck 素色圆领, crew neck sweatshirt 圆领卫衣
[联] V-neck V 领, neckline 领口, sweater 毛衣, T-shirt T 恤, collar 衣领, casual 休闲的, sweatshirt 卫衣
[记] 可数/形容词。crew neck 比 V-neck 更中性、更休闲。

**inseam**
英/美 /ˈɪnsiːm/
n. 内缝长；裤内长
[义] The seam on the inside of a trouser leg, measured from the crotch to the hem; also the measurement itself. 裤腿内侧缝线，从裆部到裤脚的长度；亦指该尺寸（32-inch inseam 32 英寸内长， short inseam 短内长）。可数。买裤时关键尺寸。
[例] ① What's your inseam? These trousers come in 30, 32, and 34. 你内长多少？这条裤子有 30、32、34 码。 ② A tailor measured his inseam before hemming the pants. 改裤前裁缝量了内长。
[搭] 32-inch inseam 32 英寸内长, measure inseam 量内长, short inseam 短内长, inseam length 内长, inseam measurement 内长尺寸
[联] hem 裤边, size 尺码, tailor 裁缝, trousers 长裤, fit 合身, measurement 尺寸, alter 修改
[记] 可数。in- + seam；与 waist size（腰围）并列，网购裤子必看。

**hemline**
英/美 /ˈhemlaɪn/
n. 裙边线；下摆高度
[义] The level of the lower edge of a skirt or dress; the height at which a garment hangs. 裙或连衣裙下缘的高度；衣摆位置（short hemline 高裙摆， knee-length hemline 及膝下摆）。可数。时尚讨论「裙越来越短」即 hemline 变化。
[例] ① Hemlines rose sharply in the sixties. 六十年代裙摆大幅升高。 ② The dress has a modest hemline just below the knee. 这条裙子下摆保守，刚过膝。
[搭] short hemline 高裙摆, knee-length hemline 及膝下摆, raise the hemline 提高裙摆, modest hemline 保守下摆, hemline trend 裙摆趋势
[联] hem 衣边, skirt 裙子, dress 连衣裙, fashion 时尚, trend 趋势, knee 膝盖, length 长度
[记] 可数；a hemline。hem + line；the hemline index 曾为半严肃的经济时尚谈资。

**coordinate**
英/美 /kəʊˈɔːdɪneɪt/ 美 /koʊˈɔːrdɪneɪt/
v. 搭配；协调
n. 配套单品
[义] To match separate pieces of clothing so they look good together; a piece designed to match others in a set. 使分件衣物配在一起好看；套装中与之相配的单件（coordinate colours 配色， coordinates 配套单品）。动词/可数名词。
[形] coordinated / coordinated / coordinating
[例] ① She coordinated a navy bag with her shoes and belt. 她海军蓝包与鞋、腰带配色。 ② The jacket and trousers are sold as coordinates. 夹克与长裤作配套单品出售。
[搭] coordinate colours 配色, coordinate an outfit 搭配一套, matching coordinates 配套单品, well coordinated 协调良好, coordinate with 与……相配
[联] match 相配, outfit 一套搭配, colour 颜色, accessory 配饰, style 风格, clash 冲突, elegant 优雅的
[记] 动词重音常在第二音节；coordinates 名词指「配套衣」；coordinate 亦指「协调组织」，需看语境。

**clash**
英/美 /klæʃ/
v. 冲突；不协调
n. 冲突
[义] Of colours or patterns, to look ugly or too strong together. 颜色或图案配在一起难看或过于冲突（clashing colours 冲突色， patterns clash 图案打架）。动词/名词。与 match / coordinate 相对。
[例] ① Stripes and bold florals clash unless you know what you're doing. 条纹与大朵印花会打架，除非你很会搭。 ② Red and pink can clash—choose one as the main colour. 红粉可能冲突——选一种作主色。
[搭] clashing colours 冲突色, patterns clash 图案冲突, clash with 与……冲突, avoid a clash 避免冲突, colour clash 色彩冲突
[联] match 相配, coordinate 搭配, colour 颜色, pattern 图案, stripe 条纹, floral 花卉的, outfit 一套搭配
[记] 动词/名词。clash 亦指「时间冲突、肢体冲突」，时尚义为比喻延伸。

**clearance**
英/美 /ˈklɪərəns/
n. 清仓；清货
[义] A sale to get rid of old stock at reduced prices; items sold in such a sale. 以减价清库存的促销；此类商品（clearance sale 清仓大甩卖， on clearance 清仓价）。不可数/可数。季末、断码常见。
[例] ① She found a winter coat on clearance for half price. 她半价清仓淘到冬大衣。 ② Clearance racks are at the back of the store. 清仓架在店后面。
[搭] clearance sale 清仓甩卖, on clearance 清仓价, clearance rack 清仓架, end-of-season clearance 季末清仓, clearance price 清仓价
[联] sale 促销, discount 折扣, outlet 奥特莱斯, bargain 便宜货, retail 零售, brand 品牌, shop 商店
[记] 不可数/可数。clearance 亦指「许可、净空高度」，需看语境。

**sale**
英/美 /seɪl/
n. 促销；大减价
[义] A period when shops sell goods at lower prices than usual. 商店以低于平常价格售货的时段（end-of-season sale 季末大减价，on sale 打折）。可数/不可数。for sale 指「待售」，on sale 指「促销中」，美式易混。
[例] ① The boots I wanted finally went on sale. 我想要的靴子终于打折了。 ② Boxing Day sales draw huge crowds in the UK. 英国节礼日大减价人潮汹涌。
[搭] on sale 打折, end-of-season sale 季末大减价, sale price 促销价, winter sale 冬季大减价, sale section 促销区
[联] discount 折扣, clearance 清仓, bargain 便宜货, shop 商店, retail 零售, brand 品牌, buy 购买
[记] 可数/不可数。on sale 美式常指「在促销」；英式 on sale 有时指「上架出售」，须看语境。

**mothball**
英/美 /ˈmɒθbɔːl/
n. 樟脑丸
v. 封存
[义] A small ball of chemical used to keep moths away from stored clothes; to store clothing away for a long time. 防蛀的小球化学品；长期封存衣物（mothballs in the closet 衣柜里放樟脑丸， clothes in mothballs 封存起来的衣服）。可数/动词。
[例] ① Store wool coats with mothballs in sealed bags over summer. 夏天羊毛大衣密封袋存并放樟脑丸。 ② She mothballed her formal dresses when she moved abroad. 出国后她把礼服封存了。
[搭] mothballs in the closet 衣柜樟脑丸, store with mothballs 用樟脑丸存放, clothes in mothballs 封存货, mothball wool 封存羊毛衣物, smell of mothballs 樟脑丸味
[联] wool 羊毛, closet 衣柜, store 存放, winter 冬天, summer 夏天, garment 一件衣, protect 保护
[记] 可数/动词。mothball 动词亦指「搁置计划」；樟脑丸味难散，现多用密封袋替代。

**shoe rack**
英/美 /ʃuː ræk/
n. 鞋架
[义] A shelf or frame for storing shoes neatly. 整齐存放鞋子的架子或框架（entryway shoe rack 玄关鞋架， over-the-door shoe rack 门后鞋架）。可数。
[例] ① Leave muddy boots on the shoe rack by the door. 泥靴放在门边鞋架上。 ② The shoe rack holds about twelve pairs. 这鞋架大约放十二双。

[联] shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, closet 衣柜, entryway 玄关, organize 整理, storage 收纳, sneaker 运动鞋
[记] 可数；a shoe rack。shoe + rack 复合；玄关常用 open shoe rack 通风防臭。

**break in**
英/美 /breɪk ɪn/
v. 穿新鞋磨合
[义] To wear new shoes until they become comfortable and fit the shape of your feet. 穿新鞋直到舒适、贴合脚型（break in new boots 磨合新靴， broken-in shoes 穿软的鞋）。短语动词。名词 break-in 指「入室盗窃」，不同义。
[形] broke in / broken in / breaking in
[例] ① Give yourself a week to break in those leather shoes. 皮鞋给自己一周磨合。 ② Well broken-in sneakers feel like slippers. 穿软的旧运动鞋像拖鞋一样舒服。
[搭] break in new shoes 磨合新鞋, break in boots 磨合靴子, broken-in shoes 穿软的鞋, take time to break in 花时间磨合, painful to break in 磨合期疼
[联] shoe 鞋, boot 靴子, leather 皮革, comfortable 舒适的, blister 水泡, new 新的, fit 合身
[记] 短语动词。broken-in 形容词（a broken-in jacket 穿旧的夹克）亦指「旧而贴身」。

**headwear**
英/美 /ˈhedweə(r)/
n. 头饰（总称）
[义] Things worn on the head, such as hats, caps, and headbands. 戴在头上的物品总称，如帽、便帽、发带。不可数。与 footwear 对称。

[联] hat 帽子, cap 便帽, beanie 针织帽, headband 发带, scarf 围巾, footwear 鞋类, accessory 配饰

**snap**
英/美 /snæp/
n. 按扣；摁扣
v. 扣上
[义] A fastener with two parts that click together; used on clothing, bags, and jeans. 两部分咔嗒扣合的扣件，用于衣、包、牛仔裤（snap closure 按扣开合， metal snaps 金属按扣）。可数/动词。
[例] ① The jacket closes with snaps instead of buttons. 这件夹克用按扣不用纽扣。 ② One snap on the pocket came off. 口袋上有一颗按扣掉了。

**patch**
英/美 /pætʃ/
n. 补丁；贴布
v. 打补丁
[义] A piece of material sewn over a hole or used as decoration on clothes. 缝在洞上或作装饰的布片（denim patch 牛仔补丁， patch pocket 贴袋， iron-on patch 烫贴补丁）。可数/动词。
[例] ① He sewed a patch over the tear in his jeans. 他在牛仔裤破口上缝了块补丁。 ② Scout patches decorated the back of her jacket. 她夹克背后缀满童子军贴章。

[记] 可数/动词。patch up 短语表「修补关系」；patch pocket 指「贴袋式口袋」。

**distressed**
英/美 /dɪˈstrest/
adj. 做旧的；磨破的
[义] Deliberately made to look old or worn, especially denim with fading and rips. 故意做旧，尤指褪色、破洞的牛仔（distressed jeans 破洞牛仔裤， distressed leather 做旧皮）。形容词。distressed 亦指「焦虑的」，语境不同。
[例] ① Distressed jeans cost more for less fabric—it is a style choice. 破洞牛仔布料更少却更贵——是风格选择。 ② The jacket had distressed edges for a vintage look. 夹克边缘做旧，营造复古感。
[搭] distressed jeans 破洞牛仔裤, distressed denim 做旧牛仔, distressed leather 做旧皮, deliberately distressed 故意做旧, lightly distressed 轻度做旧
[联] jeans 牛仔裤, denim 丹宁, vintage 复古的, rip 破洞, fade 褪色, fashion 时尚, casual 休闲的
[记] 形容词。distressed 表「 distressed 经济/情绪」时与服装义同形。

**collection**
英/美 /kəˈlekʃn/
n. 系列；当季系列
[义] A range of new clothes designed and shown together by a fashion house for a season. 时装屋为一季设计并一同展示的新款系列（spring collection 春季系列， designer collection 设计师系列）。可数。fashion collection 为行业常用语。
[例] ① The spring collection featured linen and pastel colours. 春季系列主打亚麻与粉蜡色。 ② The whole collection won't reach stores until next month. 全系列下月才到店。
[搭] spring collection 春季系列, designer collection 设计师系列, new collection 新系列, collection launch 系列发布, fashion collection 时装系列
[联] designer 设计师, fashion 时尚, runway T 台, trend 趋势, season 季节, brand 品牌, boutique 精品店
[记] 可数；a collection。collection 亦指「收藏品、合集」，时尚义为其中一域。

**retail**
英/美 /ˈriːteɪl/
n. 零售
v. 零售
[义] The sale of goods to the public in shops and online, as opposed to wholesale. 相对批发，指向公众在门店与线上的销售（retail price 零售价， retail store 零售店）。不可数/动词。服装购买多经 retail 渠道。
[例] ① The dress retails for twice what I paid on sale. 这条裙子零售价是我促销价的两倍。 ② The brand sells online and through retail partners. 该品牌在线销售并通过零售伙伴售卖。
[搭] retail price 零售价, retail store 零售店, retail partner 零售伙伴, online retail 在线零售, retail fashion 零售时装
[联] brand 品牌, boutique 精品店, sale 促销, shop 商店, outlet 奥特莱斯, buy 购买, customer 顾客
[记] 不可数/动词。retail 重音在首音节；wholesale 批发为对义。

**discount**
英/美 /ˈdɪskaʊnt/
n. 折扣
v. 打折
[义] A reduction in the usual price of clothes or other goods. 衣物等商品在平常价格基础上的削减（student discount 学生折扣，discount code 折扣码）。可数/动词。
[例] ① She got a twenty percent discount with the store membership. 她凭会员打了八折。 ② End-of-season items are heavily discounted. 季末商品大幅打折。
[搭] student discount 学生折扣, discount code 折扣码, heavy discount 大幅折扣, discount price 折扣价, get a discount 获得折扣
[联] sale 促销, clearance 清仓, bargain 便宜货, retail 零售, price 价格, buy 购买, shop 商店
[记] 可数/动词。at a discount 亦指「打折扣、不受重视」（ideas sold at a discount）。

**cape**
英/美 /keɪp/
n. 斗篷；披肩外套
[义] A sleeveless outer garment that hangs from the shoulders and covers the back and arms. 无袖外披，从肩垂下，遮盖背部与手臂（wool cape 羊毛斗篷， rain cape 雨披）。可数。比 coat 更易穿脱；戏剧、礼服、雨天均可见。
[例] ① She threw a wool cape over her dress for the walk to the car. 走去上车时她在连衣裙外披了羊毛斗篷。 ② The superhero costume included a bright red cape. 超级英雄服配有鲜红斗篷。
[搭] wool cape 羊毛斗篷, rain cape 雨披, velvet cape 丝绒斗篷, throw on a cape 披上斗篷, dramatic cape 戏剧感斗篷
[联] coat 大衣, poncho 斗篷, shawl 披肩, cloak 斗篷（近义）, elegant 优雅的, winter 冬天, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a cape。cape 亦指「海角、披肩发型」，需看语境。

**peacoat**
英/美 /ˈpiːkəʊt/
n. 豌豆大衣；海军呢大衣
[义] A short thick wool coat with a wide collar, originally worn by sailors. 短款厚羊毛大衣，宽领，原为水手穿着（navy peacoat 海军蓝豌豆大衣， double-breasted peacoat 双排扣豌豆大衣）。可数。比 overcoat 短，比 blazer 厚暖。
[例] ① A classic navy peacoat never really goes out of style. 经典海军蓝豌豆大衣永不过时。 ② He buttoned his peacoat against the harbour wind. 他扣好豌豆大衣挡风。
[搭] navy peacoat 海军蓝豌豆大衣, double-breasted peacoat 双排扣豌豆大衣, wool peacoat 羊毛豌豆大衣, classic peacoat 经典豌豆大衣, wear a peacoat 穿豌豆大衣
[联] coat 大衣, jacket 夹克, wool 羊毛, collar 衣领, winter 冬天, formal 正式的, trench coat 风衣
[记] 可数；a peacoat。pea jacket 为近义旧称；名字来源说法不一（豌豆色、粗呢料等）。

**bodysuit**
英/美 /ˈbɒdisuːt/
n. 连体紧身衣；打底连体衣
[义] A one-piece garment that covers the torso and fastens under the crotch, worn as a top or base layer. 遮盖躯干、裆下扣合的单件衣，可作上装或打底（black bodysuit 黑色连体衣， lace bodysuit 蕾丝连体衣）。可数。比 tank top 更贴身、下摆不皱。
[例] ① She wore a black bodysuit under a sheer blouse. 她在透视女衫里穿黑色连体打底。 ② Bodysuits snap at the bottom—plan bathroom breaks accordingly. 连体衣裆下按扣——上厕所得提前想好。
[搭] black bodysuit 黑色连体衣, lace bodysuit 蕾丝连体衣, long-sleeve bodysuit 长袖连体衣, wear a bodysuit 穿连体衣, fitted bodysuit 贴身连体衣
[联] tank top 背心, leggings 打底裤, layer 分层穿着, tight 紧身的, dance 舞蹈, sport 运动, blouse 女式衬衫
[记] 可数；a bodysuit。body + suit；舞者、健身、时尚叠穿均常见。

**culottes**
英/美 /ˈkuːlɒts/ 美 /kuˈlɑːts/
n. 裙裤；阔腿短裤
[义] Women's trousers cut to look like a skirt, usually wide and knee-length or mid-calf. 裁成裙形的女式裤，通常宽裤腿、及膝或中小腿（denim culottes 牛仔裙裤， high-waisted culottes 高腰裙裤）。复数形；a pair of culottes。比 skirt 有裤裆，比 shorts 更长更宽。
[例] ① Culottes give you the look of a skirt with the comfort of trousers. 裙裤有裙感却像裤子一样方便。 ② She paired culottes with a fitted blazer for work. 上班她裙裤配修身西装外套。
[搭] a pair of culottes 一条裙裤, denim culottes 牛仔裙裤, high-waisted culottes 高腰裙裤, wide culottes 阔腿裙裤, wear culottes 穿裙裤
[联] skirt 裙子, trousers 长裤, shorts 短裤, wide-leg 阔腿, casual 休闲的, summer 夏天, office 办公室
[记] 复数；a pair of culottes。法语 culotte（内裤）演变，现指宽裤腿女裤。

**capri pants**
英/美 /ˈkæpri pænts/
n. 卡普里裤；九分裤
[义] Close-fitting trousers that end at the mid-calf or just below the knee. 贴身、在小腿中部或膝下结束的裤子（black capri pants 黑色九分裤， stretch capri pants 弹力卡普里裤）。复数形；a pair of capri pants。夏季与度假常见。
[例] ① Capri pants are perfect for warm weather when full trousers feel too hot. 天热时全长裤太热，卡普里裤刚好。 ② She wore white capri pants on the cruise. 游轮上她穿白色卡普里裤。
[搭] a pair of capri pants 一条卡普里裤, black capri pants 黑色九分裤, stretch capri pants 弹力卡普里裤, summer capri pants 夏季九分裤, wear capri pants 穿卡普里裤
[联] pants 裤子, shorts 短裤, summer 夏天, casual 休闲的, beach 海滩, fitted 合身的, calf 小腿
[记] 复数；a pair of capri pants。名字来自意大利卡普里岛；亦作 capris。

**slip**
英/美 /slɪp/
n. 衬裙；连衣裙内衬
v. 滑倒
[义] A thin dress or underskirt worn under a dress to prevent it clinging or showing through. 穿在连衣裙下的薄衬裙或内搭裙（silk slip 丝绸衬裙， full slip 全长衬裙）。可数。动词 slip 表滑倒，与名词不同义。
[例] ① A silk slip stops the dress from clinging in summer heat. 丝绸衬裙能避免夏天热时裙子贴身。 ② She packed a slip in case the wedding dress needed lining. 她备了衬裙，以防婚纱需要内衬。
[搭] silk slip 丝绸衬裙, full slip 全长衬裙, half slip 半身衬裙, wear a slip 穿衬裙, lace slip 蕾丝衬裙
[联] dress 连衣裙, underwear 内衣, silk 丝绸, lace 蕾丝, layer 分层穿着, wedding 婚礼, skirt 裙子
[记] 可数；a slip。slip 亦指「纸条、失误、滑一跤」，需看语境。

**corset**
英/美 /ˈkɔːsɪt/
n. 束腰；紧身胸衣
[义] A tight undergarment worn to shape the waist and torso; historically laced, now also fashion outerwear. 收紧腰躯的内衣；历史上系带，今亦作时尚外穿（lace corset 蕾丝束腰， waist-training corset 束腰训练）。可数。现代多作时尚或历史服饰，日常内衣已少见。
[例] ① The period drama required actors to wear corsets for authenticity. 时代剧为还原历史要求演员穿束腰。 ② Fashion corsets today are often worn visibly over shirts. 如今时尚束腰常外穿在衬衫上。
[搭] lace corset 蕾丝束腰, fashion corset 时尚束腰, wear a corset 穿束腰, tight corset 紧束腰, historical corset 历史束腰
[联] waist 腰, lace 蕾丝, tight 紧身的, vintage 复古的, dress 连衣裙, underwear 内衣, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a corset。corset 历史上与女性体态审美紧密相关；穿着须注意健康与舒适。

**cravat**
英/美 /krəˈvæt/
n. 领结围巾；领巾
[义] A wide strip of fabric worn around the neck, tucked into a shirt collar; a formal alternative to a tie. 绕颈宽布条，塞进衬衫领内；领带的正式替代（silk cravat 丝绸领巾， wedding cravat 婚礼领巾）。可数。英式正式/婚礼语境；美式较少日常穿。
[例] ① He wore a silk cravat with his morning suit. 晨礼服他配丝绸领巾。 ② The cravat was tucked neatly under the wing collar. 领巾整齐塞进翼领下。
[搭] silk cravat 丝绸领巾, wedding cravat 婚礼领巾, wear a cravat 戴领巾, formal cravat 正式领巾, tied cravat 系好的领巾
[联] tie 领带, bow tie 领结, collar 衣领, formal 正式的, wedding 婚礼, elegant 优雅的, ascot 阿斯科特领巾
[记] 可数；a cravat。源自克罗地亚士兵领巾；与 bow tie、ascot 同属颈饰家族。

**pocket square**
英/美 /ˈpɒkɪt skweə(r)/
n. 口袋方巾；西装胸袋巾
[义] A small square of cloth folded and worn in the breast pocket of a suit jacket for decoration. 折好插在西装胸袋作装饰的小方巾（silk pocket square 丝绸口袋巾， folded pocket square 折叠口袋巾）。可数。与 tie 配色是经典男装细节。
[例] ① A white pocket square adds polish without a tie. 白口袋巾不系领带也显利落。 ② He matched his pocket square to his tie, not his shirt. 口袋巾与领带同色，而非与衬衫。
[搭] silk pocket square 丝绸口袋巾, folded pocket square 折叠口袋巾, white pocket square 白口袋巾, match the pocket square 配口袋巾, breast pocket square 胸袋方巾
[联] tie 领带, suit 套装, blazer 西装外套, lapel 翻领, formal 正式的, elegant 优雅的, silk 丝绸
[记] 可数；a pocket square。fold 方式（presidential fold、puff fold）是穿搭细节话题。

**locket**
英/美 /ˈlɒkɪt/
n. 盒式吊坠；相片吊坠
[义] A small case on a chain worn around the neck, often holding a photo or keepsake. 链上佩戴的小盒吊坠，常装照片或纪念物（gold locket 金盒吊坠， heart-shaped locket 心形吊坠）。可数。比 pendant 更强调「可开合小盒」。
[例] ① The locket held a tiny photo of her grandmother. 吊坠盒里夹着祖母的小照片。 ② She inherited a gold locket on her eighteenth birthday. 十八岁生日她继承了金盒吊坠。
[搭] gold locket 金盒吊坠, heart-shaped locket 心形吊坠, open a locket 打开吊坠盒, chain and locket 链与吊坠, vintage locket 复古盒吊坠
[联] necklace 项链, pendant 吊坠, jewelry 珠宝, chain 链, gift 礼物, photo 照片, sentimental 有纪念意义的
[记] 可数；a locket。lock + -et 指「小盒」；情感价值常大于材质价值。

**scrunchie**
英/美 /ˈskrʌntʃi/
n. 发圈；束发圈
[义] A fabric-covered elastic band used to hold hair in a ponytail or bun. 包布弹性发圈，用于马尾或发髻（velvet scrunchie 丝绒发圈， silk scrunchie 丝绸发圈）。可数。1980–90 年代流行，近年复古回潮。
[例] ① She pulled her hair into a ponytail with a scrunchie. 她用发圈扎马尾。 ② Velvet scrunchies are back as a nineties fashion trend. 丝绒发圈作为九十年代风潮回归。
[搭] velvet scrunchie 丝绒发圈, silk scrunchie 丝绸发圈, hair scrunchie 发圈, wear a scrunchie 戴发圈, colourful scrunchies 彩色发圈
[联] hair clip 发夹, headband 发带, hair 头发, ponytail 马尾, accessory 配饰, vintage 复古的, casual 休闲的
[记] 可数；a scrunchie。scrunch 动词「揉皱」+ -ie；sleep in a scrunchie 可减少发丝压痕。

**hair clip**
英/美 /heər klɪp/
n. 发夹
[义] A small clip or barrette used to hold hair in place or as decoration. 固定或装饰头发的小夹子（bobby pin 一字夹美式， barrette 发夹， decorative hair clip 装饰发夹）。可数。与 headband、scrunchie 同属发饰。
[例] ① She used a hair clip to keep her bangs off her face. 她用发夹把刘海别到脸侧。 ② The flower hair clip matched her dress. 花朵发夹与裙子相配。

[联] headband 发带, scrunchie 发圈, barrette 发夹, accessory 配饰, hair 头发, pin 别针, elegant 优雅的
[记] 可数；a hair clip。bobby pin 美式极常说；barrette 常指有装饰的横夹。

**gingham**
英/美 /ˈɡɪŋəm/
n. 方格布；Gingham 格纹
[义] Cotton cloth with a checked pattern of usually two colours, often white plus one colour. 通常为两色（常为白加一色）方格图案的棉布（gingham dress 方格连衣裙， blue gingham 蓝白格）。不可数/可数。与 plaid、tartan 不同：gingham 为均匀小方格。
[例] ① A blue gingham shirt is a summer classic. 蓝白格衬衫是夏日经典。 ② The picnic table had a red gingham cloth. 野餐桌铺了红白格布。
[搭] blue gingham 蓝白格, gingham dress 方格连衣裙, gingham shirt 方格衬衫, gingham check 方格布, cotton gingham 棉方格布
[联] check 格子, plaid 格子, pattern 图案, cotton 棉, summer 夏天, casual 休闲的, picnic 野餐
[记] 不可数/可数。源自马来语 genggang（条纹）；美式常与 picnic、preppy 风格联想。

**herringbone**
英/美 /ˈherɪŋbəʊn/
n. 人字纹；鱼骨纹
[义] A pattern of columns of short parallel lines sloping in opposite directions, used in tweed and knitwear. 短平行线成列、方向相反斜排形成的图案，用于花呢与针织（herringbone coat 人字纹大衣， herringbone tweed 人字纹花呢）。不可数/形容词。
[例] ① A herringbone coat looks smart without being flashy. 人字纹大衣利落不张扬。 ② The scarf had a subtle herringbone knit. 围巾是低调人字纹针织。
[搭] herringbone coat 人字纹大衣, herringbone tweed 人字纹花呢, herringbone pattern 人字纹, herringbone knit 人字纹针织, subtle herringbone 低调人字纹
[联] tweed 粗花呢, pattern 图案, coat 大衣, wool 羊毛, formal 正式的, knit 编织, classic 经典的
[记] 不可数/形容词。herring（鲱鱼）+ bone，因纹路似鱼骨；男装外套与围巾常见。

**houndstooth**
英/美 /ˈhaʊndstuːθ/
n. 千鸟格；犬牙纹
[义] A pattern of broken checks with pointed shapes, usually black and white. 尖角碎格图案，通常为黑白（houndstooth blazer 千鸟格西装外套， classic houndstooth 经典千鸟格）。不可数/形容词。
[例] ① A houndstooth blazer adds pattern without loud colours. 千鸟格西装外套有纹样却不花哨。 ② The coat used a small-scale houndstooth for a quieter look. 大衣用小号千鸟格更低调。
[搭] houndstooth blazer 千鸟格西装外套, houndstooth pattern 千鸟格, classic houndstooth 经典千鸟格, black-and-white houndstooth 黑白千鸟格, small houndstooth 小千鸟格
[联] plaid 格子, check 格子, pattern 图案, blazer 西装外套, formal 正式的, classic 经典的, tweed 粗花呢
[记] 不可数/形容词。hound（猎犬）+ tooth；亦拼 hounds-tooth；英伦经典纹样。

**polka dot**
英/美 /ˈpɒkə dɒt/ 美 /ˈpoʊkə dɑːt/
n. 波点；圆点纹
[义] A pattern of filled circles of the same size on a contrasting background. 同色圆点均匀排在对比底色上的图案（polka dot dress 波点连衣裙， red polka dots 红波点）。可数/不可数。复古、甜美风格常见。
[例] ① She wore a polka dot dress to the garden party. 花园派对她穿波点连衣裙。 ② Polka dots look playful but can be dressed up with a blazer. 波点俏皮，配西装外套也能变正式。
[搭] polka dot dress 波点连衣裙, red polka dots 红波点, polka dot shirt 波点衬衫, classic polka dots 经典波点, small polka dots 小波点
[联] pattern 图案, dress 连衣裙, vintage 复古的, floral 花卉的, stripe 条纹, playful 俏皮的, retro 复古的
[记] 可数/不可数。a polka-dot dress 作形容词复合。polka 本为波兰舞蹈名，与圆点关联来自流行文化而非词源。

**sheer**
英/美 /ʃɪr/
adj. 透明的；薄纱的
[义] Thin and transparent enough to see through; used for fabrics and tights. 薄而透、可透视的；用于织物与丝袜（sheer blouse 薄纱女衫， sheer tights 透明丝袜）。形容词。比 translucent 更常用于服装零售。
[例] ① Wear a camisole under a sheer blouse for the office. 办公室穿薄纱女衫要内搭吊带。 ② Sheer tights dress up an outfit but tear easily. 透明丝袜抬气质但易破。

[联] lace 蕾丝, chiffon 雪纺, tights 连裤袜, layer 分层穿着, delicate 娇贵的, transparent 透明的, formal 正式的
[记] 形容词。sheer 亦指「纯粹的、陡峭的」（sheer luck）；服装标签 common。

**baggy**
英/美 /ˈbæɡi/
adj. 肥大的；松垮的
[义] Hanging loosely, with too much fabric; not fitted to the body. 松垮下垂、布料过多、不贴身（baggy jeans 肥大牛仔裤， baggy sweater 松垮毛衣）。形容词。与 tight、fitted 相对；hip-hop 与 oversize 风格常用。
[例] ① Baggy jeans were everywhere in the nineties. 九十年代肥大牛仔裤遍地。 ② The sweater looked baggy after she shrank in the wash—wrong size now. 毛衣洗缩水后显得松垮——现在尺码不对了。
[搭] baggy jeans 肥大牛仔裤, baggy sweater 松垮毛衣, baggy clothes 肥大衣服, too baggy 过于肥大, baggy fit 宽松版型
[联] loose 宽松的, tight 紧身的, oversized 大廓形的, fit 合身, jeans 牛仔裤, casual 休闲的, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 形容词。bag 袋 + -y → 像袋子一样松；baggy 与 oversized 近义但 baggy 更含「邋遢」感。

**oversized**
英/美 /ˈəʊvəsaɪzd/
adj. 大廓形的；宽松版型的
[义] Deliberately larger than the normal fit, as a fashion style. 故意大于正常合身度，作为一种时尚风格（oversized blazer 大廓形西装外套，oversized T-shirt 宽松 T 恤）。形容词。比 baggy 更强调「设计廓形」而非旧衣松垮。
[例] ① An oversized blazer balances slim trousers nicely. 大廓形西装外套配修身裤很平衡。 ② Oversized hoodies are comfortable for travel. 宽松连帽衫旅行穿着舒适。
[搭] oversized blazer 大廓形西装外套, oversized T-shirt 宽松 T 恤, oversized fit 大廓形, deliberately oversized 故意大廓形, oversized coat 宽松大衣
[联] baggy 肥大的, loose 宽松的, hoodie 连帽衫, blazer 西装外套, trend 趋势, casual 休闲的, layer 分层穿着
[记] 形容词。over- + sized；oversized 为近年街头与 luxury 共有风格词。

**tailored**
英/美 /ˈteɪlərd/
adj. 剪裁合体的；定制的
[义] Made to fit well or cut in a structured, neat way; often implies professional alteration or design. 合身或剪裁结构利落；常含专业修改或设计感（tailored suit 剪裁利落的西装， tailored fit 修身版型）。形容词。动词 tailor 的过去分词作形容词。
[例] ① A tailored blazer instantly makes jeans look smarter. 剪裁利落的西装外套能让牛仔裤立刻变精神。 ② The dress was tailored to her measurements. 连衣裙按她的尺寸改裁合身。
[搭] tailored suit 剪裁利落的西装, tailored fit 修身版型, tailored trousers 剪裁利落的长裤, well tailored 剪裁精良, tailored look 利落造型
[联] tailor 裁缝, fit 合身, suit 套装, blazer 西装外套, formal 正式的, elegant 优雅的, bespoke 定制的
[记] 形容词。tailored 与 baggy/oversized 风格对立；get a jacket tailored 指请人改衣。

**fitted**
英/美 /ˈfɪtɪd/
adj. 合身的；贴身的
[义] Cut to fit close to the body without excess fabric. 剪裁贴身、无多余布料（fitted dress 合身连衣裙， fitted sheet 为床品义不同）。形容词。比 tailored 更强调「贴曲线」；fitted vs slim vs regular 为常见尺码标注。
[例] ① The fitted dress showed her figure—she felt confident in it. 合身连衣裙显身材——她穿着很自信。 ② Fitted T-shirts are harder to layer than loose ones. 贴身 T 恤比宽松款难叠穿。
[搭] fitted dress 合身连衣裙, fitted T-shirt 贴身 T 恤, fitted silhouette 贴身轮廓, fitted cut 贴身剪裁, too fitted 过于贴身
[联] tight 紧身的, loose 宽松的, fit 合身, tailored 剪裁利落的, size 尺码, body 身体, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 形容词。fitted 床品指「四角固定的床单」；服装义为「贴身剪裁」。

**capsule wardrobe**
英/美 /ˈkæpsl ˈwɔːdrəʊb/
n. 胶囊衣橱
[义] A small collection of essential clothes that can be mixed and matched for many outfits. 少量可混搭出多种穿搭的核心衣物集合（build a capsule wardrobe 构建胶囊衣橱， capsule wardrobe basics 胶囊衣橱基础款）。可数/不可数。极简与可持续时尚常用概念。
[例] ① A capsule wardrobe saves space and decision fatigue. 胶囊衣橱省空间也省「穿什么」的纠结。 ② She rebuilt her capsule wardrobe around neutral colours. 她以中性色重建胶囊衣橱。
[搭] build a capsule wardrobe 构建胶囊衣橱, capsule wardrobe basics 胶囊衣橱基础款, minimalist capsule wardrobe 极简胶囊衣橱, seasonal capsule wardrobe 季节性胶囊衣橱, capsule wardrobe concept 胶囊衣橱理念
[联] wardrobe 衣橱, outfit 一套搭配, mix and match 混搭, minimal 极简的, sustainable 可持续的, basics 基础款, closet 衣柜
[记] 可数/不可数。capsule 本义「胶囊、密封舱」；喻「小而精、可组合」。

**custom-made**
英/美 /ˈkʌstəm meɪd/
adj. 定制的；量身定做的
[义] Made for a particular customer according to their measurements and choices. 按特定顾客尺寸与选择制作（custom-made suit 定制西装， custom-made shoes 定制鞋）。形容词。美式常用；英式 bespoke 更正式，custom-made 更通用。
[例] ① Custom-made shoes take weeks but fit like nothing off the rack. 定制鞋要等数周，但合脚胜过任何成衣。 ② The dress was custom-made for the awards ceremony. 颁奖典礼那条裙子是定制的。
[搭] custom-made suit 定制西装, custom-made shoes 定制鞋, custom-made dress 定制连衣裙, fully custom-made 全定制, order custom-made 订购定制
[联] bespoke 定制的, tailor 裁缝, fit 合身, measurement 尺寸, luxury 奢侈, formal 正式的, ready-to-wear 成衣的
[记] 形容词。custom + made；与 made-to-measure（MTM）近义，介于成衣与全定制之间。

**couture**
英/美 /kuːˈtʊər/
n. 高级时装；高级定制
[义] The design and manufacture of fashionable, high-quality clothes; designer fashion at the highest level. 时尚、高品质服装的设计与制作；最高级设计师时装（haute couture 高级定制时装， couture gown 高级定制礼服）。不可数/可数。法语借词，时尚业专用语。
[例] ① Haute couture dresses can take hundreds of hours of handwork. 高级定制礼服可需数百小时手工。 ② Couture shows set trends that filter down to retail. 高级定制秀引领潮流，再渗透到零售。
[源] 法语 *couture*（缝纫），源自拉丁语 *consutura*（缝合）→ 高级缝制工艺。
[搭] haute couture 高级定制, couture gown 高级定制礼服, couture fashion 高级时装, couture house 高级时装屋, fashion couture 时尚高级定制
[联] designer 设计师, bespoke 定制的, gown 礼服, elegant 优雅的, runway T 台, luxury 奢侈, fashion 时尚
[记] 不可数/可数。haute couture 为法国法律严格定义类别；勿与普通 designer 混称。

**waistband**
英/美 /ˈweɪstbænd/
n. 腰头；裤腰
[义] The part of a skirt, trousers, or shorts that fits around the waist, often elastic or with belt loops. 裙、裤、短裤绕腰部分，常为弹性或带裤耳（elastic waistband 松紧腰头， high waistband 高腰头）。可数。
[例] ① The waistband on these jeans is too tight after lunch. 这条牛仔裤腰头午饭後太紧。 ② Replace the elastic if the waistband won't stay up. 腰头老下滑就换松紧带。
[搭] elastic waistband 松紧腰头, high waistband 高腰头, waistband too tight 腰头太紧, adjust the waistband 调整腰头, fold-over waistband 翻折腰头
[联] waist 腰, belt 腰带, trousers 长裤, pants 裤子, elastic 松紧带, fit 合身, size 尺码
[记] 可数；a waistband。waist + band 复合；low-rise / mid-rise / high-rise 与腰头位置相关。

**belt loop**
英/美 /belt luːp/
n. 裤耳；腰带环
[义] A small loop of fabric on trousers or skirts through which a belt is passed. 裤或裙上供腰带穿过的布环（belt loops on jeans 牛仔裤裤耳， missing belt loop 掉裤耳）。可数；常用复数 belt loops。
[例] ① Clip your keys to a belt loop when you're hiking. 徒步时把钥匙扣在裤耳上。 ② One belt loop tore when he caught it on a fence. 他挂到栅栏上撕掉了一个裤耳。
[搭] belt loops on jeans 牛仔裤裤耳, missing belt loop 掉裤耳, belt loop clip 裤耳扣, extra belt loops 额外裤耳, torn belt loop 撕破的裤耳
[联] belt 腰带, buckle 扣, trousers 长裤, jeans 牛仔裤, pants 裤子, waist 腰, casual 休闲的
[记] 可数；常用复数 belt loops。belt loop 亦指钥匙扣等小配件的挂环。

**pleat**
英/美 /pliːt/
n. 褶；褶裥
v. 打褶
[义] A fold in cloth made by doubling fabric and stitching or pressing it in place. 布料叠折后缝住或压烫固定的褶（pleated skirt 百褶裙， knife pleat 刀褶）。可数/动词。与 gather、ruffle 不同：pleat 为规则折叠。
[例] ① Pleats give the skirt movement when you walk. 褶裥让裙子走路时有动感。 ② The tailor pleated the excess fabric at the waist. 裁缝把腰际多余布料打了褶。
[搭] pleated skirt 百褶裙, knife pleat 刀褶, box pleat 箱褶, pleat detail 褶饰细节, sharp pleats 清晰褶裥
[联] skirt 裙子, dress 连衣裙, fold 折叠, hem 衣边, tailor 裁缝, formal 正式的, vintage 复古的
[记] 可数/动词。a pleat；pleated 形容词（pleated trousers 褶裥长裤）。

**ruffle**
英/美 /ˈrʌfl/
n. 荷叶边；褶边
v. 弄皱
[义] A strip of fabric gathered or pleated along one edge, used as decoration on clothes. 一边收拢或打褶的布条饰边（ruffle sleeve 荷叶边袖， dress with ruffles 荷叶边连衣裙）。可数/动词。动词 ruffle 表「弄乱、惹恼」，与名词饰边义同形。
[例] ① Ruffles on the collar made the blouse look romantic. 领口荷叶边让女衫显浪漫。 ② Too many ruffles can look childish on formal wear. 正装荷叶边太多会显得幼稚。
[搭] ruffle sleeve 荷叶边袖, dress with ruffles 荷叶边连衣裙, ruffle trim 荷叶边饰, delicate ruffles 精致荷叶边, ruffle detail 荷叶边细节
[联] lace 蕾丝, trim 饰边, blouse 女式衬衫, dress 连衣裙, feminine 女性的, elegant 优雅的, vintage 复古的
[记] 可数/动词。a ruffle；ruffle feathers 喻「惹恼他人」，与衣饰义无关。

**quick-dry**
英/美 /kwɪk draɪ/
adj. 快干的
[义] Designed to dry quickly after washing or sweating, common in sportswear and travel clothes. 洗后或出汗后快速干燥，常见于运动装与旅行服（quick-dry fabric 快干面料， quick-dry T-shirt 快干 T 恤）。形容词。亦作 quick-drying。
[例] ① Pack quick-dry shirts if you'll hand-wash on the trip. 旅行手洗就带快干衬衫。 ② Quick-dry fabrics wick moisture away from the skin. 快干面料把汗从皮肤吸走。
[搭] quick-dry fabric 快干面料, quick-dry T-shirt 快干 T 恤, quick-drying 快干的, quick-dry technology 快干技术, lightweight quick-dry 轻量快干
[联] sportswear 运动装, travel 旅行, wash 洗, breathable 透气的, outdoor 户外, fabric 布料, gym 健身房
[记] 形容词。quick-dry 与 moisture-wicking 常同时标注于运动标签。

**stain-resistant**
英/美 /steɪn rɪˈzɪstənt/
adj. 防污的；抗污渍的
[义] Treated so that spills and dirt are less likely to leave permanent marks. 经处理使泼洒与污垢不易留下永久痕迹（stain-resistant fabric 防污面料， stain-resistant carpet 为家居义）。形容词。商务衬衫、儿童服、桌布常见标注。
[例] ① Stain-resistant finishes help white shirts survive coffee meetings. 防污处理帮白衬衫熬过咖啡会议。 ② The treatment is stain-resistant, not stain-proof—still wipe spills quickly. 处理是防污不是防渍——洒了还是要快擦。
[搭] stain-resistant fabric 防污面料, stain-resistant finish 防污涂层, stain-resistant shirt 防污衬衫, highly stain-resistant 高防污, stain-resistant treatment 防污处理
[联] stain 污渍, stain remover 去污剂, fabric 布料, shirt 衬衫, spill 洒出, clean 清洁, formal 正式的
[记] 形容词。-resistant 表「抗……」；stain-proof 声称更高，实际仍须及时清理。

**measurement**
英/美 /ˈmeʒərmənt/
n. 尺寸；量体数据
[义] The size of part of the body or garment, or the act of taking these sizes. 身体部位或衣物的尺寸，或量体这一动作（take measurements 量尺寸， chest measurement 胸围， custom measurements 定制尺寸）。可数/不可数。网购与定制关键信息。
[例] ① The tailor wrote down every measurement before cutting the suit. 裁西装前裁缝记下各项尺寸。 ② Check the size chart—your usual size may differ by brand. 看尺码表——常穿尺码各品牌可能不同。
[搭] take measurements 量尺寸, chest measurement 胸围, waist measurement 腰围, body measurements 身体尺寸, custom measurements 定制尺寸
[联] size 尺码, fit 合身, tailor 裁缝, inseam 内长, bust 胸围, alter 修改, bespoke 定制的
[记] 可数/不可数。measurements 复数常指「全套量体数据」；online sizing 仍建议对照具体 brand chart。

**rise**
英/美 /raɪz/
n. 裤腰高度；裆位
[义] The distance between the crotch seam and the waistband of trousers. 裤裆缝与腰头之间的距离（high-rise 高腰， low-rise 低腰， mid-rise 中腰）。可数。牛仔裤与裤装标签常见 low/mid/high rise。
[例] ① High-rise jeans sit above the natural waist. 高腰牛仔裤穿在自然腰线以上。 ② Low-rise pants fell out of fashion for office wear. 低腰裤在职场穿搭中过时了。
[搭] high-rise 高腰, low-rise 低腰, mid-rise 中腰, rise measurement 裆位尺寸, natural rise 自然腰位
[联] waist 腰, waistband 腰头, jeans 牛仔裤, pants 裤子, fit 合身, size 尺码, trend 趋势
[记] 可数。a rise。rise 动词「上升」与名词裤腰义同形；high-rise 亦指「高层建筑」。

**fingerless gloves**
英/美 /ˈfɪŋɡərləs ɡlʌvz/
n. 露指手套
[义] Gloves that cover the hand and wrist but leave the fingers exposed. 遮盖手掌与手腕但露出手指的手套（knit fingerless gloves 针织露指手套， leather fingerless gloves 皮露指手套）。复数形；a pair of fingerless gloves。
[例] ① Fingerless gloves keep your hands warm while you type. 露指手套保暖还能打字。 ② Cyclists wear fingerless gloves for grip and padding. 骑行者戴露指手套增抓握与缓冲。
[搭] a pair of fingerless gloves 一副露指手套, knit fingerless gloves 针织露指手套, leather fingerless gloves 皮露指手套, wear fingerless gloves 戴露指手套, winter fingerless gloves 冬用露指手套
[联] glove 手套, mitten 连指手套, hand 手, finger 手指, knit 编织, winter 冬天, sport 运动
[记] 复数；a pair of fingerless gloves。fingerless + gloves 复合；驾驶、骑行、办公场景均常见。

**onesie**
英/美 /ˈwʌnzi/
n. 连体衣；婴儿连体服
[义] A one-piece garment covering the torso and legs, often with snaps at the crotch; popular for babies and as casual adult loungewear. 遮盖躯干与腿的单件衣，裆部常按扣；婴儿与成人居家均用（baby onesie 婴儿连体衣， adult onesie 成人连体家居服）。可数。
[例] ① The baby wore a cotton onesie all day. 宝宝整天穿棉质连体衣。 ② Novelty onesies became a quirky gift trend. 搞怪连体家居服成另类礼物潮流。
[搭] baby onesie 婴儿连体衣, adult onesie 成人连体家居服, cotton onesie 棉质连体衣, snap onesie 按扣连体衣, wear a onesie 穿连体衣
[联] pajamas 睡衣, romper 连身短裤, bodysuit 连体紧身衣, baby 婴儿, loungewear 居家服, casual 休闲的, snap 按扣
[记] 可数；an onesie。名字来自 Onesie 商标泛化；成人 onesie 多指「整身连体家居服」。

**platform**
英/美 /ˈplætfɔːrm/
n. 厚底；平台鞋
[义] A shoe sole that is significantly thicker than normal, especially under the toe and heel. 明显厚于常规的鞋底，尤指趾跟下均加厚（platform shoes 厚底鞋， platform sandals 厚底凉鞋）。可数。与 wedge 不同：platform 底常整块抬高。
[例] ① Platform sandals add height without a steep heel angle. 厚底凉鞋增高但脚跟角度不陡。 ② Seventies platform shoes are back in some collections. 七十年代厚底鞋在部分系列回归。
[搭] platform shoes 厚底鞋, platform sandals 厚底凉鞋, platform heels 厚底高跟, high platform 高厚底, wear platforms 穿厚底鞋
[联] heel 鞋跟, wedge 坡跟, shoe 鞋, sandal 凉鞋, stiletto 细高跟, vintage 复古的, tall 高的
[记] 可数；a platform。platform 亦指「平台、月台」，需看语境。

**scoop neck**
英/美 /skuːp nek/
n. 圆领；U 形领
adj. U 形领的
[义] A wide, rounded neckline that dips low on the chest, like a U shape. 胸前低宽的圆弧形领口，呈 U 形（scoop neck T-shirt U 领 T 恤， deep scoop neck 深 U 领）。可数/形容词。比 crew neck 更低、更露锁骨。
[例] ① A scoop neck T-shirt is more flattering than a crew neck for some body types. 对某些体型 U 领 T 恤比圆领更衬人。 ② The dress had a modest scoop neck, not too deep. 连衣裙是适度 U 领，不深。
[搭] scoop neck T-shirt U 领 T 恤, deep scoop neck 深 U 领, modest scoop neck 适度 U 领, scoop neckline U 形领口, scoop neck dress U 领连衣裙
[联] V-neck V 领, crew neck 圆领, neckline 领口, T-shirt T 恤, dress 连衣裙, casual 休闲的, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数/形容词。scoop 本义「勺」；与 boat neck（一字领）弧度不同。

**boat neck**
英/美 /bəʊt nek/
n. 一字领；船形领
adj. 一字领的
[义] A wide neckline that runs horizontally across the collarbone, high and straight. 横贯锁骨、较高且平直的宽领口（boat neck sweater 一字领毛衣， boat neck top 一字领上衣）。可数/形容词。法式优雅风格常见。
[例] ① A boat neck sweater shows off a necklace nicely. 一字领毛衣很适合配项链。 ② The boat neck is wider than a crew neck but doesn't dip low. 一字领比圆领宽但不低胸。
[搭] boat neck sweater 一字领毛衣, boat neck top 一字领上衣, boat neckline 一字领口, classic boat neck 经典一字领, wide boat neck 宽一字领
[联] neckline 领口, crew neck 圆领, scoop neck U 形领, sweater 毛衣, elegant 优雅的, necklace 项链, French 法国的
[记] 可数/形容词。boat 船 + neck，因横向线条似船舷；亦作 bateau neck（法语船形）。

**galoshes**
英/美 /ɡəˈlɒʃɪz/
n. 套鞋；橡胶套靴
[义] Waterproof overshoes worn over regular shoes to protect them from rain and mud. 套在常穿鞋外的防水套鞋，防雨防泥（rubber galoshes 橡胶套鞋， pair of galoshes 一副套鞋）。复数形；a pair of galoshes。美式；与 wellies（整靴）不同，galoshes 套在鞋外。
[例] ① He pulled galoshes over his dress shoes for the walk in the slush. 雪泥地里他往皮鞋外套了套鞋。 ② Kids' galoshes snap over sneakers in the rain. 雨天儿童套鞋扣在运动鞋外。
[搭] a pair of galoshes 一副套鞋, rubber galoshes 橡胶套鞋, wear galoshes 套套鞋, snap-on galoshes 扣式套鞋, children's galoshes 儿童套鞋
[联] wellies 雨靴, shoe 鞋, rain 雨, waterproof 防水的, boot 靴子, rubber 橡胶, wet 湿的
[记] 复数；a pair of galoshes。亦拼 goloshes；现代城市较少见，雨雪乡村仍有用。

**ascot**
英/美 /ˈæskɒt/ 美 /ˈæskɑːt/
n. 阿斯科特领巾；宽领带
[义] A wide necktie with pointed ends, worn looped over and secured with a pin; less formal than a cravat, more decorative than a long tie. 两端尖、绕颈后以别针固定的宽领带；比 cravat 休闲、比长领带更装饰（silk ascot 丝绸阿斯科特领巾， day ascot 日间阿斯科特）。可数。赛马会 Ascot 得名；美式复古与婚礼语境可见。
[例] ① He wore a patterned ascot with his blazer at the garden party. 花园派对他穿图案阿斯科特领巾配西装外套。 ② The ascot was held in place with a small gold pin. 阿斯科特领巾用一枚小金别针固定。
[搭] silk ascot 丝绸阿斯科特领巾, wear an ascot 戴阿斯科特领巾, patterned ascot 图案阿斯科特, day ascot 日间阿斯科特, ascot pin 阿斯科特别针
[联] cravat 领巾, tie 领带, bow tie 领结, blazer 西装外套, formal 正式的, pin 别针, elegant 优雅的
[记] 可数；an ascot。与 cravat、bow tie 同属颈饰；Ascot 亦为英国赛马地名。

**peplum**
英/美 /ˈpepluːm/
n. 腰际饰边
[义] A short flared strip of fabric attached at the waist of a jacket, blouse, or dress. 缝在夹克、女衫或连衣裙腰际的短展边（peplum top  peplum 上衣， peplum jacket  peplum 夹克）。可数。强调腰线、臀部的装饰廓形，2010 年代时尚高频词。
[例] ① The peplum jacket nips the waist and flares over the hips.  peplum 夹克收腰并在臀上展开。 ② Peplums can add shape to straight figures.  peplum 能为直筒身材增加曲线感。
[搭] peplum top  peplum 上衣, peplum jacket  peplum 夹克, peplum dress  peplum 连衣裙, waist peplum 腰际 peplum, structured peplum  structured  peplum
[联] waist 腰, blouse 女式衬衫, dress 连衣裙, ruffle 荷叶边, tailored 剪裁利落的, feminine 女性的, trend 趋势
[记] 可数；a peplum。源自希腊语 peplos（女式长袍）；现代 peplum 为腰下短摆。

**smock**
英/美 /smɒk/
n. 罩衫； smock 连衣裙
[义] A loose protective or decorative garment worn over clothes; also a dress with smocking at the chest. 宽松罩衣，可罩在其他衣服外；亦指胸前 smock 褶饰的连衣裙（artist's smock 画家罩衫， smock dress  smock 裙）。可数。
[例] ① She wore a cotton smock over her clothes while painting. 画画时她在衣服外罩棉质罩衫。 ② Smock dresses are comfortable and forgiving in summer.  smock 裙夏天穿着舒适、不挑身材。
[搭] smock dress  smock 裙, artist's smock 画家罩衫, cotton smock 棉质罩衫, smocking detail smock 褶饰, wear a smock 穿罩衫
[联] apron 围裙, dress 连衣裙, loose 宽松的, cotton 棉, casual 休闲的, summer 夏天, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a smock。smocking 指胸前平行装饰褶；儿童 smock 亦常见。

**monochrome**
英/美 /ˈmɒnəkrəʊm/
adj. 单色的；同色系搭配的
n. 单色
[义] Using only one colour or shades of one colour in an outfit. 整套穿搭仅用一种颜色或同一色系深浅（monochrome look 同色系造型， all-black monochrome 全黑同色系）。形容词/名词。比 neutral 更强调「单一色相」。
[例] ① She went for a monochrome outfit in shades of grey. 她穿深浅灰同色系一套。 ② Monochrome dressing looks polished with minimal effort. 同色系穿搭省力却显精致。
[搭] monochrome look 同色系造型, monochrome outfit 同色系穿搭, all-black monochrome 全黑同色系, monochrome style 单色风格, dress in monochrome 穿同色系
[联] neutral 中性色, colour 颜色, outfit 一套搭配, match 相配, elegant 优雅的, minimalist 极简的, black 黑色
[记] 形容词/名词。mono-（单）+ chrome（色）；摄影、设计、时尚共用。

**neutral**
英/美 /ˈnjuːtrəl/
adj. 中性色的；百搭的
[义] Referring to colours such as black, white, grey, beige, and navy that combine easily with other colours. 指黑、白、灰、米色、海军蓝等易与其他色搭配的中性色（neutral colours 中性色， neutral palette 中性色调）。形容词。胶囊衣橱常围绕 neutral 构建。
[例] ① Build your wardrobe around neutral basics first. 先把衣橱建立在百搭中性基础款上。 ② Neutral shoes go with almost any outfit. 中性色鞋子几乎配任何穿搭。
[搭] neutral colours 中性色, neutral palette 中性色调, neutral basics 中性基础款, neutral tones 中性色调, stick to neutrals 坚持中性色
[联] monochrome 单色, beige 米色, black 黑色, capsule wardrobe 胶囊衣橱, match 相配, outfit 一套搭配, casual 休闲的
[记] 形容词。neutral 亦指「中立的、中性的（政治/性别）」，服装义为色彩语境。

**department store**
英/美 /dɪˈpɑːtmənt stɔː(r)/
n. 百货商店
[义] A large shop divided into departments selling clothes, cosmetics, home goods, and more. 分部门销售服装、化妆品、家居等的大商店（department store sale 百货促销， shop at a department store 逛百货）。可数。成衣、配饰一站式购买的传统渠道。
[例] ① She bought the coat on sale at a downtown department store. 她在市中心百货促销时买了这件大衣。 ② Department stores often have personal shopping services. 百货常有个人导购服务。
[搭] department store sale 百货促销, shop at a department store 逛百货, downtown department store 市中心百货, department store brand 百货品牌, department store fashion 百货时装
[联] retail 零售, boutique 精品店, sale 促销, brand 品牌, shop 商店, buy 购买, fashion 时尚
[记] 可数；a department store。department + store 复合；与 mall 内 anchor store 常重叠。

**windcheater**
英 /ˈwɪntʃiːtə(r)/
n. 防风夹克（英式）
[义] A lightweight windproof jacket; British equivalent of windbreaker or anorak. 轻便防风夹克；英式说法，近 windbreaker 或 anorak（packable windcheater 可折叠防风夹克）。可数。主要在英式英语使用。
[例] ① Pack a windcheater—the coast can be windy even in summer. 带件防风夹克——海边夏天也可能风大。 ② In the UK, windcheater is a common word for a light jacket. 在英国，windcheater 常指轻便夹克。
[搭] packable windcheater 可折叠防风夹克, lightweight windcheater 轻便防风夹克, hooded windcheater 连帽防风夹克, wear a windcheater 穿防风夹克, nylon windcheater 尼龙防风夹克
[联] windbreaker 防风夹克, anorak 连帽夹克, jacket 夹克, waterproof 防水的, outdoor 户外, British 英国的, layer 分层穿着
[记] 可数；a windcheater。wind + cheater 复合；美式更常说 windbreaker。

**canvas**
英/美 /ˈkænvəs/
n. 帆布
[义] Strong plain-woven fabric used for shoes, bags, and casual summer clothing. 用于鞋、包与夏季休闲装的结实平纹织物（canvas sneakers 帆布鞋， canvas tote 帆布托特包）。不可数/可数。
[例] ① Canvas sneakers are cheap, breathable, and easy to wash. 帆布鞋便宜、透气、好洗。 ② The bag is heavy-duty canvas, not thin cotton. 这包是厚帆布，不是薄棉。
[搭] canvas sneakers 帆布鞋, canvas bag 帆布包, canvas fabric 帆布, heavy-duty canvas 厚帆布, cotton canvas 棉帆布
[联] cotton 棉, sneaker 运动鞋, tote bag 托特包, durable 耐用的, casual 休闲的, shoe 鞋, fabric 布料
[记] 不可数/可数。canvas 亦指「油画布、画布」，需看语境。

**thimble**
英/美 /ˈθɪmbl/
n. 顶针
[义] A small metal or plastic cap worn on the finger to push a needle when sewing. 缝纫时套在指上、用以顶针的小金属或塑料帽（silver thimble 银顶针， sewing thimble 缝纫顶针）。可数。手工缝纫传统工具。
[例] ① She kept a thimble in her sewing kit for mending clothes. 缝纫盒里她备着顶针补衣服。 ② Without a thimble, pushing the needle hurts your finger. 没有顶针，顶针会弄疼手指。
[搭] sewing thimble 缝纫顶针, silver thimble 银顶针, use a thimble 用顶针, thimble in a sewing kit 缝纫盒里的顶针, metal thimble 金属顶针
[联] needle 针, sew 缝, thread 线, stitch 针脚, sewing 缝纫, finger 手指, repair 修补
[记] 可数；a thimble。thumb + -le 指「护拇指/指端」的小物。

**halter**
英/美 /ˈhɔːltə(r)/
n. 挂脖带； halter 领
[义] A strap that passes behind the neck to support a top or dress, leaving shoulders and back bare. 绕颈后支撑上装或连衣裙的带条，露肩露背（halter top 挂脖上衣， halter dress 挂脖连衣裙）。可数。夏季、礼服、海滩常见。
[例] ① She wore a halter dress to the beach wedding. 海滩婚礼她穿挂脖连衣裙。 ② Halter tops need sunscreen on the back and shoulders. 挂脖上衣要在背和肩涂防晒。
[搭] halter top 挂脖上衣, halter dress 挂脖连衣裙, halter neckline 挂脖领, tie halter 系挂脖带, backless halter 露背挂脖
[联] strap 带条, strapless 无肩带的, dress 连衣裙, summer 夏天, beach 海滩, neckline 领口, elegant 优雅的
[记] 可数；a halter。halter 亦指「（牵牲畜的）笼头」，需看语境。

**strapless**
英/美 /ˈstræpləs/
adj. 无肩带的
[义] Without straps over the shoulders, held up by fit or internal structure. 无肩带，靠合身或内置结构固定（strapless dress 无肩带连衣裙， strapless bra 无肩带文胸）。形容词。
[例] ① Strapless dresses need a good fit or they'll slide down. 无肩带连衣裙要合身，否则会滑下来。 ② She wore a strapless gown with a light wrap for the ceremony. 典礼她穿无肩带礼服，外搭薄披肩。
[搭] strapless dress 无肩带连衣裙, strapless bra 无肩带文胸, strapless gown 无肩带礼服, strapless top 无肩带上衣, strapless fit 无肩带合身度
[联] halter 挂脖, bra 文胸, dress 连衣裙, gown 礼服, wedding 婚礼, formal 正式的, neckline 领口
[记] 形容词。strap + -less；常需 strapless bra 或防滑贴辅助。

**sleepwear**
英/美 /ˈsliːpweə(r)/
n. 睡衣（总称）
[义] Clothes worn in bed, such as pyjamas, nightgowns, and sleep shirts. 床上穿着的衣物总称，如睡衣、睡袍、睡衫。不可数。比 nightwear 更口语；与 loungewear 相比更强调「睡觉穿」。
[例] ① The brand sells sleepwear in soft cotton and modal. 该品牌卖棉与 modal 的柔软睡衣。 ② Pack comfortable sleepwear for the hotel stay. 住酒店备舒适睡衣。
[搭] cotton sleepwear 棉质睡衣, comfortable sleepwear 舒适睡衣, sleepwear set 睡衣套装, women's sleepwear 女式睡衣, soft sleepwear 柔软睡衣
[联] pajamas 睡衣, nightgown 睡袍, loungewear 居家服, cotton 棉, bedroom 卧室, comfortable 舒适的, layer 分层穿着
[记] 不可数。sleep + wear 复合；与 lingerie（内衣）不同场。

**workwear**
英/美 /ˈwɜːkweə(r)/
n. 工服；工作装（总称）
[义] Clothes designed for manual or industrial work, emphasizing durability and safety. 为体力劳动或工业工作设计、强调耐用与安全的服装总称（workwear jacket 工服夹克， durable workwear 耐用工服）。不可数。与 office wear 相对；亦指「工装风」时尚。
[例] ① The factory issued flame-resistant workwear to all staff. 工厂向全员发放阻燃工服。 ② Workwear brands have crossed into street fashion. 工服品牌已跨入街头时尚。
[搭] durable workwear 耐用工服, workwear jacket 工服夹克, industrial workwear 工业工服, workwear brand 工服品牌, flame-resistant workwear 阻燃工服
[联] uniform 制服, apron 围裙, overalls 工装连体裤, durable 耐用的, safety 安全, casual 休闲的, jacket 夹克

**bust**
英/美 /bʌst/
n. 胸围
[义] The measurement around the fullest part of the chest, used for sizing bras, dresses, and tops. 胸部最丰满处的围度，用于 bra、连衣裙与上装尺码（bust measurement 胸围， 34-inch bust 34 英寸胸围）。可数。与 waist、hip 并称三围。
[例] ① The size chart lists bust, waist, and hip measurements. 尺码表列出胸、腰、臀围。 ② The dress fits at the bust but is loose at the waist. 裙子胸围合适但腰松。
[搭] bust measurement 胸围, bust size 胸围尺码, full bust 满胸围, bust point 胸点, measure the bust 量胸围
[联] waist 腰, measurement 尺寸, size 尺码, bra 文胸, dress 连衣裙, fit 合身, tailor 裁缝
[记] 可数。bust 亦指「半身像、破产」（go bust），需看语境。

**dart**
英/美 /dɑːrt/
n. 省道；缝褶
[义] A tapered fold sewn into fabric to shape a garment to the body, especially at the bust or waist. 缝入布料、使衣身贴体的锥形褶，尤在胸或腰（bust dart 胸省， waist dart 腰省）。可数。女装剪裁基础手法。
[例] ① Darts at the waist give the dress a fitted silhouette. 腰省让连衣裙轮廓合身。 ② The tailor added a dart so the jacket closed smoothly. 裁缝加了省道，夹克才能平顺扣合。

[联] seam 接缝, tailor 裁缝, fit 合身, waist 腰, bust 胸围, sew 缝, dress 连衣裙
[记] 可数；a dart。dart 亦指「飞镖、猛冲」，服装义为专业术语。

**yoke**
英/美 /joʊk/
n. 育克；连肩片
[义] A shaped piece of fabric across the shoulders or hips, often in shirts and skirts, for fit and decoration. 横跨肩或臀的塑形布片，常见于衬衫与半裙（shoulder yoke 肩育克， back yoke 后育克）。可数。
[例] ① The shirt has a double yoke on the back for durability. 衬衫背后双育克更耐穿。 ② A curved yoke on the skirt sits smoothly over the hips. 半裙弧形育克平顺贴臀。
[搭] shoulder yoke 肩育克, back yoke 后育克, double yoke 双育克, curved yoke 弧形育克, yoke seam 育克缝
[联] seam 接缝, shirt 衬衫, skirt 裙子, shoulder 肩, fit 合身, sew 缝, denim 丹宁
[记] 可数；a yoke。yoke 亦指「牛轭、束缚」，同形异义。

**buttonhole**
英/美 /ˈbʌtnhəʊl/
n. 扣眼；钮孔
[义] A slit in fabric with reinforced edges, through which a button passes. 布料上缝边加固的开口，供纽扣穿过（buttonhole stitch 扣眼缝， lapel buttonhole 驳头扣眼）。可数。
[例] ① One buttonhole on the cuff was fraying. 袖口一个扣眼磨毛了。 ② The tailor hand-stitched the buttonholes on the suit. 裁缝手工缝了西装扣眼。
[搭] buttonhole stitch 扣眼缝, lapel buttonhole 驳头扣眼, sew a buttonhole 缝扣眼, frayed buttonhole 磨毛扣眼, hand-stitched buttonhole 手工扣眼
[联] button 纽扣, sew 缝, stitch 针脚, cuff 袖口, collar 衣领, shirt 衬衫, tailor 裁缝
[记] 可数；a buttonhole。buttonhole 动词俚语指「强留人说话」。

**rivet**
英/美 /ˈrɪvɪt/
n. 铆钉
v. 铆接
[义] A metal pin used to fasten heavy denim, especially on jeans pockets and stress points. 固定厚牛仔布的金属钉，尤见于牛仔裤口袋与受力点（copper rivets 铜铆钉， riveted pockets 铆钉口袋）。可数。Levi's 标志性细节。
[例] ① Copper rivets reinforce the corners of jean pockets. 铜铆钉加固牛仔裤袋角。 ② Vintage denim often shows rust on the rivets.  vintage 牛仔铆钉常带锈迹。
[搭] copper rivets 铜铆钉, riveted pockets 铆钉口袋, metal rivet 金属铆钉, jeans rivets 牛仔裤铆钉, reinforce with rivets 用铆钉加固
[联] jeans 牛仔裤, denim 丹宁, pocket 口袋, metal 金属, vintage 复古的, durable 耐用的, stitch 针脚
[记] 可数；a rivet。rivet 动词表「牢牢吸引（注意力）」，与铆钉义同形。

**stonewash**
英/美 /ˈstəʊnwɒʃ/
n. 石洗；石磨洗
v. 石洗处理
[义] A finishing process that washes denim with stones or enzymes to soften fabric and create a faded look. 用石或酶洗涤牛仔以软化面料、做出褪色效果的后整理工艺（stonewash jeans 石洗牛仔裤， heavy stonewash 重石洗）。不可数/动词。
[例] ① Stonewash gives jeans a broken-in feel from day one. 石洗让牛仔裤一买来就似穿旧。 ② Light stonewash looks more casual than raw denim. 浅石洗比原色牛仔更休闲。
[搭] stonewash jeans 石洗牛仔裤, heavy stonewash 重石洗, light stonewash 浅石洗, stonewash finish 石洗效果, stonewashed denim 石洗牛仔
[联] denim 丹宁, jeans 牛仔裤, fade 褪色, wash 洗, vintage 复古的, distressed 做旧的, casual 休闲的
[记] 不可数/动词。亦作 stone wash；与 acid wash（酸洗）效果不同。

**pre-shrunk**
英/美 /priː ʃrʌŋk/
adj. 预缩的；防缩的
[义] Treated during manufacturing so the fabric will shrink less in the first wash. 出厂前处理，使首次洗涤后缩水更少（pre-shrunk cotton 预缩棉， pre-shrunk T-shirt 预缩 T 恤）。形容词。
[例] ① Pre-shrunk cotton T-shirts hold their size after washing. 预缩棉 T 恤洗后尺码稳定。 ② The label says pre-shrunk, but cold wash is still safer. 标签写预缩，冷水洗仍更稳妥。
