[例] ① Organic cotton costs more but reduces chemical use on farms. 有机棉更贵但减少农场化学品使用。 ② Check for certification— not every "organic" label is equal. 看认证——并非每个「有机」标签同等。
[搭] organic cotton T-shirt 有机棉 T 恤, GOTS-certified organic cotton GOTS 认证有机棉, 100% organic cotton 百分百有机棉, organic cotton fabric 有机棉面料, buy organic cotton 购买有机棉
[联] cotton 棉, sustainable 可持续的, fabric 布料, ethical 伦理的, T-shirt T 恤, environment 环境, brand 品牌
[记] 不可数。organic 亦指「有机的（食品）」；GOTS 为纺织业常见认证。

**fast fashion**
英/美 /fɑːst ˈfæʃn/
n. 快时尚
[义] The business model of copying runway trends quickly and selling cheap clothes in high volume. 快速复制 T 台趋势、大量廉价售衣的商业模式（fast fashion brand 快时尚品牌， fast fashion industry 快时尚产业）。不可数。与 sustainable fashion 常对照讨论。
[例] ① Fast fashion encourages buying more and wearing less. 快时尚鼓励多买少穿。 ② She quit shopping at fast fashion chains for sustainability reasons. 出于可持续考虑她不再逛快时尚连锁店。
[搭] fast fashion brand 快时尚品牌, fast fashion industry 快时尚产业, fast fashion cycle 快时尚周期, avoid fast fashion 避开快时尚, fast fashion retailer 快时尚零售商
[联] sustainable 可持续的, trend 趋势, brand 品牌, retail 零售, cheap 便宜的, environment 环境, second-hand 二手的
[记] 不可数。fast + fashion 复合；H&M、Zara 等为典型代表。

**ethical**
英/美 /ˈeθɪkl/
adj. 合乎伦理的；负责任的
[义] Relating to fair treatment of workers and responsible sourcing in clothing production. 与服装生产中公平对待劳工、负责任采购相关（ethical fashion 伦理时尚， ethical brand 伦理品牌）。形容词。常与 sustainable 并用但侧重劳工与人权。
[例] ① Ethical brands publish where their clothes are made. 伦理品牌会公开产地。 ② "Ethical" without proof can be marketing— look for audits. 「伦理」若无佐证可能是营销——看审计报告。
[搭] ethical fashion 伦理时尚, ethical brand 伦理品牌, ethical sourcing 伦理采购, shop ethically 伦理购物, ethical production 伦理生产
[联] sustainable 可持续的, fair trade 公平贸易, brand 品牌, worker 工人, environment 环境, organic 有机的, transparent 透明的
[记] 形容词。ethical 亦指「道德的（一般）」；fashion 语境特指供应链伦理。

**lint roller**
英/美 /lɪnt ˈrəʊlə(r)/
n. 粘毛滚筒
[义] A roll of sticky paper on a handle used to remove lint and hair from clothing. 手柄上粘性纸卷，用于去除衣物毛絮与毛发（pack a lint roller 带粘毛滚筒， sticky lint roller 粘性粘毛滚筒）。可数。
[例] ① Keep a lint roller by the door for black coats and pets. 门口放粘毛滚筒，黑大衣和宠物毛都能对付。 ② Lint rollers run out fast if you have a shedding cat. 猫掉毛多的话粘毛滚筒用得很快。
[搭] pack a lint roller 带粘毛滚筒, sticky lint roller 粘性粘毛滚筒, use a lint roller 用粘毛滚筒, lint roller sheet 粘毛纸, travel lint roller 旅行粘毛滚筒
[联] lint 毛絮, clean 清洁, coat 大衣, dark clothes 深色衣物, pet 宠物, travel 旅行, neat 整洁的
[记] 可数；a lint roller。refill 替换芯常见；深色衣、面试前必备。

**fabric shaver**
英/美 /ˈfæbrɪk ˈʃeɪvə(r)/
n. 去球器
[义] A small electric device that removes pills from sweaters and other knitwear. 去除毛衣等针织品表面毛球的小型电动装置（electric fabric shaver 电动去球器， de-pill sweaters 去球）。可数。亦说 lint shaver、defuzzer。
[例] ① A fabric shaver revived his old wool sweater. 去球器让他旧羊毛衫焕然一新。 ② Run the fabric shaver lightly— don't press too hard. 去球器轻贴表面——别按太猛。
[搭] electric fabric shaver 电动去球器, use a fabric shaver 用去球器, fabric shaver for sweaters 毛衣去球器, gently shave fabric 轻去球, de-pill with a fabric shaver 用去球器去球
[联] pill 起球, sweater 毛衣, knit 编织, wool 羊毛, lint 毛絮, clean 清洁, repair 修补
[记] 可数；a fabric shaver。与 lint roller 互补：roller 粘毛，shaver 去球。

**shoe tree**
英/美 /ʃuː triː/
n. 鞋撑
[义] A shaped insert placed inside shoes to maintain their form and absorb moisture when not worn. 不穿的鞋内放置的定型内撑，保持鞋形并吸湿（cedar shoe trees 雪松鞋撑， wooden shoe trees 木制鞋撑）。可数；常用复数 shoe trees。
[例] ① Cedar shoe trees help leather dress shoes keep their shape. 雪松鞋撑帮皮鞋保持鞋形。 ② Insert shoe trees as soon as you take off the shoes. 脱鞋后尽快放入鞋撑。
[搭] cedar shoe trees 雪松鞋撑, wooden shoe trees 木制鞋撑, insert shoe trees 放入鞋撑, pair of shoe trees 一副鞋撑, leather shoe trees 皮鞋鞋撑
[联] shoe 鞋, leather 皮革, boot 靴子, care 保养, formal 正式的, polish 鞋油, closet 衣柜
[记] 可数；常用复数 shoe trees。shoe + tree 复合；与 shoe horn（鞋拔）同为保养配件。

**laundry hamper**
英/美 /ˈlɒndri hæmpə(r)/
n. 脏衣篮
[义] A tall basket with a lid for collecting dirty clothes before washing. 带盖高篮，洗涤前收集脏衣（bedroom laundry hamper 卧室脏衣篮， wicker laundry hamper 藤编脏衣篮）。可数。比 laundry basket 更常指「带盖、立式」。
[例] ① Sort lights and darks into two laundry hampers. 浅色深色分两个脏衣篮。 ② The hamper was overflowing again by midweek. 到周中脏衣篮又满出来了。
[搭] bedroom laundry hamper 卧室脏衣篮, wicker laundry hamper 藤编脏衣篮, laundry hamper with lid 带盖脏衣篮, empty the laundry hamper 倒空脏衣篮, overflowing laundry hamper 满溢脏衣篮
[联] laundry basket 洗衣篮, laundry 洗衣, clothes 衣服, wash 洗, bedroom 卧室, sort 分类, detergent 洗涤剂
[记] 可数；a laundry hamper。hamper 亦指「阻碍、礼品篮」，需看语境。

**kaftan**
英/美 /ˈkæftæn/ 美 /ˈkɑːftæn/
n. 长袍；卡夫坦
[义] A long loose robe or dress, often with wide sleeves, worn in Middle Eastern and resort fashion. 长而宽松的长袍或连衣裙，常宽袖，中东与度假时尚均见（silk kaftan 丝绸长袍， beach kaftan 海滩长袍）。可数。亦拼 caftan。
[例] ① She wore a printed kaftan over her swimsuit at the pool. 泳池边她在泳衣外罩印花长袍。 ② Silk kaftans work as elegant cover-ups in hot climates. 热天丝绸长袍是雅致外搭。
[搭] silk kaftan 丝绸长袍, beach kaftan 海滩长袍, printed kaftan 印花长袍, wear a kaftan 穿长袍, loose kaftan 宽松长袍
[联] robe 长袍, dress 连衣裙, beach 海滩, silk 丝绸, loose 宽松的, summer 夏天, wrap 裹
[记] 可数；a kaftan。土耳其/波斯传统服饰进入国际度假与休闲穿搭。

**steam**
英/美 /stiːm/
v. 蒸汽熨；蒸汽去皱
n. 蒸汽
[义] To remove wrinkles from fabric using hot steam, with or without pressing on an iron. 用热蒸汽去除织物褶皱，可配合或不配合熨斗压平（steam a dress 蒸汽熨连衣裙， steam instead of iron 用蒸汽代替熨烫）。动词。与 iron 相比更省接触、适合娇贵面料。
[形] steamed / steamed / steaming
[例] ① She steamed the silk blouse rather than risk scorching it with a hot iron. 她蒸汽熨丝绸女衫，免得热熨斗烫焦。 ② Steam the curtains while they hang to save taking them down. 窗帘挂着蒸汽熨，省得拆下来。
[搭] steam a dress 蒸汽熨连衣裙, steam instead of iron 用蒸汽代替熨烫, steam out wrinkles 蒸汽去皱, handheld steamer 手持蒸汽熨, steam on a hanger 挂烫
[联] iron 熨, wrinkle 褶皱, garment steamer 挂烫机, delicate 娇贵的, silk 丝绸, crease 折痕, laundry 洗衣
[记] 动词。steam 亦指「蒸（食物）」「冒蒸汽」；garment care 语境看宾语。

**press**
英/美 /pres/
v. 压烫；熨平
n. 压烫
[义] To smooth clothes with a hot iron, especially with firm downward pressure on creases. 用热熨斗压平衣物，尤指对折痕施力下压（press a seam 压烫接缝， give it a quick press 快速熨一下）。动词/可数名词。dry cleaner 常说 press and clean。
[形] pressed / pressed / pressing
[例] ① Press the collar flat before you pack the shirt. 装箱前把领子熨平。 ② The dry cleaner will clean and press your suit by Friday. 干洗店周五前会把你的西装洗净压烫好。
[搭] press a seam 压烫接缝, give it a quick press 快速熨一下, clean and press 洗净压烫, well-pressed 熨得笔挺的, press the crease 压烫折痕
[联] iron 熨, crease 折痕, wrinkle 褶皱, collar 衣领, suit 套装, dry clean 干洗, tailor 裁缝
[记] 动词/名词。press 亦指「按压、新闻界」；clothes press 指压烫或熨衣板。

**garment steamer**
英/美 /ˈɡɑːmənt ˈstiːmə(r)/
n. 挂烫机；蒸汽熨衣机
[义] A device that produces steam to remove wrinkles from hanging clothes. 产生蒸汽、用于挂烫去皱的装置（handheld garment steamer 手持挂烫机， travel garment steamer 旅行挂烫机）。可数。比传统熨斗更适合连衣裙、窗帘等大面积垂坠衣物。
[例] ① A garment steamer is gentler on delicate fabrics than a hot iron. 挂烫机比热熨斗对娇贵面料更温和。 ② She keeps a steamer in the closet for last-minute touch-ups. 她在衣柜旁放挂烫机，临出门前快速处理。
[搭] handheld garment steamer 手持挂烫机, travel garment steamer 旅行挂烫机, use a garment steamer 用挂烫机, steam with a garment steamer 挂烫机蒸汽熨, fill the steamer 给挂烫机加水
[联] steam 蒸汽熨, iron 熨斗, wrinkle 褶皱, delicate 娇贵的, dress 连衣裙, travel 旅行, laundry 洗衣
[记] 可数；a garment steamer。亦简称 steamer；与 steam iron（蒸汽熨斗）不同，通常不直接接触面料。

**starch**
英/美 /stɑːtʃ/
n. 浆粉；上浆
v. 上浆
[义] A substance used to stiffen cotton shirts and collars; to treat fabric with it. 使棉衬衫与领子挺括的上浆剂；给织物上浆（spray starch 喷雾浆料， heavily starched collar 浆得很硬的领子）。不可数/动词。正装衬衫、制服常见。
[例] ① He likes his dress shirts lightly starched for a crisp look. 他喜欢正装衬衫轻上浆，显得利落。 ② Too much starch can make collars crack over time. 浆太厚领子久了会裂。
[搭] spray starch 喷雾浆料, heavily starched collar 硬浆领子, starch a shirt 给衬衫上浆, light starch 轻浆, starch and press 上浆并压烫
[联] iron 熨, press 压烫, collar 衣领, crisp 挺括的, cotton 棉, formal 正式的, laundry 洗衣
[记] 不可数/动词。starch 亦指食物「淀粉」；laundry starch 专指衣用浆粉。

**relaxed fit**
英/美 /rɪˈlækst fɪt/
n. 宽松版型
adj. 宽松合身的
[义] A clothing cut with extra room for comfort, looser than regular fit. 比常规版型更宽松、余量更大的剪裁（relaxed fit jeans 宽松牛仔裤， relaxed fit shirt 宽松衬衫）。名词/形容词。介于 regular fit 与 oversized 之间。
[例] ① Relaxed fit is ideal if you want comfort without looking baggy. 想要舒适又不想显垮就选宽松版型。 ② He switched to relaxed fit chinos for weekend wear. 周末他改穿宽松版型卡其裤。
[搭] relaxed fit jeans 宽松牛仔裤, relaxed fit shirt 宽松衬衫, relaxed fit cut 宽松剪裁, true relaxed fit 标准宽松, comfortable relaxed fit 舒适宽松
[联] regular fit 常规版型, slim fit 修身版型, loose 宽松的, oversized 超大号的, comfortable 舒适的, jeans 牛仔裤, casual 休闲的
[记] 名词/形容词。各品牌 relaxed 定义不一；与 loose fit 常近义可互换。

**shirring**
英/美 /ˈʃɜːrɪŋ/
n. 平行抽褶；松紧抽褶
[义] Parallel rows of gathering held by elastic thread, creating stretchy fullness. 用弹力线固定多行平行抽褶，形成有弹性蓬松感的技法（shirring at the waist 腰部松紧抽褶， shirred dress 抽褶连衣裙）。不可数。与 smocking（装饰密褶）不同，shirring 靠松紧线。
[例] ① Shirring at the back lets the dress stretch without a zipper. 背部松紧抽褶让连衣裙无拉链也能伸缩。 ② Shirred tops are popular in summer resort wear. 松紧抽褶上装在夏日度假装里很常见。
[搭] shirring at the waist 腰部松紧抽褶, shirred dress 抽褶连衣裙, elastic shirring 弹力抽褶, shirred bodice 抽褶上身, decorative shirring 装饰抽褶
[联] gather 抽褶, smocking 缩褶绣, elastic 有弹力的, dress 连衣裙, stretch 弹力, sew 缝, summer 夏天
[记] 不可数。shirr 为动词；shirred 形容词（a shirred top）。

**dupatta**
英/美 /dʊˈpʌtə/
n. 杜帕塔；长围巾
[义] A long scarf worn with South Asian dress, draped over the head or shoulders. 南亚传统服饰搭配的长围巾，可披头或肩（silk dupatta 丝绸杜帕塔， match the dupatta to the suit 围巾与套装配色）。可数。salwar kameez、saree 语境常见。
[例] ① She pinned the dupatta over one shoulder for the ceremony. 典礼上她把杜帕塔别在一侧肩上。 ② The dupatta should complement the colour of the kameez. 杜帕塔颜色应衬托长衫。
[搭] silk dupatta 丝绸杜帕塔, drape a dupatta 披杜帕塔, match the dupatta 搭配杜帕塔, embroidered dupatta 刺绣杜帕塔, pin the dupatta 别住杜帕塔
[联] scarf 围巾, saree 纱丽, traditional 传统的, silk 丝绸, drape 披, wedding 婚礼, South Asian 南亚的
[记] 可数；a dupatta。印地语借词；与 shawl（披肩）功能近但形制与 draping 方式不同。

**bodycon**
英/美 /ˈbɒdikɒn/ 美 /ˈbɑːdikɑːn/
adj. 紧身的；包身的
n. 包身裙
[义] Extremely tight-fitting, hugging the body (from "body conscious"). 极贴身、勾勒身型（body conscious 缩写， bodycon dress 包身连衣裙， bodycon skirt 包身半裙）。形容词/名词。派对、夜店、红毯常见。
[例] ① The bodycon dress showed every curve— she wore shapewear underneath. 包身裙勾勒每道曲线——她里面穿了塑身衣。 ② Bodycon works best in stretchy fabric with some give. 包身款最好用有弹性的面料。
[搭] bodycon dress 包身连衣裙, bodycon skirt 包身半裙, tight bodycon 紧身包身, stretch bodycon 弹力包身, wear bodycon 穿包身款
[联] fitted 合身的, tight 紧身的, dress 连衣裙, stretch 弹力, party 派对, shapewear 塑身衣, silhouette 轮廓
[记] 形容词/名词。body + con(scious) 缩写；与 A-line、shift 等宽松廓形相对。

**A-line**
英/美 /ˈeɪ laɪn/
adj. A 字形的；A 字版的
n. A 字裙
[义] Wider at the hem than at the waist, forming an A shape. 下摆比腰宽，呈 A 字形（A-line skirt A 字半裙， A-line dress A 字连衣裙）。形容词/名词。多数身型穿着效果友好，经典廓形。
[例] ① An A-line skirt balances broad shoulders nicely. A 字半裙能平衡宽肩。 ② The A-line coat flares gently from the waist. A 字大衣从腰部轻轻展开。
[搭] A-line skirt A 字半裙, A-line dress A 字连衣裙, A-line coat A 字大衣, classic A-line 经典 A 字, flattering A-line 衬身型的 A 字
[联] skirt 半裙, dress 连衣裙, silhouette 轮廓, waist 腰, flare 喇叭形, fitted 合身的, classic 经典的
[记] 形容词/名词。A + line 复合；与 pencil skirt（铅笔裙）相对。

**wrap dress**
英/美 /ræp dres/
n. 裹身连衣裙
[义] A dress that wraps around the body and ties at the waist, adjustable for fit. 绕身并在腰部系带的连衣裙，可调合身度（silk wrap dress 丝绸裹身裙， classic wrap dress 经典裹身裙）。可数。Diane von Furstenberg 使此廓形闻名。
[例] ① A wrap dress adjusts easily if you're between sizes. 裹身裙卡在码间也好调节。 ② She chose a floral wrap dress for the garden party. 花园派对她选了印花裹身裙。
[搭] silk wrap dress 丝绸裹身裙, classic wrap dress 经典裹身裙, wear a wrap dress 穿裹身裙, tie a wrap dress 系裹身裙, flattering wrap dress 衬身型的裹身裙
[联] dress 连衣裙, tie 系, waist 腰, silk 丝绸, flattering 衬身型的, casual 休闲的, office 办公室
[记] 可数；a wrap dress。wrap 动词「裹、缠绕」；与 wrap top（裹身上衣）同系。

**shift dress**
英/美 /ʃɪft dres/
n. 直筒连衣裙；箱型连衣裙
[义] A simple straight dress that hangs from the shoulders with little waist definition. 从肩部直垂、几乎不收腰的简单连衣裙（classic shift dress 经典直筒裙， sleeveless shift 无袖直筒裙）。可数。1960 年代 Mod 风格代表廓形。
[例] ① A shift dress is easy to wear and hides the waist. 直筒裙好穿，也不强调腰线。 ② She paired a sleeveless shift with low heels for the gallery opening. 画廊开幕她无袖直筒裙配低跟鞋。
[搭] classic shift dress 经典直筒裙, sleeveless shift 无袖直筒裙, wear a shift dress 穿直筒裙, mod shift dress Mod 风直筒裙, simple shift 简约直筒裙
[联] dress 连衣裙, straight 直筒的, sleeveless 无袖的, vintage 复古的, casual 休闲的, A-line A 字形的, silhouette 轮廓
[记] 可数；a shift dress。shift 亦指「轮班、移动」；dress 语境专指此廓形。

**outseam**
英/美 /ˈaʊtsiːm/
n. 外缝；侧缝
[义] The seam running down the outside of a trouser leg or along the outer edge of a garment. 沿裤腿外侧或衣身外缘的缝线（outseam length 外缝长度， selvedge outseam 赤耳外缝）。可数。与 inseam（内缝/裆缝）相对；高档牛仔常露 selvedge outseam。
[例] ① Check the outseam if the trousers twist when you walk. 走路裤管拧的话看外缝是否正。 ② Selvedge jeans show a coloured stripe on the outseam when cuffed. 赤耳牛仔裤卷起时外缝露色条。
[搭] outseam length 外缝长度, selvedge outseam 赤耳外缝, straight outseam 笔直外缝, measure the outseam 量外缝, outer seam 外缝
[联] inseam 内缝, seam 接缝, selvedge 布边, jeans 牛仔裤, trousers 长裤, tailor 裁缝, measure 测量
[记] 可数；an outseam。out + seam 复合；裤装版型与改衣常用术语。

**armhole**
英/美 /ˈɑːmhəʊl/ 美 /ˈɑːrmhoʊl/
n. 袖窿；袖洞
[义] The opening in a garment where the sleeve is attached or where the arm passes through. 衣服上与袖子连接或手臂穿过的开口（tight armhole 紧袖窿， deep armhole 深袖窿）。可数。版型影响活动度与外观。
[例] ① The armholes were too tight— she couldn't lift her arms comfortably. 袖窿太紧——她抬手都不舒服。 ② A deeper armhole gives a relaxed, drop-shoulder look. 深袖窿呈休闲落肩感。
[搭] tight armhole 紧袖窿, deep armhole 深袖窿, armhole seam 袖窿缝, adjust the armhole 改袖窿, sleeveless armhole 无袖袖窿
[联] sleeve 袖子, seam 接缝, fit 合身, shoulder 肩, sew 缝, bodice 上身, comfortable 舒适的
[记] 可数；an armhole。arm + hole 复合；tailor 改衣常动 armhole 而非只改袖长。

**welt pocket**
英/美 /welt ˈpɒkɪt/ 美 /welt ˈpɑːkɪt/
n. 嵌线袋；西装袋
[义] A pocket with a narrow fabric strip (welt) around the opening, common on jackets and trousers. 袋口有窄布条（嵌线）镶边的口袋，常见于夹克与西裤（jacket welt pocket 西装嵌线袋， besom welt pocket 平嵌线袋）。可数。比 patch pocket 更正式。
[例] ① Welt pockets on a blazer should lie flat, not gap open. 西装夹克嵌线袋应贴平，别张着嘴。 ② Tailors sew welt pockets by hand on bespoke suits. 定制西装的嵌线袋常手工缝制。
[搭] jacket welt pocket 西装嵌线袋, besom welt pocket 平嵌线袋, welt pocket opening 嵌线袋口, flap welt pocket 带盖嵌线袋, breast welt pocket 胸嵌线袋
[联] pocket 口袋, patch pocket 贴袋, blazer 西装夹克, suit 套装, tailor 裁缝, formal 正式的, sew 缝
[记] 可数；a welt pocket。welt 亦指鞋边沿条；口袋语境指嵌线条。

**slash pocket**
英/美 /slæʃ ˈpɒkɪt/ 美 /slæʃ ˈpɑːkɪt/
n. 斜插袋；开线袋
[义] A pocket cut into the garment with a diagonal or vertical slit opening, often on jackets or trousers. 斜向或纵向开缝切入衣身的口袋，常见于夹克与西裤（side slash pocket 侧斜插袋， slash pockets on trousers 西裤斜插袋）。可数。与 patch pocket（外贴）相对，更利落。
[例] ① Slash pockets on dress trousers keep the line clean. 正装西裤斜插袋保持线条干净。 ② The coat has slash pockets instead of bulky patch ones. 大衣用斜插袋而非臃肿的贴袋。
[搭] side slash pocket 侧斜插袋, slash pockets on trousers 西裤斜插袋, diagonal slash pocket 斜向插袋, clean slash pocket 利落斜插袋, jacket slash pocket 夹克斜插袋
[联] pocket 口袋, patch pocket 贴袋, welt pocket 嵌线袋, trousers 西裤, jacket 夹克, tailored 剪裁利落的, formal 正式的
[记] 可数；a slash pocket。slash 动词「划开」；亦说 slit pocket。

**hook and eye**
英/美 /hʊk ənd aɪ/
n. 钩眼扣
[义] A small metal hook and loop used to fasten garments at the neck, waist, or top of a zip. 金属小钩与环，用于领口、腰际或拉链顶端固定（hook-and-eye closure 钩眼扣开合， sew on a hook and eye 缝钩眼扣）。可数；常用复数 hooks and eyes。
[例] ① The dress zips up and has a hook and eye at the top. 连衣裙拉链拉上，顶端还有钩眼扣。 ② Replace loose hooks and eyes before they fail at an event. 活动前把松了的钩眼扣换掉。
[搭] hook-and-eye closure 钩眼扣开合, sew on a hook and eye 缝钩眼扣, metal hook and eye 金属钩眼扣, top hook and eye 顶部钩眼扣, replace hooks and eyes 更换钩眼扣
[联] fasten 扣紧, zip 拉链, button 纽扣, bra 文胸, waist 腰, sew 缝, closure 开合
[记] 可数；常用复数 hooks and eyes。比纽扣隐蔽，常见于裙腰、bra、礼服。

**elastic waist**
英/美 /ɪˈlæstɪk weɪst/
n. 松紧腰
[义] A waistband containing elastic thread for stretch and comfort. 内含弹力线的腰头，可伸缩、穿着舒适（elastic waist trousers 松紧腰长裤， full elastic waist 全松紧腰）。可数/不可数。休闲裤、睡裤、部分半裙常见。
[例] ① Elastic waist trousers are forgiving after a big meal. 松紧腰裤子吃撑了也不勒。 ② The skirt has a hidden elastic waist under the fold. 半裙褶下藏有松紧腰。
[搭] elastic waist trousers 松紧腰长裤, full elastic waist 全松紧腰, elastic waistband 松紧腰带, comfortable elastic waist 舒适松紧腰, hidden elastic waist 隐藏松紧腰
[联] waist 腰, stretch 弹力, comfortable 舒适的, trousers 长裤, casual 休闲的, drawstring 抽绳, pajamas 睡衣
[记] 可数/不可数。elastic + waist 复合；与 paper-bag waist（纸袋腰）同为休闲腰头类型。

**wide-leg**
英/美 /waɪd leɡ/
adj. 宽腿的；阔腿的
[义] Trousers that are wide from hip to hem. 从臀至裤脚均较宽的长裤（wide-leg trousers 阔腿裤， wide-leg jeans 阔腿牛仔裤）。形容词。1970 年代与近年复古均流行。
[例] ① Wide-leg trousers balance a fitted top nicely. 阔腿裤配修身上装很平衡。 ② She hemmed the wide-leg jeans to just skim the floor. 她把阔腿牛仔裤改到刚好扫地。
[搭] wide-leg trousers 阔腿裤, wide-leg jeans 阔腿牛仔裤, wide-leg cut 阔腿剪裁, high-waisted wide-leg 高腰阔腿, classic wide-leg 经典阔腿
[联] flare 喇叭裤, trousers 长裤, jeans 牛仔裤, high-waisted 高腰的, vintage 复古的, silhouette 轮廓, hem 改边
[记] 形容词。wide-leg 与 palazzo pants（极宽飘逸）有重叠；tailor 改裤长时注意扫脚感。

**bootcut**
英/美 /ˈbuːtkʌt/
adj. 微喇的；靴形的
n. 微喇裤
[义] Trousers that are slim through the thigh and flare slightly below the knee to fit over boots. 大腿处较 slim、膝下微展以套靴的裤型（bootcut jeans 微喇牛仔裤， classic bootcut 经典微喇）。形容词/名词。2000 年代主流，现仍常见。
[例] ① Bootcut jeans balance hips without the drama of full flare. 微喇牛仔裤平衡臀线，又不像大喇叭那么夸张。 ② The bootcut opening should cover the heel of your boot. 微喇裤脚应盖住靴跟。
[搭] bootcut jeans 微喇牛仔裤, classic bootcut 经典微喇, bootcut fit 微喇版型, low-rise bootcut 低腰微喇, slim bootcut 修身微喇
[联] flare 喇叭裤, jeans 牛仔裤, slim 修身的, vintage 复古的, fit 合身, hem 改边, casual 休闲的
[记] 形容词/名词。boot + cut 复合；与 straight leg、skinny 同属牛仔裤型谱系。

**flare**
英/美 /fleə(r)/
n. 喇叭裤；喇叭形
v. 展开；外扩
[义] Trousers that widen sharply from the knee downward; the widened shape itself. 自膝下明显外扩的裤型；或指外扩廓形本身（flare jeans 喇叭牛仔裤， bell-bottom flare 大喇叭）。可数/动词。1970 年代 bell-bottom 为极端版。
[例] ① Flare jeans are back on runways with a high waist. 喇叭牛仔裤以高腰造型重回 T 台。 ② The skirt has a gentle flare from the hips. 半裙从臀部轻轻展开。
[搭] flare jeans 喇叭牛仔裤, bell-bottom flare 大喇叭, gentle flare 轻微展开, wide flare 大喇叭, flare from the knee 膝下外扩
[联] bootcut 微喇裤, wide-leg 阔腿裤, jeans 牛仔裤, vintage 复古的, silhouette 轮廓, hem 改边, 1970s 七十年代
[记] 可数/动词。flare 亦指「闪光、突发」；pants 语境专指喇叭廓形。

**skinny**
英/美 /ˈskɪni/
adj. 紧身的；窄脚的
[义] Very tight-fitting, especially trousers that hug the leg from hip to ankle. 极贴身，尤指从臀至踝均紧贴的裤型（skinny jeans 紧身牛仔裤， skinny fit 紧身版型）。形容词。2010 年代极流行，现与 wide-leg 交替流行。
[例] ① Skinny jeans are hard to pull on once they're wet from rain. 紧身牛仔裤淋湿后很难套进去。 ② She prefers skinny trousers with ankle boots. 她爱紧身裤配踝靴。
[搭] skinny jeans 紧身牛仔裤, skinny fit 紧身版型, super skinny 超紧身, skinny leg 窄裤腿, black skinny jeans 黑色紧身牛仔
[联] slim fit 修身版型, tight 紧身的, jeans 牛仔裤, leggings 打底裤, stretch 弹力, fit 合身, ankle 踝
[记] 形容词。skinny 口语亦指「极瘦的（人）」；fashion 语境多指裤型。

**mom jeans**
英/美 /mɒm dʒiːnz/ 美 /mɑːm dʒiːnz/
n. Mom 牛仔裤；Mom 裤
[义] High-waisted, relaxed-fit jeans associated with 1980s–90s style, often tapered at the ankle. 高腰、偏宽松、常踝部略收的牛仔裤，关联八九十年代风格（ vintage mom jeans 复古 Mom 裤， high-waisted mom jeans 高腰 Mom 牛仔裤）。复数。2010 年代后作为对 skinny 的反动重新流行。
[例] ① Mom jeans look good with a tucked-in tee and belt. Mom 牛仔裤塞 T 恤、系腰带就很好看。 ② She thrifted a pair of light-wash mom jeans. 她在旧货店淘了条浅色 Mom 牛仔裤。
[搭] vintage mom jeans 复古 Mom 裤, high-waisted mom jeans 高腰 Mom 牛仔裤, light-wash mom jeans 浅色 Mom 牛仔裤, classic mom jeans 经典 Mom 裤, pair of mom jeans 一条 Mom 牛仔裤
[联] high-waisted 高腰的, jeans 牛仔裤, vintage 复古的, relaxed fit 宽松版型, retro 复古的, belt 腰带, casual 休闲的
[记] 复数。mom + jeans 复合；略带幽默与怀旧；与 boyfriend jeans（更宽更垮）略不同。

**gilet**
英/美 /ˈdʒɪleɪ/ 美 /dʒɪˈleɪ/
n. 马甲；无袖背心
[义] A sleeveless jacket worn over a shirt or sweater for extra warmth. 无袖短外套，套在衬衫或毛衣外保暖（quilted gilet 绗缝马甲， down gilet 羽绒马甲）。可数。英式常用；美式多 say vest，但 vest 亦指西装背心，易混。
[例] ① He layered a gilet over a flannel shirt for the hike. 徒步他在法兰绒衬衫外套了件马甲。 ② A down gilet packs small for travel. 羽绒马甲旅行好收纳。
[搭] quilted gilet 绗缝马甲, down gilet 羽绒马甲, wear a gilet 穿马甲, fleece gilet 抓绒马甲, lightweight gilet 轻量马甲

**puffer jacket**
英/美 /ˈpʌfə dʒækɪt/
n. 羽绒服；面包服
[义] A quilted jacket filled with down or synthetic insulation, creating a puffy look. 绗缝填充羽绒或化纤保暖层的夹克，外观蓬松（down puffer jacket 羽绒面包服， long puffer 长款羽绒服）。可数。冬季街头与户外均极常见。
[例] ① A good puffer jacket is light but warm in freezing weather. 好羽绒服天寒地冻也轻而暖。 ② She chose a cropped puffer to avoid looking bulky. 她选短款面包服免得显臃肿。

[联] down 羽绒, quilted 绗缝的, winter 冬天, warm 保暖的, coat 大衣, parka 派克大衣, layer 分层穿着

**bomber jacket**
英/美 /ˈbɒmə dʒækɪt/ 美 /ˈbɑːmər dʒækɪt/
n. 飞行员夹克；轰炸机夹克
[义] A short waist-length jacket with elastic cuffs and hem, originally for pilots. 短款及腰、袖口与下摆有松紧的夹克，原为飞行员穿着（leather bomber jacket 皮飞行员夹克， satin bomber 缎面飞行员夹克）。可数。MA-1 为经典型号。
[例] ① He threw a bomber jacket over his hoodie for the evening. 晚上他在连帽衫外随手套了件飞行员夹克。 ② The bomber's ribbed cuffs keep the wind out. 飞行员夹克罗纹袖口挡风。
[搭] leather bomber jacket 皮飞行员夹克, satin bomber 缎面飞行员夹克, classic bomber 经典飞行员夹克, MA-1 bomber MA-1 飞行员夹克, wear a bomber jacket 穿飞行员夹克

**base layer**
英/美 /beɪs ˈleɪə(r)/
n. 贴身层；打底层
[义] The first layer of clothing worn next to the skin for warmth or moisture management. 贴身穿的最内层，用于保暖或排汗（thermal base layer 保暖打底， moisture-wicking base layer 排汗打底）。可数。户外三层穿衣法（base / mid / shell）之首层。
[例] ① Start with a merino base layer on cold ski days. 滑雪冷天先穿美利奴打底。 ② A good base layer wicks sweat so you don't chill when you stop. 好打底排汗，停下来也不着凉。
[搭] thermal base layer 保暖打底, moisture-wicking base layer 排汗打底, merino base layer 美利奴打底, wear a base layer 穿打底, base layer top 打底上衣
[联] thermal 保暖内衣, layer 分层穿着, fleece 抓绒, outdoor 户外的, sport 运动, warm 保暖的, moisture 湿气
[记] 可数；a base layer。base + layer 复合；与 thermal underwear 有重叠。

**petite**
英/美 /pəˈtiːt/
adj. 小个子的； petite 码的
n. petite 尺码
[义] A clothing size range designed for shorter women, typically under 5'4". 为较矮女性设计的尺码段，通常指身高 5 英尺 4 英寸以下（petite section petite 专区， petite sizing petite 尺码）。形容词/名词。与 regular、 tall 并列的常见分区。
[例] ① She always shops in petite— regular sleeves are too long. 她总在 petite 区买——常规码袖子太长。 ② Petite isn't just smaller; proportions are adjusted too. petite 不只是更小，比例也改过。
[搭] petite section petite 专区, petite sizing petite 尺码, petite fit petite 版型, petite length petite 长度, shop petite 买 petite 码
[联] size 尺码, tall 高个子码, fit 合身, length 长度, proportion 比例, section 专区, alter 修改
[记] 形容词/名词。法语借词原义「娇小」；零售专指成衣短版尺码线。

**plus size**
英/美 /plʌs saɪz/
adj. 大码的
n. 大码
[义] Clothing sizes above the standard range, typically from about UK 18 / US 14 upward. 超出标准范围的尺码，通常自英码 18 / 美码 14 起（plus-size fashion 大码时尚， plus-size section 大码专区）。形容词/名词。行业用语 evolving，inclusive sizing 更中性说法亦增。
[例] ① More brands now offer plus-size options online and in stores. 更多品牌线上线下都有大码。 ② Plus-size models have changed how runways look. 大码模特改变了 T 台面貌。
[搭] plus-size fashion 大码时尚, plus-size section 大码专区, plus-size clothing 大码服装, inclusive sizing 包容尺码, extended sizes 扩展尺码
[联] size 尺码, fit 合身, fashion 时尚, inclusive 包容的, body 身体, retail 零售, model 模特
[记] 形容词/名词。plus-size 常作复合形容词；curvy、extended sizes 为相关用语。

**double-breasted**
英/美 /ˌdʌbl ˈbrestɪd/
adj. 双排扣的
[义] With two parallel rows of buttons on the front, common on coats and blazers. 前片有两排平行纽扣，常见于大衣与西装夹克（double-breasted blazer 双排扣西装， double-breasted coat 双排扣大衣）。形容词。与 single-breasted 相对；正式、复古感强。
[例] ① A double-breasted blazer looks sharp with tailored trousers. 双排扣西装配西裤很精神。 ② Double-breasted coats are warmer because the front overlaps. 双排扣大衣前片重叠更保暖。
[搭] double-breasted blazer 双排扣西装, double-breasted coat 双排扣大衣, double-breasted suit 双排扣套装, classic double-breasted 经典双排扣, six-button double-breasted 六扣双排扣
[联] single-breasted 单排扣的, blazer 西装夹克, button 纽扣, formal 正式的, lapel 翻领, suit 套装, coat 大衣
[记] 形容词。double + breasted 复合；peak lapel 常与双排扣同现。

**empire waist**
英/美 /ˈempaɪə weɪst/
n. 帝政腰；高腰线
[义] A waistline placed just under the bust, with a skirt flowing from there. 腰线设在胸下、裙身由此展开的廓形（empire waist dress 帝政腰连衣裙， empire line 帝政线）。可数/不可数。得名于拿破仑时代帝政风格；孕装、浪漫礼服常见。
[例] ① An empire waist dress suits pregnancy and hides the midriff. 帝政腰连衣裙适合孕期、也遮腰腹。 ② The empire line makes short legs look longer. 帝政线让短腿显长。
[搭] empire waist dress 帝政腰连衣裙, empire line 帝政线, empire waist gown 帝政腰礼服, high empire waist 高帝政腰, flowing empire skirt 飘逸帝政裙
[联] waist 腰, dress 连衣裙, bust 胸围, gown 礼服, maternity 孕妇的, vintage 复古的, silhouette 轮廓
[记] 可数/不可数。empire 指拿破仑「帝国」时尚；与 natural waist（自然腰线）相对。

**cocktail dress**
英/美 /ˈkɒkteɪl dres/ 美 /ˈkɑːkteɪl dres/
n. 鸡尾酒礼服；小礼服
[义] A semi-formal dress suitable for evening parties and cocktail events, typically knee-length. 半正式、适合晚宴与鸡尾酒会的连衣裙，通常及膝（little black cocktail dress 黑色小礼服， wear a cocktail dress 穿鸡尾酒礼服）。可数。比 evening gown 短、比 day dress 正式。
[例] ① The invitation said cocktail attire— she chose a knee-length dress. 请柬写 cocktail 着装——她选了及膝连衣裙。 ② A classic cocktail dress works with heels and simple jewelry. 经典鸡尾酒礼服配高跟鞋与简约首饰即可。
[搭] little black cocktail dress 黑色小礼服, wear a cocktail dress 穿鸡尾酒礼服, knee-length cocktail dress 及膝鸡尾酒礼服, elegant cocktail dress 优雅鸡尾酒礼服, cocktail attire 鸡尾酒着装
[联] dress 连衣裙, formal 正式的, party 派对, gown 礼服, heels 高跟鞋, jewelry 首饰, evening 晚间
[记] 可数；a cocktail dress。cocktail party 鸡尾酒会 → cocktail dress；LBD 常指 little black dress。

**midi**
英/美 /ˈmɪdi/
adj. 中长的；及小腿的
n. 中长款
[义] Reaching mid-calf, between mini and maxi length. 长度及小腿中部，介于 mini 与 maxi 之间（midi skirt 中长半裙， midi dress 中长连衣裙）。形容词/名词。2010 年代后重新流行。
[例] ① A midi skirt looks polished with ankle boots. 中长半裙配踝靴很体面。 ② The dress is midi, not maxi— it stops below the knee. 连衣裙是中长不是及踝——停在膝下。
[搭] midi skirt 中长半裙, midi dress 中长连衣裙, midi length 中长款, flattering midi 衬身型的中长, pleated midi 百褶中长
[联] maxi 及踝长, mini 迷你, skirt 半裙, dress 连衣裙, length 长度, hemline 衣摆线, vintage 复古的
[记] 形容词/名词。midi 亦指 MIDI 音乐协议，拼写同形异义。

**true to size**
英/美 /truː tə saɪz/
adj. 正码的；尺码标准的
[义] Fitting as expected for the labelled size, neither large nor small. 与标称尺码预期一致，不大不小（runs true to size 正码， true-to-size fit 标准尺码合身）。形容词短语。网购评论高频。
[例] ① Reviews say these boots run true to size— order your usual. 评论说这双靴正码——按平时码买。 ② If you're between sizes and it runs true, size up. 卡在码间而它又正码，就买大一号。
[搭] runs true to size 正码, true-to-size fit 标准尺码合身, generally true to size 总体正码, true to size chart 与尺码表一致, order true to size 按正码下单
[联] size 尺码, fit 合身, runs small 偏小, runs large 偏大, size up 买大一号, online shopping 网购, review 评论
[记] 形容词短语。runs small / runs large 为一对反义评论用语。

**size up**
英/美 /saɪz ʌp/
v. 买大一号
[义] To buy a larger size than you usually wear. 购买比平时更大的尺码（size up for layering 为叠穿买大一号， size up in sneakers 球鞋买大一号）。动词短语。与 size down（买小一号）相对。
[例] ① Size up if you plan to wear a thick sweater underneath. 里面要套厚毛衣就买大一号。 ② Many people size up half a size in running shoes. 跑鞋很多人大半码买。
[搭] size up for layering 为叠穿买大一号, size up half a size 大半码买, size up in sneakers 球鞋买大一号, consider sizing up 考虑买大, size up one 买大一号
[联] size down 买小一号, fit 合身, size 尺码, layer 分层穿着, between sizes 卡在码间, true to size 正码的, try on 试穿
[记] 动词短语。size sb/sth up 另义「打量、评估」，需看语境。

**spot treat**
英/美 /spɒt triːt/ 美 /spɑːt triːt/
v. 局部去渍
n. 局部处理
[义] To apply stain remover directly to a small area before washing. 洗涤前将去渍剂直接涂在局部污渍上（spot treat a stain 局部去渍， spot treat before washing 洗前局部处理）。动词/名词。比整件浸泡更省时也护色。
[例] ① Spot treat the coffee stain before it sets. 咖啡渍还没固着就先局部处理。 ② She spot treated the collar and then threw the shirt in the wash. 她领子局部去渍后把衬衫扔进洗衣机。
[搭] spot treat a stain 局部去渍, spot treat before washing 洗前局部处理, spot treat with remover 用去渍剂局部处理, spot treat grease 局部去油渍, quick spot treat 快速局部去渍
[联] stain 污渍, stain remover 去渍剂, laundry 洗衣, wash 洗, pre-treat 预洗, fabric 布料, clean 清洁
[记] 动词/名词。spot + treat 复合；标签常见 pretreat / pre-treat 同义。

**lookbook**
英/美 /ˈlʊkbʊk/
n. 造型册；Lookbook
[义] A collection of photos showing how a brand's clothes can be styled for a season. 展示品牌当季服装搭配方式的图集（seasonal lookbook 季刊造型册， online lookbook 线上造型册）。可数。时尚营销与电商常用。
[例] ① The brand released its spring lookbook online. 品牌在网上发布了春季造型册。 ② Stylists pull outfits straight from the lookbook for shoots. 造型师拍摄时直接照搬造型册搭配。
[搭] seasonal lookbook 季刊造型册, online lookbook 线上造型册, spring lookbook 春季造型册, browse the lookbook 浏览造型册, lookbook shoot 造型册拍摄
[联] fashion 时尚, brand 品牌, style 风格, outfit 一套搭配, catalog 型录, runway T 台, marketing 营销
[记] 可数；a lookbook。look + book 复合；与 catalog（含价格 SKU）相比更偏造型灵感。
